* UI/Look
** Fonts
*** Default
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
;; Doom's font variables: `doom-font', `doom-variable-pitch-font',
;; `doom-big-font' (for `doom-big-font-mode').
;; Each takes a font-spec, a font string ("Input Mono-12") or an xlfd string.
(setq
 doom-font (font-spec :family "Sarasa Mono HC" :size 12)
 doom-variable-pitch-font (font-spec :family "Sarasa UI HC" :size 12))
#+end_src

** Others
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
;; Line numbers: nil disables, `relative' for relative.
(setq display-line-numbers-type t)

;; Delete duplicated history
(setq history-delete-duplicates t)

;; fullscreen on startup
;; (add-to-list 'initial-frame-alist '(fullscreen . maximized))

;; tranparency NOTE It makes the text transparent too
;; (set-frame-parameter (selected-frame) 'alpha '(100 85))
;; (add-to-list 'default-frame-alist '(alpha 100 10))

(setq tab-width 4)

(setopt enable-local-variables t)

(setopt scroll-lock-mode t)
#+end_src
* Input
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(setopt default-input-method "chinese-cns-tsangchi")
#+end_src
* Org Directory
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
;; Must be set before org loads.
(setopt org-directory "~/Personal/org")
#+end_src
* Scratch Buffer Use Org Mode
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
;; scratch buffer default `org-mode'
(setopt doom-scratch-initial-major-mode 'org-mode)
#+end_src
* Org
** Archive Location
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! org (setopt org-archive-location "archive/%s_archive::"))
#+end_src
** Simple Org Configs
- ref :: https://explog.in/notes/writingsetup.html
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! org
  (setopt org-hide-emphasis-markers t
         ;; org-adapt-indentation nil
         ;; org-indent-indentation-per-level 1
         org-complete-tags-always-offer-all-agenda-tags t
         org-log-into-drawer t
         org-log-reschedule 'note
         org-log-redeadline 'note
         org-id-locations-file (concat org-directory "/.orgids.gpg")
         ))
#+end_src
** Priority
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! org
  (setopt org-priority-default 67
         org-priority-lowest 69
         org-priority-faces '((65 . error)
                              (66 . warning)
                              (67 . warning)
                              (68 . success)
                              (69 . success))))
;; org-priority-faces
#+end_src

** Todo Keywords
*** Default
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! org
  (setopt org-todo-keywords
         '((sequence "TODO(t)" "NEXT(n)" "PROJ(p)" "WAIT(w@)" "HOLD(h@)" "|" "DONE(d)" "KILL(k@)")
           (sequence "[ ](T)" "[?](W@)" "|" "[X](D)")
           (sequence "INBOX" "|" ))))
#+end_src
*** Faces
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! org (cl-pushnew '("INBOX" org-todo) org-todo-keyword-faces))
#+end_src
** Agenda
*** fanshi/make-line
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defun fanshi/make-line ()
  "make a line of window-width in the next line"
  (concat "\n" (make-string (window-width) 9472)))
#+end_src

*** Org-Agenda
**** Clock
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! org-agenda
  (setopt org-clock-report-include-clocking-task t
   org-agenda-clockreport-parameter-plist (quote (:link t :maxlevel 4 :fileskip0 t :compact t :narrow 80))
   org-agenda-start-with-log-mode '(state closed clock)))
#+end_src

**** Agenda Tweak
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! org-agenda
  (setopt org-agenda-block-separator 9472
         org-agenda-compact-blocks t
         org-agenda-breadcrumbs-separator " / "
         org-agenda-span 'day
         org-agenda-start-day nil
         org-agenda-start-on-weekday nil
         org-deadline-warning-days 30
         org-agenda-current-time-string "⬲ NOW -- NOW --"
         org-agenda-prefix-format '(;; (agenda . " %-3i %18s  %?-12t %-25b ")
                                    ;; (agenda . " %-3i %-44b %?18s %?-12t")
                                    ;; (agenda . " %-3i %-44b %?-18s %?-12t")
                                    (agenda . " %-3i %-44b %11s %?-12t")
                                    ;; (todo . " %-3i                     ")
                                    (todo . " %-3i %-44b %?-12t")
                                    (tags . " %i %-12:c")
                                    (search . " %i %-12:c"))
         org-agenda-format-date (lambda (date) (concat (fanshi/make-line) "\n" (org-agenda-format-date-aligned date)))
         org-agenda-sorting-strategy '((agenda time-up habit-down priority-down category-keep)
                                      (todo priority-down category-keep)
                                      (tags priority-down category-keep)
                                      (search category-keep))
         org-agenda-skip-additional-timestamps-same-entry t))
#+end_src

**** Org Super Agenda
***** fanshi/agenda
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(setopt fanshi/agenda
       '((:name "Clocked Today 📰📰📰" :log t)
         ;; (:name "Calendar 📅📅📅" :time-grid t :and (:scheduled today :not (:habit t) ))
         (:name "Calendar 📅📅📅" :time-grid t :scheduled today)
         (:discard (:todo ("DONE"  "[X]")))
         (:name "Deadlines Just Aren't Real To Me Until I'm Staring One In The Face 🚨🚨🚨" :deadline today :order 2)
         (:name "What Is Dead May Never Die 🚣🚣🚣" :deadline past :order 3)
         (:name "Defuse The Bomb 💣💣💣" :deadline future :order 4)
         (:name "Déjà Vu 🔁🔁🔁" :and (:habit t :todo ("TODO" "[ ]")) :order 5) ;; 🧟🧟🧟
         ;; (:name "Déjà Vu 🔁🔁🔁" :and (:habit t :todo ("TODO" "[ ]") :scheduled today) :order 5) ;; 🧟🧟🧟
         ;; (:name "Déjà vécu 🥶🥶🥶" :and (:habit t :todo ("TODO" "[ ]") :scheduled past) :order 6) ;; 🧟🧟🧟
         ;; (:name "Presque vu ⏩⏩⏩" :and (:habit t :todo ("TODO" "[ ]") :scheduled future) :order 7) ;; 🧟🧟🧟
         ;; (:name "Meetings"
         ;;  :and (:todo "MEETING" :scheduled future)
         ;;  :order 8)
         ))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! (:and org org-gtd)
  (setopt fanshi/alltodo
         `((:discard (:scheduled t :deadline t :regexp ,org-scheduled-time-hour-regexp))
           (:name "Important 💎💎💎" :tag "Payment" :priority "A" :order 2) ;;🚔🚔🚔
           ;; (:name "Do I really look like a guy with a plan??? 🃏🃏🃏" :and (:todo "TOPLAN" :priority> "D") :order 3)
           (:name "Camping 🏕🏕🏕" :todo "WAIT" :order 11) ; Set order of this section 💎💎💎
           ;; (:name "Peek Into Future 🔮🔮🔮" :scheduled future :order 5)
           ;; (:name "Quick Picks 🚀🚀🚀" :and (:effort< "0:15" :todo ("TODO" "[ ]")) :order 4)
           (:name "Next in line 🚀🚀🚀" :and (:todo "NEXT") :order 4)
           (:discard (:and (:file-path ,org-gtd-default-file-name) ))

***** fanshi/worktodo
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! org
  (setopt fanshi/worktodo
         `((:discard (:scheduled t :deadline t :regexp ,org-scheduled-time-hour-regexp :not (:file-path "work")))
           (:auto-outline-path t  :order 7))))
#+end_src
***** fanshi/org-private-agenda-file-regexp (include GPG for private agenda)
[[https://emacs.stackexchange.com/a/36543][org mode - Include .org.gpg files in org-agenda - Emacs Stack Exchange]]
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! org-agenda
  (defconst fanshi/org-private-agenda-file-regexp
    (rx (regexp (s-chop-suffix "\\'" (custom--standard-value 'org-agenda-file-regexp))) (opt ".gpg") eos)))
#+end_src

***** Use Org Super Agenda
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(use-package! org-super-agenda
  :after org-agenda
  :config
  (setq org-agenda-show-log t
        ;; NOTE: https://github.com/alphapapa/org-super-agenda/issues/50
        org-super-agenda-header-map (make-sparse-keymap)
        ;; fanshi/org-agenda-header (concat "\n" (make-string (window-width) 9472))
        ;; fanshi/make-org-agenda-header (defun () (concat "\n" (make-string (window-width) 9472)))
        org-agenda-custom-commands '(("p" "Private Agenda"
                                      ((agenda "" ((org-super-agenda-groups fanshi/agenda)
                                                   (org-agenda-file-regexp fanshi/org-private-agenda-file-regexp)))
                                       (alltodo "" ((org-agenda-overriding-header (fanshi/make-line))
                                                    (org-super-agenda-groups (cons `(:discard (:file-path ("work"))) fanshi/alltodo))
                                                    (org-agenda-file-regexp fanshi/org-private-agenda-file-regexp)))
                                       (alltodo "" ((org-agenda-overriding-header (fanshi/make-line))
                                                    (org-super-agenda-groups fanshi/worktodo)
                                                    (org-agenda-file-regexp fanshi/org-private-agenda-file-regexp)))
                                       ))
                                     ("w" "Work Agenda"
                                      ((agenda "" ((org-super-agenda-groups (cons `(:discard (:not (:file-path ("work")))) fanshi/agenda))
                                                   (org-agenda-file-regexp fanshi/org-private-agenda-file-regexp)))
                                       (alltodo "" ((org-agenda-overriding-header (fanshi/make-line))
                                                    (org-super-agenda-groups fanshi/worktodo)
                                                    (org-agenda-file-regexp fanshi/org-private-agenda-file-regexp)))))))
  :config
  (org-super-agenda-mode))
#+end_src

** Web Tool
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(use-package! org-web-tools
  :after-call org-capture
  :init (defun fanshi/org-web-tools--org-link-for-url ()
          (let ((url (current-kill 0)))
            (condition-case err
                (let* ((html (org-web-tools--get-url url))
                       (title (org-web-tools--html-title html)))
                  (if title (org-make-link-string url title)
                    (error "HTML page at URL has no title") url))
              (error url))))
  :defer t)
#+end_src

                                  :prompt "Select project: " )))))
  (advice-add 'org-gtd--path :filter-return (lambda (return-gtd-path)
                                               "gtd file path .gpg appended when not inbox"
                                               (if (or (string-match org-gtd-inbox return-gtd-path) ;; inbox
                                                       (string-match "ai" return-gtd-path)) ;; for ai

  (setf (alist-get "l" org-gtd-capture-templates nil nil #'string=)

  (setf (alist-get "L" org-gtd-capture-templates nil nil #'string=)

        :desc "GTD quick actions" "C-c ." #'org-gtd-agenda-transient)

** Org Capture Template for AI Tasks
All AI task entries (delegated tasks, queued gptel requests) are created
through a single org-capture template instead of hand-inserted strings.
`fanshi/ai-task--capture' is the programmatic entry point: callers pass a
heading, optional body and properties, and get back the org-id of the new
entry.  The heading is not a =%?= placeholder (that prompts interactively);
it is stashed in =fanshi/ai-task--capture-data= and inserted by the
template's =:hook=.  The template is registered in
=org-gtd-capture-templates= under =fanshi/ai-task-capture-key= (bound to
=a=), so it can also be used interactively via =org-gtd-capture= (=SPC X
a=).  The registration claims that key (replacing any pre-existing template
with it), and the programmatic path depends on the entry remaining in
=org-gtd-capture-templates= -- do not remove it.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! org-gtd
  (defvar fanshi/ai-task-capture-key "a"
    "Capture key of the AI task template in `org-gtd-capture-templates'.")

  (defvar-local fanshi/ai-task--capture-data nil
    "Plist with the entry data for the current AI task capture.
Keys: :heading (required), :body, :properties (alist).")

  (defvar fanshi/ai-task--captured-id nil
    "Org-id of the entry created by the current AI task capture, set by
`fanshi/ai-task--capture-insert-body'.")

  (defun fanshi/ai-task--capture-insert-body ()
    "Insert the heading, properties and body of `fanshi/ai-task--capture-data' at point.
Runs as the `:hook' template function, with point in the new entry in
the capture buffer, before `:immediate-finish' finalizes the capture.
The template has no `%?' placeholder (it would prompt interactively), so
the heading is inserted here too.  Also ensures the entry has an org-id
and stores it in `fanshi/ai-task--captured-id'."
    (let* ((data fanshi/ai-task--capture-data)
           (heading (plist-get data :heading))
           (body (plist-get data :body))
           (properties (remove nil (plist-get data :properties))))
      (org-back-to-heading)
      (when (and heading (string-trim heading))
        (save-excursion
          ;; Append the heading to the `* TODO ' keyword that the template
          ;; provides, so the entry reads `* TODO <heading>'.
          (goto-char (line-end-position))
          (insert (string-trim heading))))
      (dolist (prop properties)
        (org-entry-put (point) (car prop) (cdr prop)))
      (setq fanshi/ai-task--captured-id (org-id-get-create (point)))
      (when (and body (string-trim body))
        (save-excursion
          (org-end-of-subtree t)
          (insert (if (bolp) (concat body "\n") (concat "\n" body "\n")))))))

  (setf (alist-get fanshi/ai-task-capture-key org-gtd-capture-templates nil nil #'string=)
        (list "AI task" 'entry
              `(file ,(org-gtd--path "org-gtd-ai-tasks"))
              ;; The heading comes from `fanshi/ai-task--capture-data' (set by
              ;; `fanshi/ai-task--capture') and the template's `:hook'
              ;; (`fanshi/ai-task--capture-insert-body') appends it to the
              ;; `TODO' heading, NOT from a `%?' placeholder: `%?' makes
              ;; `org-capture' prompt interactively for the heading, which the
              ;; programmatic path cannot answer, so the entry would be finalized
              ;; with an empty heading and the caller's task text would be lost.
              (string-join `("* TODO "
                             ":PROPERTIES:"
                             ,(concat ":" (org-gtd-type-property 'delegated :who) ": ai")
                             ":END:"
                             "%U")
                           "\n")
              :immediate-finish t
              :hook #'fanshi/ai-task--capture-insert-body))

  (defun fanshi/ai-task--capture (heading &optional body properties)
    "Create a TODO entry in the AI tasks file via the org-capture template.
HEADING is the heading text (without the TODO keyword); it is inserted into
the entry by the template's `:hook'.  BODY is optional text stored below
the heading; PROPERTIES is an optional alist of org properties to set
on the new entry (nil entries are ignored).
Returns the org-id of the new entry.

`org-gtd-capture' is called with NO-REFILE set: org-gtd's refile step
assumes point sits on a real entry, but in the capture buffer point is at
point-min (no heading), so its due-date call ends up setting an org property
with a nil value and Emacs signals \"Attempt to set a constant symbol: nil\".
That aborts the capture before the template's `:hook' can run, so the
org-id is never recorded and `Delegate' fails.  Skipping NO-REFILE is safe
here: `:immediate-finish' files the entry into the backlog file, which is
exactly where delegated tasks belong.

The heading is passed through `fanshi/ai-task--capture-data' and inserted by
the template's `:hook' (`fanshi/ai-task--capture-insert-body'), not via the
`%?' placeholder.  The old code tried to feed the heading to `%?' with
`cl-letf' on `org-capture-initial'; that is not a valid `cl-letf' place
(`(declare ...)' is not a generalized variable), so `cl-letf' expanded the
binding to `(setq nil heading)' and raised \"Attempt to set a constant
symbol: nil\" from within `Delegate'.  `org-capture-initial' is a lexically
scoped `let' variable in `org-capture' anyway, so it cannot be rebound from
here."
    (unwind-protect
        (progn
          (setq fanshi/ai-task--capture-data
                (list :heading heading :body body :properties properties))
          (setq fanshi/ai-task--captured-id nil)
          (org-gtd-capture t fanshi/ai-task-capture-key))
      (setq fanshi/ai-task--capture-data nil))
    (unless fanshi/ai-task--captured-id
      (error "AI task capture did not produce an entry"))
    fanshi/ai-task--captured-id))
#+end_src

** Paste image with pngpaste
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! org
  (defun fanshi/clipboard-contains-image-p ()
    "Return non-nil if clipboard contains image data."
    (when-let* ((targets (gui-get-selection 'CLIPBOARD 'TARGETS)))
      (seq-some (lambda (type)
                  (and (symbolp type)
                       (string-prefix-p "image/" (symbol-name type))))
                targets)))

  (defun fanshi/evil-paste-image ()
    "Paste clipboard image using pngpaste, return filename or nil if not image."
    (when (fanshi/clipboard-contains-image-p)
      (let* ((ext "png")
             (filename (format "~/Downloads/clipboard-%s.%s" (format-time-string "%Y%m%d%H%M%S") ext)))
        (call-process (executable-find "pngpaste") nil nil nil (expand-file-name filename))
        (org-insert-link nil (concat "file:" filename))
        filename)))

  (defun fanshi/advice-evil-paste-image (orig-fn &rest args)
    "If clipboard has image, paste image instead of text.
Else call original evil-paste-after."
    (if (and (bound-and-true-p evil-mode)
             (fanshi/clipboard-contains-image-p))
        (fanshi/evil-paste-image)
      (apply orig-fn args)))

  (advice-add 'evil-paste-after :around #'fanshi/advice-evil-paste-image)
  (advice-add 'evil-paste-before :around #'fanshi/advice-evil-paste-image))
#+end_src

** fanshi/clone-from-project
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! projectile
  (defun fanshi/clone-from-project (dir)
    "Clone the infrastructure of an existing project DIR to make a new project"
    (if-let* ((project-type (projectile-project-type dir))
              (project-files-to-copy (symbol-value (intern-soft (format "fanshi/project-files-to-copy/%s" project-type))))
              (new-project-directory (file-name-as-directory (read-directory-name "Create new project at directory: "))))
        (progn
          ;; NOTE: make new directory
          (make-directory new-project-directory t)
          ;; NOTE: copy files
          (dolist (wildcards-or-file project-files-to-copy)
            (dolist (file (projectile-verify-file-wildcard wildcards-or-file dir))
              (dired-copy-file file new-project-directory 1)))
          ;; NOTE: init project
          (fanshi/init-new-project new-project-directory)
          ;; NOTE: switch to the new project
          (projectile-switch-project-by-name new-project-directory))
      (user-error "Unsupported project type to clones: %s, %s" project-type project-files-to-copy))))
#+end_src
** fanshi/choose-and-clone-for-new-project
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! projectile
  (defun fanshi/choose-and-clone-for-new-project ()
    "To choose an existing porject, and clone the infrastructure of it to make a new project"
    (interactive)
    (if-let* ((project-type (completing-read "Project type to clone: " fanshi/clonable-project-types))
              (projects (cl-remove-if (lambda (p) (not (string-equal project-type (projectile-project-type p))))
                                      projectile-known-projects)))
        (projectile-completing-read "Clone project: " projects :action #'fanshi/clone-from-project)
      (user-error "There are no clonable projects for project-type %s" project-type))))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! eglot
  (advice-add 'eglot--format-markup :around #'limit-argument-length)
  (defun limit-argument-length (orig input)
    "TEMP fix for eglot freeze with haskell-language-server"
    (let ((ty (plist-get input :kind))
          (v  (plist-get input :value)))
      (if (string= ty "markdown")
          (funcall orig (list :kind ty :value (seq-take v 700)))
        (funcall orig input)))))

** Typescript
- ref :: https://www.reddit.com/r/emacs/comments/b7rsxu/behold_orgbabelexecutetypescript/
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! (org ob-async)
  (defun org-babel-execute:typescript (body params)
    "babel execute typescript"
    (let* ((tmp-ts-file (org-babel-temp-file "scripts" ".ts"))
           (tmp-js-file (concat (substring tmp-ts-file 0 -2) "js"))
           (strict (if (assq :strict params) "--strict " ""))
           (target (if (assq :target params) (format " -t %s " (cdr (assq :target params))) ""))
           (cmd (concat "tsc " strict target (shell-quote-argument tmp-ts-file))))
      (with-temp-file tmp-ts-file (insert body))
      (with-temp-buffer
        (if (eq (call-process-shell-command cmd nil t) 0)
            (progn
              (insert-file-contents tmp-js-file)
              (if (assq :js params)
                  (buffer-string)
                (require 'ob-js)
                (org-babel-execute:js (buffer-string) params)))
          (buffer-string)))))

  ;; maybe related? https://github.com/hlissner/doom-emacs/issues/2198
  (add-to-list 'ob-async-no-async-languages-alist "typescript"))
#+end_src

                                     (python-shell-send-string-no-output python-shell-eval-setup-code lispy-python-proc)
                                     (python-shell-send-string-no-output python-shell-eval-file-setup-code lispy-python-proc))

* Not so long
** Problem
The so-long.el is for text files with long lines (think of e.g. minified JavaScript or CSS libs).
No point to enable it globally and moreover it broke some ux for me like the following:
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle no
(helpful-callable #'org-toggle-link-display)
#+end_src
Because so-long-mode triggered on the source elisp file, the "coding: utf-8" local variable doesn't work and hence asking me for the coding system.
** So...
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(global-so-long-mode -1)
#+end_src

* jj helpers
** fanshi/call-jj
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(defun fanshi/call-jj (dir &rest args)
  "Run `jj ARGS' in DIR and return stdout as a string.
The invocation directory is pinned to DIR by `let'-binding
`default-directory', which `call-process' uses as the child's working
directory; this makes `@' and `@-' resolve in DIR even when the calling buffer
is indirect.  NIL DIR means `default-directory'.  On failure, signal an error
carrying DIR, jj's stderr and the exit status.

stdout goes to a temp buffer so it can be handed back to the caller; stderr goes
to a temp file because the stderr slot of `call-process' accepts nil, t or a
file name, never a buffer."
  (let ((stdout (generate-new-buffer " *jj stdout*"))
        (stderr-file (make-temp-file "jj-stderr"))
        (default-directory (or dir default-directory)))
    (unwind-protect
        (let ((status
               (apply #'call-process "jj" nil
                      `(,stdout ,stderr-file)
                      nil "--color=never" args)))
          (when (/= status 0)
            (error "jj %s failed in %s (exit %d): %s"
                   (mapconcat #'identity args " ")
                   dir
                   status
                   (let ((stderr (with-temp-buffer
                                   (insert-file-contents stderr-file)
                                   (string-trim (buffer-string)))))
                     (if (string-empty-p stderr) "unknown error" stderr))))
          (with-current-buffer stdout (buffer-string)))
      (when (buffer-live-p stdout)
        (kill-buffer stdout))
      ;; Deleting must not mask the error a failing body already raised.
      (ignore-errors (delete-file stderr-file)))))
#+end_src
** fanshi/jj-workspace-root
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(defun fanshi/jj-workspace-root (&optional dir)
  "Return the current workspace's root path, or nil if not resolvable.
Nil-safe wrapper over `jj workspace root' (via `fanshi/call-jj', which pins
DIR as the child's working directory, so the lookup is independent of the
calling buffer).  DIR is the directory to resolve from; NIL means
`default-directory'.  jj derives the workspace from DIR, so this is the root of
whatever workspace DIR lives in; callers needing the *main* root after switching
into a sub-workspace must capture it before the switch.

Unlike `fanshi/call-jj', which signals on failure, this swallows the error
(`ignore-errors', which also covers jj missing from `exec-path') and returns
nil, so callers can fall back."
  (let ((root (ignore-errors (fanshi/call-jj dir "workspace" "root"))))
    (and (stringp root) (not (string-blank-p root)) (file-name-as-directory (string-trim root)))))
#+end_src

** Fix response org heading level
Disabled: breaks `fanshi/gptel-multisend'. Ref:
https://github.com/karthink/gptel/issues/1226#issuecomment-3828162897
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle no
(after! gptel
  (defun gptel-fix-response-levels (beg end)
    "Shift heading levels within the gptel response BEG END only."
    (save-excursion
      (let ((parent-level
             (save-excursion
               (goto-char beg)
               (if (re-search-backward org-heading-regexp nil t)
                   (org-current-level)
                 0))))

        (save-restriction
          (narrow-to-region beg end)
          (goto-char (point-min))

          (when (re-search-forward org-heading-regexp nil t)
            (let* ((current-level (org-current-level))
                   (target-level (1+ parent-level))
                   (diff (- target-level current-level)))

              (unless (zerop diff)
                (goto-char (point-min))
                (while (re-search-forward "^\\(\\*+\\) " nil t)
                  (let* ((stars (match-string 1))
                         (new-level (max 1 (+ (length stars) diff))))
                    (replace-match (concat (make-string new-level ?*) " ") t t)))
                (message "Gptel: Adjusted response depth by %d." diff))))))))

  (add-hook 'gptel-post-response-functions #'gptel-fix-response-levels))
#+end_src
** Auto continue on not finished responses
*** Config
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defcustom fanshi/gptel-auto-continue-enabled t
  "Automatically continue truncated responses."
  :type 'boolean
  :group 'gptel)

(defcustom fanshi/gptel-auto-continue-max-retries 25
  "Max auto-continues per response before giving up."
  :type 'integer
  :group 'gptel)

(defvar-local fanshi/gptel--auto-continue-count nil
  "Auto-continues in the current turn.")

(defvar-local fanshi/gptel--aborted nil
  "Non-nil when the last request was aborted; auto-continue skips it.")

(defvar-local fanshi/gptel--auto-continued nil
  "Non-nil while an auto-continue is in flight. Reset by `fanshi/notify'.")
#+end_src
*** fanshi/gptel--aborted related advices
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defvar fanshi/gptel-loop--pending-abort)
  (defvar fanshi/gptel-loop--aborted)

  (defun fanshi/gptel-mark-turn-aborted (&optional buf)
    "Mark BUF's turn aborted."
    (with-current-buffer (or buf (current-buffer))
      (setq-local fanshi/gptel--aborted t)
      (unless (let ((p fanshi/gptel-loop--pending-abort))
                (and (processp p) (process-live-p p)))
        (setq-local fanshi/gptel-loop--aborted t))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-abort--set-aborted-flag-a (buf)
    "Advice for `gptel-abort': set BUF's aborted flags."
    (fanshi/gptel-mark-turn-aborted buf))
  (advice-add 'gptel-abort :before #'fanshi/gptel-abort--set-aborted-flag-a)

  (defun fanshi/gptel-send--unset-aborted-flag-a (&optional arg)
    "Advice for `gptel-send': clear the aborted flag unless ARG."
    (unless arg
      (setq-local fanshi/gptel--aborted nil)))

  (advice-add 'gptel-send :after #'fanshi/gptel-send--unset-aborted-flag-a))
#+end_src
*** fanshi/gptel--not-done-auto-continue-or-notify
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/notify ()
    "Notify that the response is done and reset the turn's counters."
    (setq-local fanshi/gptel--auto-continue-count nil
                fanshi/gptel--auto-continued nil)
    (ignore-errors
      (ns-do-applescript
       (format "display notification \"%s\" with title \"%s\" sound name \"Submerge\""
               "Response complete" "GPTel"))))

  (defun fanshi/auto-continue ()
    "Delete the truncated tail and re-send."
    (unless fanshi/gptel--auto-continue-count
      (setq-local fanshi/gptel--auto-continue-count 0))
    (incf fanshi/gptel--auto-continue-count)
    (setq-local fanshi/gptel--auto-continued t)
    (let ((fanshi/prevent-duplicate-gptel-send nil))
      (without-restriction
        (delete-region (+ 1 (point)) (point-max))
        (gptel-send))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--not-done-auto-continue-or-notify (beg end)
    "Post-response hook: auto-continue if BEG END looks truncated, else notify."
    (cond
     ((not fanshi/gptel-auto-continue-enabled) (fanshi/notify))
     (fanshi/gptel--aborted
      (setq-local fanshi/gptel--aborted nil)
      (message "gptel: request was aborted, skipping auto-continue")
      (fanshi/notify))
     ((and fanshi/gptel--auto-continue-count (>= fanshi/gptel--auto-continue-count fanshi/gptel-auto-continue-max-retries))
      (message "gptel: Max auto-continues reached (%d), stopping."
               fanshi/gptel-auto-continue-max-retries)
      (fanshi/notify))
     (t (save-excursion
          (with-restriction beg end
            (if (eq (count-words (point-min) (point-max)) 0)
                (progn (message "gptel: empty response, skip auto continue")
                       (fanshi/notify))
              (goto-char (point-max))
              (while (re-search-backward (rx (+ (any blank ?\n)) point) nil t) 1)
              (let ((response-start
                     (save-excursion (text-property-search-backward 'gptel 'response t) (point))))
                (cond
                 ((save-excursion (re-search-backward (rx ":" point) response-start t))
                  (message "gptel: response ends with a colon, auto continue")
                  (fanshi/auto-continue))
                 ((save-excursion (re-search-backward (rx (| "[" "(" "{" ) point) response-start t))
                  (message "gptel: response ends with an open parenthesis/bracket, auto continue")
                  (fanshi/auto-continue))
                 ((save-excursion (re-search-backward (rx "Let me " ) response-start t))
                  (message
                   "gptel: 'Let me ' detected without tool call to follow up, auto continue"
                   (match-string 1))
                  (fanshi/auto-continue))
                 ((and (member 'coding fanshi/last-gptel-presets)
                       (save-excursion (re-search-backward (rx "Code Report" (+ anychar) "Summary" (+ anychar) "Changes Made") response-start t)))
                  (let ((project-type (or projectile-project-type (projectile-project-type))))
                    (cond
                     ((eq project-type 'doom)
                      (if-let ((check-result (fanshi/gptel--post-response-check/doom)))
                          (progn
                            (message "Gptel doom code check: %s" (plist-get check-result :error))
                            (if (and (> fanshi/gptel-code-check-auto-fix-max-retries 0)
                                     (< fanshi/gptel--code-check-fix-count fanshi/gptel-code-check-auto-fix-max-retries))
                                (fanshi/gptel--code-check-auto-fix check-result)
                              (fanshi/notify)))
                        (message "Gptel doom code check succeeded")
                        (fanshi/notify)))
                     (t (message "gptel: Code Report detected, skip auto continue. (%s)" project-type) (fanshi/notify))))
                  (message "gptel: Code Report detected, skip auto continue")
                  (fanshi/notify))
                 ((save-excursion (re-search-backward (rx "#end_src" point) response-start t))
                  (message "gptel: end of src block detected, skip auto continue")
                  (fanshi/notify))
                 ((save-excursion (re-search-backward (rx (any alnum) point) response-start t))
                  (message "gptel: response ends mid-word, auto continue")
                  (fanshi/auto-continue))
                 (t (fanshi/notify))))))))))
  ;; Weight 100: after loop guard, before jj auto-commit.
  (add-hook 'gptel-post-response-functions #'fanshi/gptel--not-done-auto-continue-or-notify 100))
#+end_src
** Token Escape
Ref: https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/issues/19947#issuecomment-3971115828

*** fanshi/gptel-escape-sensitive-token
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defun fanshi/gptel-escape-sensitive-token (_fsm)
  "Escape `<__' tokens in the prompt buffer."
  (goto-char (point-min))
  (while (re-search-forward (regexp-quote "<__") nil t)
    (replace-match "&lt;__")))
#+end_src
*** fanshi/gptel-escape-tool-args
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defun fanshi/gptel-escape-tool-args (tool-call)
  "Escape <__something__> in TOOL-CALL's :args.  Hook: `gptel-pre-tool-call-functions'."
  (let* ((args (plist-get tool-call :args))
         (new-args (cl-loop for (k v) on args by #'cddr
                            collect k and collect
                            (if (stringp v)
                                (replace-regexp-in-string
                                 "<__"
                                 "&lt;__"
                                 v t t)
                              ;; FIXME non-string args are not escaped.
                              v))))
    (unless (equal args new-args)
      `(:args ,new-args))))
#+end_src
*** fanshi/gptel-escape-tool-result
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defun fanshi/gptel-escape-tool-result (tool-call)
  "Escape <__something__> in TOOL-CALL's :result.  Hook: `gptel-post-tool-call-functions'."
  (when-let* ((result (plist-get tool-call :result))
              ((stringp result)))
    `(:result ,(replace-regexp-in-string "<__" "&lt;__" result t t))))
#+end_src

** For gptel-send with active region under org-mode
gptel folds responses with `org-cycle', which runs `org-tab-first-hook' and lets
yasnippet expand at a trigger or active region. Suppress that during insertion.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel--suppress-yas-a (orig-fun &rest args)
    "Run ORIG-FUN with `+org-yas-expand-maybe-h' removed from `org-tab-first-hook'."
    (let ((org-tab-first-hook (remq #'+org-yas-expand-maybe-h org-tab-first-hook)))
      (apply orig-fun args)))

  (advice-add 'gptel--insert-response :around #'fanshi/gptel--suppress-yas-a)
  (advice-add 'gptel-curl--stream-insert-response :around #'fanshi/gptel--suppress-yas-a)
  (advice-add 'gptel--display-tool-results :around #'fanshi/gptel--suppress-yas-a))

**** fanshi/restrict-to-projectile-files-a
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/restrict-to-projectile-files-a (&rest args)
    "Restrict file writes to project root.  No context exceptions."
    (let ((path (car args)))
      (unless (file-exists-p path)
        (error "Error: File or directory %s does not exist." path))
      (if-let* ((root (projectile-project-root))
                (abs-root (expand-file-name root))
                (abs-path (expand-file-name path)))
          (unless (string-prefix-p abs-root abs-path)
            (error "Error: File or directory %s is not available (restricted to project root %s)" path root))
        (error "Error: Unexpected project root or path: %s, %s" root path))))

  (advice-add 'gptel-agent--edit-files :before #'fanshi/restrict-to-projectile-files-a)
  (advice-add 'gptel-agent--insert-in-file :before #'fanshi/restrict-to-projectile-files-a))

**** fanshi/gptel-backlog-path
The AI task backlog sits outside every project root.  The agent never names
it: =BreakdownTask=, =Delegate= and =QueryBacklog= resolve this path
internally, so no scrum tool takes a file argument and the sandbox needs no
backlog exception.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defun fanshi/gptel-backlog-path ()
  "Expanded path of the org-gtd AI task backlog, or nil if `org-gtd' is not loaded."
  (when (fboundp 'org-gtd--path)
    (expand-file-name (org-gtd--path "org-gtd-ai-tasks"))))
#+end_src
**** fanshi/restrict-to-projectile-plus-context-a
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/restrict-to-projectile-files-plus-context-a (&rest args)
    "Restrict file reads to the project root, except gptel context and temp files.
Temp files are what `gptel-agent--truncate-buffer' writes for oversized results."
    (let ((file-path (expand-file-name (car args))))
      (unless (file-exists-p file-path)
        (error "Error: File or directory %s does not exist." file-path))
      (unless
          (or
           (cl-find-if (lambda (context)
                         (let ((fname (expand-file-name context)))
                           (if (file-directory-p context)
                               (file-in-directory-p file-path fname)
                             (string= file-path fname))))
                       fanshi/gptel-context-files)
           (file-in-directory-p file-path
                                (expand-file-name "gptel-agent-temp"
                                                  (temporary-file-directory))))
        (apply #'fanshi/restrict-to-projectile-files-a args))))
  (advice-add 'gptel-agent--read-file-lines :before #'fanshi/restrict-to-projectile-files-plus-context-a))

*** nono
**** fanshi/handle_nono_output
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defun fanshi/handle_nono_output (exit-code)
  "Clean nono's output from the buffer given process EXIT-CODE.
Strips ANSI escapes plus nono's logging and banner."
  (ansi-color-apply-on-region (point-min) (point-max))
  (save-excursion
    (goto-char (point-min))
    (if (zerop exit-code)
        (when-let ((pos (search-forward "Applying sandbox...\n" nil t)))
          (delete-region (point-min) pos))
      (when-let ((pos (search-forward "NONO DIAGNOSTIC" nil t)))
        (delete-region (point-min) pos))
      (goto-char (point-max))
      (when-let ((pos (search-backward "To grant additional access, re-run with:" nil t)))
        (delete-region pos (point-max)))
      (goto-char (point-min))
      (insert (format "Command failed with exit code %d:\nSTDOUT+STDERR:\n" exit-code)))))
#+end_src
**** fanshi/nono-context-allow-read-file-args-list

(defun fanshi/nono-context-allow-read-file-args-list (&optional context-files)
  "nono read-allow args for CONTEXT-FILES (default `fanshi/gptel-context-files')."
  (mapcan (lambda (file)
            (list (if (file-directory-p file) "--read" "--read-file") (expand-file-name file)))
          (or context-files fanshi/gptel-context-files)))
