**** fanshi/gptel-agent--execute-bash-a
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent--execute-bash-a (callback command)
    "Run COMMAND under nono in bash, calling CALLBACK with its output."
    (let ((output-buffer (generate-new-buffer " *gptel-agent-bash*"))
          (root (projectile-project-root)))
      (if root
          (make-process
           :name "gptel-agent-bash"
           :buffer output-buffer
           :command (append (list fanshi/nono "run" "--allow" root
                                  "--allow" (expand-file-name "~/.cache/nix")
                                  "--read-file" (expand-file-name "~/.gitconfig"))
                            (fanshi/nono-context-allow-read-file-args-list)
                            (list "--" "bash" "-c" command))
           :connection-type 'pipe
           :sentinel
           (lambda (process _event)
             (when (memq (process-status process) '(exit signal))
               (let* ((exit-code (process-exit-status process))
                      (output (with-current-buffer (process-buffer process)
                                (fanshi/handle_nono_output exit-code)
                                (gptel-agent--truncate-buffer (format "%s" command))
                                (buffer-string))))
                 (kill-buffer (process-buffer process))
                 (funcall callback output)))))
        (funcall callback (format "Project root not found, command blocked: %s" command)))))
  (advice-add 'gptel-agent--execute-bash :override #'fanshi/gptel-agent--execute-bash-a))
#+end_src
**** fanshi/gptel-agent--glob-a
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent--glob-a (pattern &optional path depth)
    "Find files matching PATTERN (case-insensitive regex) under PATH via `tree'.
DEPTH limits recursion. Matches are sorted by modification time."
    (when (string-empty-p pattern)
      (error "Error: pattern must not be empty"))
    (if (not path)
        (setq path ".")
      (unless (file-exists-p path)
        (error "Error: path %s doesn't exist" path))
      (unless (file-readable-p path)
        (error "Error: path %s is not readable" path))
      (unless (file-directory-p path)
        (error "Error: path %s is not a directory" path)))
    (unless (executable-find "tree")
      (error "Error: Executable `tree` not found.  This tool cannot be used"))

    (let ((full-path (expand-file-name path))
          (root (projectile-project-root)))
      (unless root
        (error "Error: Project root not found. This tool cannot be used"))
      (let* ((args (append (list "run" "--allow" root)
                           (fanshi/nono-context-allow-read-file-args-list)
                           (list "--" "tree" "-l" "-f" "-i" "-I" ".git"
                                 "--sort=mtime" "--ignore-case"
                                 "--prune" "-P" pattern full-path)))
             (args (if (natnump depth)
                       (nconc args (list "-L" (number-to-string depth)))
                     args)))
        (with-temp-buffer
          (fanshi/handle_nono_output
           (apply #'call-process fanshi/nono nil '(t t) nil args))
          (gptel-agent--truncate-buffer "glob")
          (buffer-string)))))
  (advice-add 'gptel-agent--glob :override #'fanshi/gptel-agent--glob-a))
#+end_src
**** fanshi/gptel-agent--grep-a
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent--grep-a (regex path &optional glob context-lines)
    "Search for REGEX in file or directory PATH using ripgrep.

REGEX is PCRE-format. PATH is a file or directory.
GLOB restricts matched files; CONTEXT-LINES adds context (0-15, default 0).

Matches are grouped by file with line numbers, at most 1000 per file, sorted by
modification time."
    (unless (file-exists-p path)
      (error "Error: File or directory %s does not exist" path))
    (unless (file-readable-p path)
      (error "Error: File or directory %s is not readable" path))

    (let ((root (projectile-project-root))
          (grepper (or (executable-find "rg") (executable-find "grep"))))
      (unless root
        (error "Error: project root not found, this tool cannot be used"))
      (unless grepper
        (error "Error: ripgrep/grep not available, this tool cannot be used"))
      (let* ((cmd (file-name-sans-extension (file-name-nondirectory grepper)))
             (args
              (cond
               ((string= "rg" cmd)
                (delq nil (list "--sort=modified"
                                (and (natnump context-lines)
                                     (format "--context=%d" context-lines))
                                (and glob (format "--glob=%s" glob))
                                ;; "--files-with-matches"
                                "--max-count=1000"
                                "--heading" "--line-number" "-e" regex
                                (expand-file-name (substitute-in-file-name path)))))
               ((string= "grep" cmd)
                (delq nil (list "--recursive"
                                (and (natnump context-lines)
                                     (format "--context=%d" context-lines))
                                (and glob (format "--include=%s" glob))
                                "--max-count=1000"
                                "--line-number" "--regexp" regex
                                (expand-file-name (substitute-in-file-name path)))))
               (t (error "Error: failed to identify grepper"))))
             (args (append (list "run" "--allow" root)
                           (fanshi/nono-context-allow-read-file-args-list)
                           (list "--" grepper) args)))
        (with-temp-buffer
          (let ((exit-code
                 (apply #'call-process fanshi/nono nil '(t t) nil args)))
            (if (= exit-code 1)
                "No matches found."   ;; grep's 1 means no match, not an error
              (fanshi/handle_nono_output exit-code)
              (gptel-agent--truncate-buffer "grep")
              (buffer-string)))))))
  (advice-add 'gptel-agent--grep :override #'fanshi/gptel-agent--grep-a))
#+end_src
*** gptel-agent--read-url
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent--read-url-a (tool-cb url)
    "Fetch URL text and call TOOL-CB with it.
Local file URLs are rejected: they would bypass the `Read' tool's restrictions.
Page text is reduced to the readable article and truncated."
    (if (or (string-prefix-p "file://" url)
            (string-prefix-p "file:" url)
            (string-prefix-p "/" url)
            (string-prefix-p "~" url)
            (string-match-p "^[a-zA-Z]:" url)) ;; Windows path, e.g. C:\
        (funcall tool-cb
                 (format "Error: WebFetch cannot read local files \"%s\". Use the Read tool for local files." url))
      (gptel-agent--fetch-with-timeout
       url
       (lambda (cb)
         (progn
           (goto-char (point-min))
           ;; FIXME handle other content types (images etc.)?
           (if (search-forward "content-type: application/pdf" (save-excursion (forward-paragraph) (point)) t)
               (funcall cb "FIXME: pdf returned from the url, but no pdf handler is implemented")
             (goto-char (point-min))
             (forward-paragraph)
             (condition-case errdata
                 (let ((dom (libxml-parse-html-region (point) (point-max))))
                   (with-temp-buffer
                     (eww-score-readability dom)
                     (shr-insert-document (eww-highest-readability dom))
                     (decode-coding-region (point-min) (point-max) 'utf-8)
                     (gptel-agent--truncate-buffer "WebFetch")
                     (funcall cb (buffer-string))))
               (error (funcall cb (format "Error: Request failed with error data:\n%S"
                                          errdata)))))))
       tool-cb (format "Fetch for \"%s\"" url))))
  (advice-add 'gptel-agent--read-url :override #'fanshi/gptel-agent--read-url-a))
#+end_src
*** Handle URL fetch errors gracefully
`gptel-agent--fetch-with-timeout' does not catch synchronous `url-retrieve' errors
(DNS failure, connection refused), so WebFetch on a bad URL prints "error in process
sentinel" and halts the request. Catch them and route them to the tool callback.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel-agent
  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent--fetch-with-timeout-a (orig-fn url url-cb tool-cb failed-msg &rest args)
    "Route a synchronous `url-retrieve' error from ORIG-FN through TOOL-CB."
    (condition-case err
        (apply orig-fn url url-cb tool-cb failed-msg args)
      (error
       (funcall tool-cb
                (format "Error: %s failed with error: %S"
                        failed-msg err))
       nil)))
  (advice-add 'gptel-agent--fetch-with-timeout :around #'fanshi/gptel-agent--fetch-with-timeout-a))
#+end_src
*** liberate restricted tools!
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defvar fanshi/safe-tool '("Write" "Edit" "Bash" "Agent"))
#+end_src
** Tools
*** Elisp Help Tool
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent--helpful-symbol (symbol-name &optional force-variable)
    "Curated help for SYMBOL-NAME (function, variable, macro or face).

Like `helpful' minus the UI-only sections. Emits, as available: Summary,
Obsolete Info, Signature, Value / Global Value, Original Value, Documentation,
Advice, Aliases, Implementations, Source Code.

Errors come back as a `<tool_call_error>' result, not a signal."
    (require 'helpful)
    (if-let ((symbol (intern-soft symbol-name)))
        (progn
          (if (and force-variable (not (boundp symbol)) (fboundp symbol))
              (fanshi/gptel--tool-call-error-result
               "force-variable is t but %s has no variable binding (function only)" symbol)
            ;; Prefer force-variable; otherwise guess from being fbound.
            (-let* ((callable-p (if force-variable
                                    nil
                                  (fboundp symbol)))
                    (primitive-p (helpful--primitive-p symbol callable-p))
                    ((def-buf def-pos _opened)
                     (if (or (not primitive-p) find-function-C-source-directory)
                         (helpful--definition symbol callable-p)
                       '(nil nil nil)))
                    (source (when def-buf
                              (helpful--source symbol callable-p def-buf def-pos)))
                    (source-path (when def-buf (buffer-file-name def-buf))))
            (with-temp-buffer
              (insert (helpful--summary symbol callable-p def-buf def-pos) "\n\n")

              (when (helpful--obsolete-info symbol callable-p)
                (insert (helpful--format-obsolete-info symbol callable-p) "\n\n"))

              ;; Callables, but not keyboard macros.
              (when (and callable-p (not (helpful--kbd-macro-p symbol)))
                (insert "Signature\n")
                (insert (helpful--syntax-highlight (helpful--signature symbol)) "\n\n"))

              (when (not callable-p)
                (let* ((val (helpful--sym-value symbol nil))
                       (heading-text
                        (if (local-variable-if-set-p symbol)
                            "Global Value"
                          "Value")))
                  (insert heading-text "\n")
                  (insert (helpful--format-value symbol val) "\n\n")
                  (when (helpful--original-value-differs-p symbol)
                    (insert "Original Value\n")
                    (insert (helpful--format-value symbol (car (helpful--original-value symbol))) "\n\n"))))

              (let ((docstring (helpful--docstring symbol callable-p))
                    (version-info (unless callable-p
                                    (helpful--version-info symbol))))
                (when (or docstring version-info)
                  (insert "Documentation\n")
                  (when docstring
                    (insert (helpful--format-docstring docstring) "\n"))
                  (when version-info
                    (insert (s-word-wrap 70 version-info) "\n"))))

              (when (helpful--advised-p symbol)
                (insert "Advice\n")
                (insert (format "This %s is advised."
                                (if (macrop symbol) "macro" "function")) "\n\n"))

              (when-let ((aliases (helpful--aliases symbol callable-p)))
                (insert "Aliases\n")
                (insert (s-join "\n" (--map (helpful--format-alias it callable-p) aliases))
                        "\n\n"))

              ;; Generic methods only.
              (when callable-p
                (ignore-errors
                  (let ((func symbol)
                        (content))
                    (when (fboundp #'cl--generic-describe)
                      (with-temp-buffer
                        (declare-function cl--generic-describe "cl-generic" (function))
                        (cl--generic-describe func)
                        (goto-char (point-min))
                        (when (re-search-forward "^Implementations:$" nil t)
                          (setq content (buffer-substring (point) (point-max))))))
                    (when content
                      (insert "Implementations\n")
                      (insert (s-trim content) "\n\n")))))

              (when (or source-path primitive-p)
                (insert "Source Code\n")
                (when source-path
                  (insert (format ";; Defined in %s\n" source-path)))
                (when (and primitive-p (not source-path))
                  (insert "C code is not yet loaded.\n")))
              (when source
                (cond
                 ((stringp source)
                  (insert (helpful--syntax-highlight source)))
                 ((and (consp source) (eq (car source) 'closure))
                  (insert (helpful--syntax-highlight
                           (concat ";; Closure converted to defun by helpful.\n"
                                   (helpful--pretty-print
                                    (helpful--format-closure symbol source))))))
                 (t
                  (insert (helpful--syntax-highlight
                           (concat ";; Could not find source code, showing raw function object.\n"
                                   (helpful--pretty-print source))))))
                (insert "\n"))
              (buffer-substring-no-properties (point-min) (point-max)))))
      (fanshi/gptel--tool-call-error-result "Unknown symbol %s" symbol-name))))

  (gptel-make-tool
   :name "ElispHelp"
   :function #'fanshi/gptel-agent--helpful-symbol
   :description "Look up Elisp documentation/source code for a symbol (function, variable, macro, face). Alwyas use this over WebFetch when looking up elisp source code for symbol"
   :args '((:name "symbol-name" :type string
            :description "Name of the Elisp symbol to look up, e.g. \"gptel-send\" or \"buffer-file-name\", default heuretic is callable-first")
           (:name "force-variable" :type boolean
            :description "Flag to force a variable lookup"
            :optional t))
   :category "elisp"
   :async nil))
#+end_src
*** Imenu Tool
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel-read-imenu (file-path)
    "The imenu index of FILE-PATH as a flat list of items with positions.
Opens the file if unvisited. Index comes from `consult-imenu--items-safe'."
    (fanshi/restrict-to-projectile-files-plus-context-a file-path)
    (if-let* ((buffer (or (find-buffer-visiting file-path)
                          (find-file-noselect file-path)))
              (items (with-current-buffer buffer
                       (require 'consult-imenu)
                       (consult-imenu--items-safe))))
        (with-temp-buffer
          (insert "Items:\n")
          (dolist (item items)
            (let ((name (car item))
                  (pos (cdr item)))
              (insert (with-current-buffer buffer
                        (save-excursion
                          (goto-char pos)
                          (format "- %s: line %s, col %s\n"
                                  name
                                  (line-number-at-pos nil t)
                                  (current-column) ))))))
          (gptel-agent--truncate-buffer "imenu")
          (buffer-substring-no-properties (point-min) (point-max)))
      "Error: file not found"))

  (after! gptel
    (gptel-make-tool
     :name "Imenu"
     :function #'fanshi/gptel-read-imenu
     :description "Get the imenu index of a file. Returns a list of all named items with their line/column positions. Use this to understand the file structure before reading or editing - much more efficient than scanning the entire file. Takes a file_path argument to specify which file to index."
     :args '((:name "file_path"
              :type string
              :description "The path to the file whose imenu index to retrieve"))
     :category "read-tools"
     :async nil)))
#+end_src
*** Tool Groups
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(defvar fanshi/research-tools '("read-tools" "web-read-tools"))
(defvar fanshi/coding-tools '("read-tools" "Edit")
  "Base tools for the coding preset. Web tools are excluded on purpose;
re-add with `@web'.")
(defvar fanshi/scrum-tools '("read-tools" "QueryBacklog" "BreakdownTask" "Delegate")
  "The Scrum Master's tools.")
(defvar fanshi/web-tools '("WebFetch" "WebSearch")
  "Web tools, listed explicitly instead of via the \"web-read-tools\" category,
which also holds YouTube.")
(after! gptel-agent
  (defun fanshi/update-tools-category (new-category toolnames-list)
    "Move TOOLNAMES-LIST from their current categories to NEW-CATEGORY."
    (dolist (toolname toolnames-list)
      (let* ((tool (gptel-get-tool toolname))
             (old-category (gptel-tool-category tool)))
        (unless (string= old-category new-category)
          (aset tool 6 new-category)
          (push (cons toolname tool) (alist-get new-category gptel--known-tools nil nil #'equal))
          (let ((old-alist (alist-get old-category gptel--known-tools nil nil #'equal)))
            (when old-alist
              (setf (alist-get old-category gptel--known-tools nil nil #'equal)
                    (assoc-delete-all toolname old-alist #'equal))))))))

  (fanshi/update-tools-category "read-tools" '("Glob" "Imenu" "Read" "Grep"))
  (fanshi/update-tools-category "write-tools" '("Edit" "Write"))
  (fanshi/update-tools-category "web-read-tools" '("WebSearch" "WebFetch" "YouTube")))
#+end_src
** Tool call enhancement
Validate tool-call args before execution, and return failures as an error result
that hints at the correct usage.
*** fanshi/gptel--validate-tool-args-hook
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defun fanshi/gptel--validate-tool-args-hook (tool-call)
  "`gptel-pre-tool-call-functions' hook: validate TOOL-CALL, else `(:block ..)'."
  (let ((name (plist-get tool-call :name))
        (buffer (plist-get tool-call :buffer)))
    (cond
     ((not name) `(:block "Error: tool called with empty name"))
     ((not (gptel-get-tool name)) `(:block ,(format "Error: Unknown tool called: %s" name)))

     (t
      ;; `gptel-tools' is buffer-local: a globally known tool can be unequipped here.
      (if-let* ((tools (with-current-buffer buffer
                         (or fanshi/last-presets-gptel-tools
                             gptel-tools)))
                (tool-spec (seq-find (lambda (tool) (equal name (gptel-tool-name tool))) tools)))
          (if-let ((errors (fanshi/gptel--validate-tool-args tool-spec (plist-get tool-call :args))))
              (unless (string= errors "") `(:block ,errors))
            (when (member name fanshi/safe-tool) `(:confirm nil)))
        `(:block ,(format "Error: tool %s is not available" name)))))))
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel--validate-simple-tool-arg-type (type arg)
    "Whether ARG has the simple (non-aggregate) tool arg TYPE."
    (pcase type
      ((or 'null "null") (null arg))
      ((or 'string "string") (stringp arg))
      ((or 'number "number") (numberp arg))
      ((or 'integer "integer") (integerp arg))
      ((or 'boolean "boolean") (or (and (booleanp arg) (eq t arg)) (eq :json-false arg)))
      (_ (progn (message "Warning: FIXME tool call validation skipped for type %s: %s" type arg) t))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--validate-tool-args (tool-spec args)
    "Validate ARGS (a plist) against TOOL-SPEC, a `gptel-tool'.
Nil when valid, else a string describing the errors."
    (let* ((tool-spec-args (gptel-tool-args tool-spec))
           (expected-args (mapcar (lambda (pl) (plist-get pl :name)) tool-spec-args))
           (unepected-arg-errs
            (mapconcat
             (lambda (input-arg)
               (let ((tool-name (s-chop-prefix ":" (symbol-name input-arg))) )
                 (unless (member tool-name expected-args)
                   (format "Error: tool call arg %s is not expected.\n" tool-name))))
             (doom-plist-keys args) "")))
      (if (string-empty-p unepected-arg-errs)
          (mapconcat
           (lambda (spec-arg)
             (let* ((name (plist-get spec-arg :name))
                    (input_arg (plist-get args (intern (concat ":" name))))
                    (description (plist-get spec-arg :description))
                    (optional (plist-get spec-arg :optional))
                    (enum (plist-get spec-arg :enum))
                    (type (plist-get spec-arg :type))
                    (simple-error (format "Error: tool call arg %s (%s) expected type %s but got %s\n\n" name description type input_arg)))
               (unless (and optional (null input_arg))
                 (cond
                  ((not (fanshi/gptel--validate-simple-tool-arg-type type input_arg)) simple-error)
                  ((and enum (null (seq-position enum input_arg))) (format "Error: tool call arg %s (%s) expected enum (in %s) but got %s\n\n" name description enum input_arg))
                  ((and (eq type 'array) (not (listp input_arg))) simple-error)
                  ((eq type 'array) (let ((items (plist-get spec-arg :items)))
                                      (when (and items (all (lambda (item) (not (fanshi/gptel--validate-simple-tool-arg-type item input_arg optional))) items))
                                        (format "Error: tool call arg %s (%s) expected array with item type in %s but got %s\n\n" name description items input_arg))))
                  ;; FIXME no tool has an object arg yet, so objects are unvalidated.
                  ((eq type 'object) nil)
                  (t nil)))))
           (gptel-tool-args tool-spec) "")
        (format "%s\n expected %s" unepected-arg-errs expected-args))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--validate-tool-args-hook (tool-call)
    "`gptel-pre-tool-call-functions' hook: validate TOOL-CALL, else `(:block ..)'."
    (let ((name (plist-get tool-call :name))
          (buffer (plist-get tool-call :buffer)))
      (cond
       ((not name) `(:block "Error: tool called with empty name"))
       ((not (gptel-get-tool name)) `(:block ,(format "Error: Unknown tool called: %s" name)))

       (t
        ;; `gptel-tools' is buffer-local: a globally known tool can be unequipped here.
        (if-let* ((tools (with-current-buffer buffer
                           (or fanshi/last-presets-gptel-tools
                               gptel-tools)))
                  (tool-spec (seq-find (lambda (tool) (equal name (gptel-tool-name tool))) tools)))
            (let ((errors (fanshi/gptel--validate-tool-args tool-spec (plist-get tool-call :args))))
              (if (string= errors "")
                  (when (member name fanshi/safe-tool) `(:confirm nil))
                `(:block ,errors)))
          `(:block ,(format "Error: tool %s is not available" name)))))))

  ;; Depth 80: the project-rule check runs at 90, so a ban can still override the
  ;; `(:confirm nil)' for a tool in `fanshi/safe-tool'.
  (add-hook 'gptel-pre-tool-call-functions
            #'fanshi/gptel--validate-tool-args-hook 80)

  (defun fanshi/gptel--process-tool-call-a (args)
    "Replace a nil tool-spec in ARGS with a dummy `gptel-tool'.
A nil spec makes `gptel--display-tool-results' signal in the sentinel and halt the
request."
    (when (null (cadr args))
      (setf (cadr args) (gptel--make-tool-internal)))
    args)
  (advice-add 'gptel--process-tool-call :filter-args #'fanshi/gptel--process-tool-call-a))
#+end_src
*** Require Imenu before Read
Block `Read' on a file not yet indexed with `Imenu' this session, so the agent gets
a structure index instead of the whole file. Files whose mode signals
`imenu-unavailable' are never blocked. A buffer-local alist records indexed files:
the `Read' pre-tool hook checks it, the `Imenu' post-tool hook updates it.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-imenu-before-read t
    "Block `Read' on files not yet indexed with `Imenu' this session."
    :type 'boolean
    :group 'gptel)

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-imenu-tool-call-file-states nil
    "Alist (FILE-PATH . BLOCKED-P) of files in the Imenu/Read dance.")

  (defun fanshi/gptel--imenu-makes-sense-p (file-path)
    "Whether `Imenu' works for FILE-PATH, by indexing its buffer."
    (if-let* ((buffer (or (find-buffer-visiting file-path)
                          (find-file-noselect file-path))))
        (with-current-buffer buffer
          (require 'imenu)
          (condition-case nil
              (progn
                (funcall imenu-create-index-function)
                t)
            (imenu-unavailable nil)))
      nil))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--imenu-tool-called (tool-call)
    "`gptel-post-tool-call-functions' hook: record a successful `Imenu' call."
    (when (string= (plist-get tool-call :name) "Imenu")
      (let* ((args (plist-get tool-call :args))
             (file-path (plist-get args :file_path))
             (buf (plist-get tool-call :buffer)))
        (when (and file-path buf)
          (with-current-buffer buf
            (let ((entry (assoc file-path fanshi/gptel-imenu-tool-call-file-states)))
              (if entry
                  (setcdr entry nil)
                (push (cons file-path nil) fanshi/gptel-imenu-tool-call-file-states))))))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--imenu-before-read (tool-call)
    "`gptel-pre-tool-call-functions' hook: require `Imenu' before `Read'.
Blocks the call when the file is not yet indexed and imenu applies."
    (when (and fanshi/gptel-imenu-before-read
               (string= (plist-get tool-call :name) "Read"))
      (let* ((args (plist-get tool-call :args))
             (file-path (plist-get args :file_path))
             (buf (plist-get tool-call :buffer)))
        (when (and file-path
                   (file-exists-p file-path)
                   (not (with-current-buffer buf
                          (assoc file-path fanshi/gptel-imenu-tool-call-file-states)))
                   (fanshi/gptel--imenu-makes-sense-p file-path))
          (with-current-buffer buf
            (push (cons file-path 'need-imenu-call) fanshi/gptel-imenu-tool-call-file-states))
          `(:block ,(format "Error: You are about to Read %s without first calling Imenu on it. Call the Imenu tool on this file first to get its structure index, then Read the relevant line range." file-path))))))

  (add-hook 'gptel-post-tool-call-functions #'fanshi/gptel--imenu-tool-called)
  (add-hook 'gptel-pre-tool-call-functions #'fanshi/gptel--imenu-before-read))
#+end_src
*** Timout when tool calling asking for user confirmation
The user may be away, so auto-reject after a timeout and tell the model to find
another way.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defvar fanshi/gptel-tool-call-confirm-timeout 30)
  (defvar fanshi/gptel-tool-call-confirm-mutex (make-mutex))
  (defvar fanshi/tool-call-confirm-timer)

  (defun fanshi/gptel--display-tool-calls-a (tool-calls info &optional _use-minibuffer)
    "Start the auto-reject timer for pending TOOL-CALLS."
    (let ((ov (cdr-safe (get-char-property-and-overlay (point) 'gptel-tool))))
      (setq fanshi/tool-call-confirm-timer
            (run-at-time
             fanshi/gptel-tool-call-confirm-timeout nil
             (lambda ()
               (with-mutex fanshi/gptel-tool-call-confirm-mutex
                 (gptel--reject-tool-calls tool-calls ov)))))))

  (advice-add 'gptel--display-tool-calls :after #'fanshi/gptel--display-tool-calls-a)

  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent--clear-tool-calls-confirmation-timer (&rest _args)
    "Cancel the pending confirmation timer once the user has answered."
    (when (mutexp fanshi/gptel-tool-call-confirm-mutex)
      (with-mutex fanshi/gptel-tool-call-confirm-mutex
        (when fanshi/tool-call-confirm-timer
          (cancel-timer fanshi/tool-call-confirm-timer)
          (setq fanshi/tool-call-confirm-timer nil)))))
  (advice-add 'gptel--accept-tool-calls :before #'fanshi/gptel-agent--clear-tool-calls-confirmation-timer)
  (advice-add 'gptel--reject-tool-calls :before #'fanshi/gptel-agent--clear-tool-calls-confirmation-timer))
#+end_src
*** Force Read tool to read less
**** fanshi/read-tool-max-lines
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defcustom fanshi/read-tool-max-lines 200
  "Max lines the Read tool may read at once.")
#+end_src
**** fanshi/gptel-agent--read-file-lines-a
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel-agent
  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent--read-file-lines-a (filename start-line end-line)
    "Reject a Read of FILENAME whose range is inverted or over the max."
    (unless (>= end-line start-line)
      (error "Error: Read tool end-line %s < startline %s" end-line start-line ))
    (unless (<= (- end-line start-line) fanshi/read-tool-max-lines)
      (error "Error: Read tool only allow reading %s lines each time, you tried to read from line %s to line %s" fanshi/read-tool-max-lines start-line end-line)))

  (advice-add 'gptel-agent--read-file-lines :before #'fanshi/gptel-agent--read-file-lines-a))
#+end_src
**** Update tool doc
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel-agent
  (when-let* ((read-tool (gptel-get-tool "Read")))
    (aset read-tool 3 "Read file contents between specified line numbers `start_line` and `end_line`,
     with both ends included.

     Consider using the \"Imenu\" tool to find the right range to read first.

     Read at most `fanshi/read-tool-max-lines' lines at a time")
    (aset read-tool 4 '((:name "file_path"
                         :type "string"
                         :description "The path to the file to be read.")
                        (:name "start_line"
                         :type "integer"
                         :description "The line to start reading from"
                         )
                        (:name "end_line"
                         :type "integer"
                         :description "The line up to which to read")))
    (setf
     (alist-get "Read"
                (alist-get (gptel-tool-category (gptel-get-tool "Read")) gptel--known-tools nil nil #'equal)
                nil nil #'equal)
     read-tool)))
#+end_src
*** Tool Call Failures removal
Once a retry of a failed tool call succeeds, mark the earlier failed blocks with the
`'ignore' text property so `gptel--parse-buffer' skips them; they stay visible for
debugging.

Tools must not let a bare signal escape: gptel flattens it to text with *no* error
marker, which reads as success. Return `fanshi/gptel--tool-call-error-result' (or
text starting with "Error:") instead.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel--tool-call-error-result (format-string &rest args)
    "Tool-call error result: FORMAT-STRING/ARGS wrapped in `<tool_call_error>'.
Use this instead of signalling: a flattened signal carries no error marker."
    (concat "<tool_call_error>\n"
            (apply #'format format-string args)
            "\n</tool_call_error>"))

  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-clean-failed-tool-calls t
    "Hide failed tool calls preceding a successful retry of the same tool."
    :type 'boolean
    :group 'gptel)

  (defun fanshi/gptel--tool-call-is-error-p (tool-call)
    "Whether TOOL-CALL's result is an error.
An error is `:error t' (a hook blocked the call), or a string :result starting with
\"Error:\" or `<tool_call_error>'."
    (let ((result (plist-get tool-call :result)))
      (or (eq t (plist-get tool-call :error))
          (and (stringp result)
               (let ((r (string-trim-left result)))
                 (or (string-prefix-p "<tool_call_error>" r)
                     (string-prefix-p "Error:" r)))))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--hide-failed-tool-calls (tool-call)
    "`gptel-post-tool-call-functions' hook: hide same-tool failures before a success.
Walks backward over the chat buffer, marking each preceding failure of the same tool
with 'ignore."
    (when fanshi/gptel-clean-failed-tool-calls
      (let* ((name (plist-get tool-call :name))
             (orig-buf (get-buffer (plist-get tool-call :buffer))))
        (when (and orig-buf (not (fanshi/gptel--tool-call-is-error-p tool-call)))
          (with-current-buffer orig-buf
            (save-excursion
              (goto-char (point-max))
              ;; The current success may not be in the buffer yet.
              (while (/= (point) (point-min))
                (let ((end (point))
                      (start (goto-char (previous-single-property-change (point) 'gptel nil (point-min)))))
                  (when-let* ((prop (get-char-property (point) 'gptel))
                              (tool-p (eq (car-safe prop) 'tool)))
                    (condition-case result
                        (save-excursion
                          (let* ((tool-call-text (read (current-buffer)))
                                 (call-name (let ((n (plist-get tool-call-text :name)))
                                              (and (stringp n) n)))
                                 (result-start (point)))
                            (cond
                             ((and (string= name call-name)
                                   (progn
                                     (plist-put tool-call-text :result
                                                (buffer-substring-no-properties
                                                 result-start
                                                 (save-match-data
                                                   (if (save-excursion
                                                         (goto-char result-start)
                                                         (re-search-forward
                                                          "\n#+end_tool" end t))
                                                       (match-beginning 0)
                                                     end))))
                                     (fanshi/gptel--tool-call-is-error-p
                                      tool-call-text)))
                              (put-text-property start end 'gptel 'ignore))
                             ;; Stop at first different tool or non-failure
                             (t 'done))))
                      (:success (when (equal result 'done) (goto-char (point-min))))
                      (t nil)))
                  (ignore-errors (backward-char))))))))))

  (add-hook 'gptel-post-tool-call-functions
            #'fanshi/gptel--hide-failed-tool-calls))
#+end_src
** Loop Guard: detect and break degenerate LLM loops
Two detectors, one per kind of degeneration:

- *Type A — mid-stream*: one completion repeats the same passage verbatim. Detected
  incrementally from a rolling shingle counter in =gptel-post-stream-hook=; on trip,
  the request is aborted. Tool blocks are stripped before shingling; repeated tool
  calls are counted separately from a canonical key over the whole agentic chain,
  and only byte-identical *consecutive* calls count.
- *Type B — inter-turn*: consecutive responses are near-duplicates, detected post
  response by bigram Jaccard similarity: one nudge, then stop intervening.

Type A aborts the in-flight request; Type B runs after the turn, so it can only
notify. =fanshi/gptel-auto-continue-max-retries= backstops repetition that shows up
as auto-continues (the budgets are independent).

Both catch errors from the *detector* only, so a real bug in nudge, notify or the
deferred abort surfaces.

Abort flags: =fanshi/gptel--aborted= makes the auto-continue handler skip the turn,
=fanshi/gptel-loop--aborted= makes Type B skip it. Manual aborts arm both via
=fanshi/gptel-mark-turn-aborted=.

Both read but never modify the buffer, apart from `read'ing a tool-call plist for
its key. A malformed plist makes =fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-call-key= return nil;
`read' on a truncated one signals =end-of-file=, deliberately not caught.

Both need the response region *stable* between chunks: the shingler compares the
previous normalized text as a prefix of the current one, so a mid-stream edit
restarts detection.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-loop-guard-enabled t
    "Detect and break degenerate LLM loops."
    :type 'boolean
    :group 'gptel)

  ;; ---------- Type A: mid-stream shingle detector ----------

  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-loop-shingle-words 12
    "Number of words per shingle for mid-stream loop detection."
    :type 'integer
    :group 'gptel)

  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-loop-repeat-threshold 3
    "Occurrences of a shingle (within `fanshi/gptel-loop-repeat-max-gap') that declare a loop."
    :type 'integer
    :group 'gptel)

  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-loop-repeat-max-gap 300
    "Max word distance between re-occurrences of a shingle to count as repetition."
    :type 'integer
    :group 'gptel)

  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-loop-redundancy-window 60
    "Rolling window (in shingles) for the redundancy-rate check."
    :type 'integer
    :group 'gptel)

  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-loop-redundancy-threshold 0.6
    "Fraction of shingles in the window already seen before a loop is declared."
    :type 'float
    :group 'gptel)

  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-loop-min-words 150
    "Minimum response length (words) before mid-stream detection activates."
    :type 'integer
    :group 'gptel)

  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-loop-tool-repeat-threshold 3
    "Consecutive identical tool calls before aborting."
    :type 'integer
    :group 'gptel)

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-loop--state nil
    "Mid-stream state: (NORM-PREV PREV-WORDS NEXT-I SHINGLE-POS RING RING-POS RING-COUNT).

Drop the whole variable to nil when the detector is skipped for a chunk — never just
the prefix.")

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-loop--pending-abort nil
    "Process of a request whose mid-stream trip has not been aborted yet, or nil.
One trip per buffer, so under `fanshi/gptel-multisend' a second request's trip is
skipped — and may end up aborting the sibling instead.")

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-scan-pos nil
    "Position up to which tool-call blocks have been scanned.
Monotone within an agentic turn.")

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-last-key nil
    "Canonical key of the last full tool call scanned.")

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-consecutive 0
    "Consecutive identical full tool calls seen.")

  (defconst fanshi/gptel-loop--footer-window 64
    "Chars to search past a tool block's property run for its footer.")

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--normalize (text)
    "Collapse whitespace in TEXT and downcase it, for shingling."
    (downcase (replace-regexp-in-string "[[:space:]]+" " " text)))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--detect-response (text)
    "Scan response TEXT incrementally for degenerate repetition.
A reason string, or nil. Only shingles completed since the previous call are
recorded, so calling it with the full response region on every chunk is safe. State
resets when the region restarts or shrinks.

Callers must NOT skip this while leaving the state armed."
    (let* ((sw fanshi/gptel-loop-shingle-words)
           (rw fanshi/gptel-loop-redundancy-window)
           (norm (fanshi/gptel-loop--normalize text))
           (state fanshi/gptel-loop--state)
           (norm-prev (nth 0 state))
           (prev-words (nth 1 state))
           (next-i (nth 2 state))
           (shingle-pos (nth 3 state))
           (ring (nth 4 state))
           (ring-pos (nth 5 state))
           (ring-count (nth 6 state))
           (words nil) (i 0) (reason nil))
      (cond
       ;; New sub-response: region restarted, or text changed underneath us.
       ((or (null state)
            (< (length norm) (length norm-prev))
            (not (string-prefix-p norm-prev norm)))
        (setq words (split-string norm " " 'suppress-empty)
              shingle-pos (make-hash-table :test #'equal :size 512)
              ring (make-vector rw nil)
              ring-pos 0
              ring-count 0
              i 0
              next-i 0))
       ;; Continue from the frontier: shingles within `sw' words of the previous
       ;; end were incomplete, so revisit them.
       (t (setq words (nconc prev-words
                             (split-string (substring norm (length norm-prev)) " " 'suppress-empty))
                i (min next-i (length words)))))
      (let ((n (length words)))
        ;; Below `fanshi/gptel-loop-min-words' the frontier does not advance, so
        ;; later calls re-walk shingles that completed meanwhile.
        (when (>= n fanshi/gptel-loop-min-words)
          (while (and (< i n) (<= (+ i sw) n) (not reason))
            (let* ((shingle (mapconcat #'identity (seq-subseq words i (+ i sw)) " "))
                   (prev (gethash shingle shingle-pos)))
              (if prev
                  (let ((count (1+ (car prev))))
                    (puthash shingle (cons count i) shingle-pos)
                    (when (and (<= (- i (cdr prev)) fanshi/gptel-loop-repeat-max-gap)
                               (>= count fanshi/gptel-loop-repeat-threshold))
                      (setq reason (format "shingle repeated %d times: \"%s...\""
                                           count (substring shingle 0 (min 60 (length shingle)))))))
                (puthash shingle (cons 1 i) shingle-pos))
              (aset ring ring-pos shingle)
              (setq ring-pos (mod (1+ ring-pos) rw)
                    ring-count (min (1+ ring-count) rw)))
            (cl-incf i))
          (setq next-i i))
        ;; Redundancy: fraction of the recorded shingles in the window that recur
        ;; near the end. After the record loop, so the ring is complete.
        (when (and (not reason) (= ring-count rw))
          (let ((seen 0))
            (dotimes (idx rw)
              (let ((s (aref ring idx)))
                (when-let ((entry (and s (gethash s shingle-pos))))
                  (when (and (>= (car entry) 2)
                             (<= (- n (cdr entry)) fanshi/gptel-loop-repeat-max-gap))
                    (cl-incf seen)))))
            (when (>= (/ (float seen) rw) fanshi/gptel-loop-redundancy-threshold)
              (setq reason (format "redundancy: %d of last %d shingles re-occur within %d words"
                                   seen rw fanshi/gptel-loop-repeat-max-gap)))))
        (setq-local fanshi/gptel-loop--state (list norm words next-i shingle-pos ring ring-pos ring-count))
        reason)))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-state-reset (&optional scan-pos)
    "Reset the tool-call detector, resuming the scan from SCAN-POS."
    (setq fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-scan-pos scan-pos
          fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-last-key nil
          fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-consecutive 0))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-call-key (beg)
    "Canonical key for the tool call at BEG, or nil if unreadable.
Arguments can be megabytes, so the key is \"name sxhash prefix\". A malformed block
yields nil; `end-of-file' from a truncated plist is not caught."
    (save-excursion
      (condition-case nil
          (progn
            (goto-char beg)
            (let ((call (read (current-buffer))))
              (when (and (consp call) (plist-member call :name))
                (let ((args (prin1-to-string (plist-get call :args))))
                  (format "%s %x %s"
                          (or (plist-get call :name) "")
                          (sxhash args)
                          (truncate-string-to-width args 100 nil nil t))))))
        ((wrong-type-argument invalid-read-syntax) nil))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-block-bounds-1 (block-start call-start end)
    "(BLOCK-START CALL-START BLOCK-END) from a block's header and body."
    (let ((run-end (or (next-single-property-change call-start 'gptel nil end) end)))
      (list block-start call-start
            (fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-block-end run-end end))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-block-bounds (pos end)
    "(BLOCK-START CALL-START BLOCK-END) for the tool block at POS, or nil.
POS is where the `gptel' property changes at a block's start; END bounds the
footer search.

Which part of a block carries the property varies by front-end, hence the three
shape tests: the body run, an 'ignore run (Markdown header, or a block hidden by
`fanshi/gptel--hide-failed-tool-calls'), or an unpropertized/'response run just
before the body."
    (let ((prop (get-char-property pos 'gptel)))
      (cond
       ((eq 'tool (car-safe prop))
        (fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-block-bounds-1
         (save-excursion (goto-char pos) (line-beginning-position)) pos end))
       ((eq prop 'ignore)
        (save-excursion
          (goto-char pos)
          (beginning-of-line)
          (let ((line (point)))
            (cond
             ((looking-at-p "[ \t]*```[ \t]*tool")
              (forward-line)
              (fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-block-bounds-1 line (point) end))
             ((looking-at-p "[ \t]*#\\+begin_tool")
              (fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-block-bounds-1 line pos end))
             (t nil)))))
       ((or (null prop) (eq prop 'response))
        ;; Locate the header from the tool run that follows.
        (save-excursion
          (let ((next (next-single-property-change pos 'gptel nil end)))
            (when (and next (eq 'tool (car-safe (get-char-property next 'gptel))))
              (let ((line (progn (goto-char next) (line-beginning-position))))
                (if (and (<= pos line)
                         (looking-at-p "[ \t]*#\\+begin_tool")
                         (string-blank-p
                          (buffer-substring-no-properties pos line)))
                    (fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-block-bounds-1 line next end)
                  nil))))))
       (t nil))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-block-end (run-end end)
    "End of the tool block whose property run ends at RUN-END.
The footer (`#+end_tool' or a Markdown fence) normally is not part of the run, so it
follows RUN-END and is found here; otherwise RUN-END."
    (save-excursion
      (goto-char (max (1- run-end) (point-min)))
      (if (and (re-search-forward "\n\\(?:#\\+end_tool[^\n]*\n?\\|```[ \t]*\\(?:\n\\|\\'\\)\\)"
                                  (min end (+ run-end fanshi/gptel-loop--footer-window)) t)
               (<= (match-beginning 0) run-end))
          (point)
        run-end)))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-gap-p (from to)
    "Whether FROM TO holds only tool blocks, footers and separators.
I.e. \"same turn, tool call in between\", so those blocks still count; anything else
means the turn changed."
    (let ((pos from)
          (junk (list (and (boundp 'gptel-response-separator)
                           (default-value 'gptel-response-separator))
                      (and (fboundp 'gptel-response-prefix-string)
                           (ignore-errors (funcall #'gptel-response-prefix-string))))))
      (while (and pos (< pos to))
        (let ((block (fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-block-bounds pos to))
              (next (or (next-single-property-change pos 'gptel nil to) to)))
          (cond
           (block
            ;; Skip the block plus its footer, which is not `tool'.
            (setq pos (max (nth 2 block) (1+ pos))))
           ((eq (get-char-property pos 'gptel) 'ignore)
            (setq pos next))
           (t
            (let ((rest (buffer-substring-no-properties pos next)))
              (setq rest (replace-regexp-in-string
                          "[ \t]*#\\+\\(?:begin\\|end\\)_tool[^\n]*[ \t]*" "" rest))
              (dolist (j junk)
                (when (stringp j) (setq rest (string-replace j "" rest))))
              (setq pos (if (string-blank-p rest) next nil))))))
        (and pos t))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--response-parts (scan-start end text-start &optional read-keys)
    "Scan tool blocks between SCAN-START and END for (TEXT KEYS SCAN-POS).
TEXT is the text from TEXT-START to END with tool blocks removed. KEYS are the
canonical keys of blocks at or after SCAN-START, and only when READ-KEYS is non-nil.
SCAN-POS is the end of the last block scanned."
    (let ((pos (min scan-start text-start))
          (last-copy text-start)
          (segments nil)
          (keys nil)
          (scan-pos scan-start))
      (while (< pos end)
        (let ((next (next-single-property-change pos 'gptel nil end)))
          (if (or (null next) (>= next end))
              (progn
                (when (< last-copy end)
                  (push (buffer-substring-no-properties last-copy end) segments))
                (setq pos end))
            (let ((block (fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-block-bounds next end)))
              (if (null block)
                  (setq pos next)
                (cl-destructuring-bind (b-start call-start b-end) block
                  (let ((new-block (>= b-start scan-pos))
                        (block-start (max last-copy b-start))
                        (block-end (max last-copy b-end)))
                    (when (> block-start last-copy)
                      (push (buffer-substring-no-properties last-copy block-start) segments))
                    (when (and new-block read-keys)
                      (push (fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-call-key call-start) keys))
                    (setq scan-pos (max scan-pos block-end)
                          last-copy block-end
                          pos (max last-copy (1+ next))))))))))
      (when (< last-copy end)
        (push (buffer-substring-no-properties last-copy end) segments))
      (list (apply #'concat (nreverse segments)) (nreverse keys) scan-pos)))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--response-text (start end)
    "The text of region START END with tool blocks removed."
    (nth 0 (fanshi/gptel-loop--response-parts start end start)))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--detect-tool-repetition (keys)
    "Reason if KEYS contain `fanshi/gptel-loop-tool-repeat-threshold' repeats.
A nil key (unreadable block) breaks the streak."
    (let ((threshold fanshi/gptel-loop-tool-repeat-threshold)
          (reason nil))
      (dolist (key keys)
        (cond
         ((and key (equal key fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-last-key))
          (cl-incf fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-consecutive))
         (key
          (setq fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-last-key key
                fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-consecutive 1))
         (t (setq fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-last-key nil
                  fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-consecutive 0)))
        (when (and key (>= fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-consecutive threshold))
          (setq reason (format "tool call repeated %d times: %s"
                               fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-consecutive key))))
      reason))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--detect-stream (start end)
    "Update the mid-stream detectors over the response region START END.
A reason to abort, or nil. START is the start of the current `response' run, END the
position to scan up to.

Tool scanning is not keyed to START: a tool block interrupts the `response' run, so
keying the streak off START would reset it at every tool call."
    (let* ((scan-start fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-scan-pos)
           parts text keys reason)
      (when (or (null scan-start)
                (> scan-start end)
                (and (< scan-start start)
                     (not (fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-gap-p scan-start start))))
        (fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-state-reset start)
        (setq scan-start start))
      (setq parts (fanshi/gptel-loop--response-parts scan-start end start t)
            text (nth 0 parts)
            keys (nth 1 parts))
      (setq fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-scan-pos (nth 2 parts))
      (setq reason (fanshi/gptel-loop--detect-response text))
      (or reason (fanshi/gptel-loop--detect-tool-repetition keys))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--stream-check ()
    "`gptel-post-stream-hook' handler: abort the request if the response degenerates.

Runs inside gptel's process filter, so only detection happens here; the abort is
deferred to idle time and acts on the recorded process through the FSM and abort
closure, bypassing `gptel-abort'.

The response region comes from the `gptel' text property, never `point-max': tool
output would pollute the shingler. With no run at point there is nothing to
shingle, so the detector and its state are dropped.

Must return nil: this hook runs at the end of the process filter."
    (when (and fanshi/gptel-loop-guard-enabled
               gptel-mode
               (not fanshi/gptel-loop--pending-abort))
      (let ((scan-bak fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-scan-pos)
            (key-bak fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-last-key)
            (count-bak fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-consecutive))
        (condition-case err
            (let* ((no-region-p nil)
                   (region
                    (when (and (> (point) (point-min))
                               (eq 'response (get-text-property (1- (point)) 'gptel)))
                      (let ((match (save-excursion
                                     (text-property-search-backward 'gptel 'response))))
                        (cons (if match (prop-match-beginning match) (point-min))
                              (point)))))
                   (reason (if region
                               (fanshi/gptel-loop--detect-stream (car region)
                                                                 (cdr region))
                             ;; No `response' run: nothing to shingle.
                             (progn
                               (setq no-region-p t
                                     fanshi/gptel-loop--state nil)
                               nil))))
              (when reason
                (when-let* ((entry (cl-find-if
                                    (lambda (e)
                                      (eq (plist-get (gptel-fsm-info (cadr e)) :buffer)
                                          (current-buffer)))
                                    gptel--request-alist))
                            (process (car entry))
                            (other-trip-p
                             (let ((p fanshi/gptel-loop--pending-abort))
                               (and (processp p) (process-live-p p)))))
                  (if other-trip-p
                      (message "gptel-loop-guard: mid-stream loop detected (%s), another trip pending; deferring."
                               reason)
                    (setq-local fanshi/gptel-loop--pending-abort process)
                    (setq fanshi/gptel-loop--state nil)
                    (unless no-region-p
                      (fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-state-reset (cdr region)))
                    (setq-local fanshi/gptel--aborted t
                                fanshi/gptel-loop--aborted t)
                    (message "gptel-loop-guard: mid-stream loop detected (%s), aborting." reason)
                    (let ((buf (current-buffer)))
                      (run-with-idle-timer
                       0 nil
                       (lambda ()
                         (when (buffer-live-p buf)
                           (with-current-buffer buf
                             (let* ((pending fanshi/gptel-loop--pending-abort)
                                    (proc (and (processp pending)
                                               (process-live-p pending)
                                               pending))
                                    (entry (and proc (alist-get proc gptel--request-alist)))
                                    (fsm (and entry (car entry)))
                                    (abort-fn (and entry (cdr entry)))
                                    (fsm-info (and fsm (gptel-fsm-info fsm)))
                                    (cb (and fsm-info (plist-get fsm-info :callback))))
                               (setq-local fanshi/gptel-loop--pending-abort nil)
                               (when (and proc fsm (functionp abort-fn))
                                 (setq-local fanshi/gptel--aborted t
                                             fanshi/gptel-loop--aborted t)
                                 (when (functionp cb)
                                   (with-demoted-errors "loop-guard callback: %S"
                                     (funcall cb 'abort fsm-info)))
                                 (ignore-errors (funcall abort-fn))
                                 (setf (alist-get proc gptel--request-alist nil 'remove) nil)
                                 (gptel--fsm-transition fsm 'ABRT)
                                 (setq-local fanshi/gptel--aborted t
                                             fanshi/gptel-loop--aborted t)
                                 (fanshi/notify))
                               ;; Nothing to abort: the turn completed and its hooks ran.
                               (unless proc
                                 (setq-local fanshi/gptel--aborted nil
                                             fanshi/gptel-loop--aborted nil))))))))))))
          (error
           ;; Best-effort rollback of the tool-call state; the shingle state is
           ;; dropped, not restored.
           (setq fanshi/gptel-loop--state nil
                 fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-scan-pos scan-bak
                 fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-last-key key-bak
                 fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-consecutive count-bak)
           (message "gptel-loop-guard: stream check failed: %s"
                    (error-message-string err)))
          ;; Must return nil.
          nil))))

  (add-hook 'gptel-post-stream-hook #'fanshi/gptel-loop--stream-check)

  ;; ---------- Type B: inter-turn similarity ----------

  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-loop-similarity-threshold 0.8
    "Bigram Jaccard similarity between consecutive responses that suspects a loop."
    :type 'float
    :group 'gptel)

  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-loop-nudge-max 1
    "Nudges before giving up on an inter-turn loop. Refilled only by a clean turn."
    :type 'integer
    :group 'gptel)

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-loop--last-response nil
    "Normalized word list of the previous assistant response.")

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-loop--nudge-count 0
    "Nudges sent so far. Not reset by `fanshi/gptel-send--reset-loop-state-a': it
must survive the nudge's own send.")

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-loop--aborted nil
    "Non-nil if this buffer's last request was aborted (guard or user).")

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--normalize-words (text)
    "Strip markup and downcase TEXT into a word list."
    (split-string
     (replace-regexp-in-string
      "[#*+|`~\\[\\]{}=:-]" " "
      (downcase (replace-regexp-in-string "[[:space:]]+" " " text)))
     " " 'suppress-empty))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--jaccard (a b)
    "Bigram Jaccard similarity between word lists A and B, or nil if nothing to compare."
    (let ((inter 0) (union 0))
      (when (and a b)
        (let ((ba (make-hash-table :test #'equal))
              (bb (make-hash-table :test #'equal)))
          (cl-loop for w1 on a
                   while (cdr w1)
                   for bg = (concat (car w1) " " (cadr w1))
                   do (puthash bg (1+ (gethash bg 0)) ba))
          (cl-loop for w1 on b
                   while (cdr w1)
                   for bg = (concat (car w1) " " (cadr w1))
                   do (puthash bg (1+ (gethash bg 0)) bb))
          (maphash (lambda (bg va)
                     (let ((vb (gethash bg 0 bb)))
                       (cl-incf union (max va vb))
                       (when vb (cl-incf inter (min va vb)))))
                   ba)
          (maphash (lambda (bg vb)
                     (unless (gethash bg ba) (cl-incf union vb)))
                   bb)))
      (if (= union 0) nil (/ (float inter) union))))

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-loop--from-nudge nil
    "Non-nil when the guard's own nudge produced the current turn.")

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--nudge ()
    "Ask the agent to break its loop."
    (setq-local fanshi/gptel-loop--from-nudge t)
    (let ((fanshi/prevent-duplicate-gptel-send nil))
      (without-restriction
        (setq-local fanshi/gptel--auto-continued t)
        (goto-char (point-max))
        (insert "\n\n"
                "STOP: you are repeating the same content with no progress. "
                "Do not repeat what you just wrote. Either take a concrete different action, "
                "or state in one sentence exactly what is blocking you.\n")
        (gptel-send))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-loop--post-response-check (beg end)
    "`gptel-post-response-functions' handler: detect near-duplicate responses.

Similarity is computed before the nudge is sent, since `gptel-send' runs the reset
advice that clears the state read here. Registered at position 10, ahead of the
auto-continue hook at 100.

Unlike Type A this does not abort — the turn is complete. The policy is
`fanshi/gptel-loop-nudge-max' nudges, then only notify."
    (let ((from-nudge fanshi/gptel-loop--from-nudge))
      (when from-nudge
        (kill-local-variable 'fanshi/gptel-loop--from-nudge))
      (pcase-let* ((`(,trip ,sim)
                    (catch 'fanshi/loop-check-done
                      (condition-case err
                          (when (and fanshi/gptel-loop-guard-enabled
                                     (not (prog1 fanshi/gptel-loop--aborted
                                            (setq-local fanshi/gptel-loop--aborted nil))))
                            (let* ((response (fanshi/gptel-loop--response-text beg end))
                                   (words (fanshi/gptel-loop--normalize-words response)))
                              (when (>= (length words) 40)
                                (let ((sim (cond
                                            ((or (null fanshi/gptel-loop--last-response)
                                                 from-nudge)
                                             nil)
                                            (t (fanshi/gptel-loop--jaccard
                                                fanshi/gptel-loop--last-response
                                                words)))))
                                  (setq fanshi/gptel-loop--last-response words)
                                  (let ((trip (and sim
                                                   (>= sim
                                                       fanshi/gptel-loop-similarity-threshold))))
                                    (throw 'fanshi/loop-check-done
                                           (list trip sim))))))
                            (error
                             (message "gptel-loop-guard: post-response check failed: %s"
                                      (error-message-string err))))))
                    (trip
                     (cond
                      ((< fanshi/gptel-loop--nudge-count fanshi/gptel-loop-nudge-max)
                       (cl-incf fanshi/gptel-loop--nudge-count)
                       (message "gptel-loop-guard: response %.0f%% similar to previous, nudging (%d/%d)."
                                (* 100 sim) fanshi/gptel-loop--nudge-count fanshi/gptel-loop-nudge-max)
                       (fanshi/gptel-loop--nudge))
                      (t
                       (message "gptel-loop-guard: repeated near-identical responses (sim %.0f%%), giving up."
                                (* 100 sim))
                       ;; Drop the fingerprint so the loop is not re-reported.
                       (setq-local fanshi/gptel-loop--last-response nil)
                       (fanshi/notify))))
                    ;; A clean turn refills the budget.
                    ((and (guard trip (null trip)) sim)
                     (setq-local fanshi/gptel-loop--nudge-count 0)))))))

  ;; Position 10: run before the auto-continue hook (at 100).
  (add-hook 'gptel-post-response-functions #'fanshi/gptel-loop--post-response-check 10)

  ;; Reset turn-level state on every send. The nudge counter is not reset here.
  (defun fanshi/gptel-send--reset-loop-state-a (&optional arg)
    "Reset the loop guard's turn-level state for a fresh send."
    (unless arg
      (let ((trip-pending (let ((p fanshi/gptel-loop--pending-abort))
                            (and (processp p) (process-live-p p)))))
        (setq-local fanshi/gptel-loop--last-response nil
                    fanshi/gptel-loop--state nil
                    fanshi/gptel-loop--pending-abort nil)
        (unless trip-pending
          (setq-local fanshi/gptel-loop--aborted nil))
        (fanshi/gptel-loop--tool-state-reset))))

  (advice-add 'gptel-send :after #'fanshi/gptel-send--reset-loop-state-a))
#+end_src
** Inject TOOLS and TOOL_USAGE into Agent specification dynamically
*** fanshi/gptel-agent--tool-system
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel-agent
  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent--tool-system (agent-tools)
    "Detailed usage instructions for AGENT-TOOLS, in Markdown."
    (let ((blocks))
      (dolist (tool-spec agent-tools)
        (let ((tools (gptel-get-tool tool-spec)))
          ;; A spec may name one tool or a list.
          (unless (listp tools)
            (setq tools (list tools)))
          (let ((blocks-in))
            (dolist (tool tools)
              (when-let* ((name (gptel-tool-name tool))
                          (desc (gptel-tool-description tool))
                          (args (gptel-tool-args tool)))
                (let ((arg-lines
                       (mapconcat
                        (lambda (arg-plist)
                          (let* ((arg-name (plist-get arg-plist :name))
                                 (arg-type (or (plist-get arg-plist :type) "string"))
                                 (arg-desc (plist-get arg-plist :description))
                                 (optional-p (plist-get arg-plist :optional))
                                 (enum (plist-get arg-plist :enum)))
                            (format "- `%s`%s (**%s**): %s%s"
                                    arg-name
                                    (if optional-p " [optional]" "")
                                    arg-type
                                    (if enum (format "Allowed: %s" (string-join enum ", ")) "")
                                    arg-desc)))
                        args "\n")))
                  (push (format "## Tool: %s\n\n%s\n\n%s"
                                name
                                (string-trim desc)
                                (unless (string-empty-p arg-lines)
                                  (concat "**Arguments:**\n" arg-lines)))
                        blocks-in))))
            (setq blocks (nconc blocks blocks-in)))))
      (apply #'concat (nreverse blocks)))))
#+end_src
** fanshi/gptel-make-openai
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(defun fanshi/gptel-make-openai (backend-name host &optional pass-entry protocol set-as-backend)
  "Register an OpenAI-compatible backend named BACKEND-NAME on HOST.
Models are discovered via /v1/models. PASS-ENTRY is a `+pass-get-secret' entry name,
or a key used directly. PROTOCOL defaults to \"http\". With SET-BACKEND, make the new
backend the global `gptel-backend'."
  (let* ((url-request-method "GET")
         (url-request-data nil)
         (pass-key (when (and pass-entry (stringp pass-entry)) (+pass-get-secret pass-entry)))
         (url-request-extra-headers (if pass-key
                                        `(("Authorization" . ,(concat "Bearer " pass-key))
                                          ,@url-request-extra-headers)
                                      url-request-extra-headers))
         (protocol (or protocol "http")))
    (url-retrieve (concat protocol "://" host "/v1/models")
                  (lambda (status)
                    (if (eq (car status) :error)
                        (user-error (format "%s" (cdr status)))
                      (goto-char url-http-end-of-headers)
                      (let* ((json-object-type 'alist)
                             (json-array-type 'list)
                             (backend (gptel-make-openai backend-name
                                        :host host
                                        :protocol protocol
                                        :key (or pass-key (unless (stringp pass-entry) pass-entry))
                                        :stream t
                                        :models (cl-loop
                                                 for model in (alist-get 'data (json-read))
                                                 for id = (alist-get 'id model)
                                                 unless (string-match "dflash" id)
                                                 unless (string-match "Kokoro" id)
                                                 unless (string-match "VoxCPM2" id)
                                                 unless (string-match "fish-audio" id)
                                                 unless (string-match "Wan2.2" id)
                                                 collect (intern id)))))
                        (when set-as-backend (setopt gptel-backend backend))))))))
#+end_src

*** Project instructions from AGENTS.org
Per-project instruction snippets: a project's ~AGENTS.org~ has one top-level heading
per preset name.

*Caveat:* applying a preset stores the *resolved* message in ~gptel--system-message~,
so saving a preset from the transient (~C-s~) or editing the live system message (~s~)
bakes the current project's instructions into a plain string and loses the lookup.
Adding a directive is fine — gptel merges it with the freshly resolved message.

The keys are preset names; each value is a plist of ~'TOOL \\=' (ARG-KEYWORD \\='
FILE-RULES)~, where FILE-RULES is a bare ~FILE-REGEXP~ (generic block) or a
~(FILE-REGEXP . BAN-REASON)~ pair. Tool names match case-insensitively; the regexp
with `string-match-p' (unanchored, so ~config\\.el$~ matches at any depth). Naming the
argument keyword (~:file_path~ / ~:path~) tells the hook which argument holds the file.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defvar fanshi/gptel-agents-file-name "AGENTS.org"
    "Per-project agent instructions file under the project root.
Top-level headings are preset names; the text under one is that preset's
instructions.")

  (defvar fanshi-gptel-project-rules nil
    "Per-project mechanically enforced rules for agent tool calls.
Set it in a project's ~.dir-locals.el~. An alist of ~(PRESET . RULES)~, where RULES
is a plist of ~TOOL-SYMBOL \\=' (ARG-KEYWORD \\=' FILE-RULES)~ entries; FILE-RULES is
a bare ~FILE-REGEXP~ or a ~FILE-REGEXP . BAN-REASON~ pair. A tool call is blocked when
the applied presets include PRESET, the tool name matches TOOL-SYMBOL
case-insensitively, and the ARG-KEYWORD argument's value matches FILE-REGEXP.
Enforced by `fanshi/gptel--enforce-project-rules', which reads this out of the
project root's ~.dir-locals.el~ directly rather than relying on Emacs having applied
it. The ~safe-local-variable~ property below only governs the ~hack-local-variables~
prompt.")

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-agents-rules nil
    "This session's enforced project rules: a plist of ~TOOL-SYMBOL \\='
(ARG-KEYWORD \\=' FILE-RULES)~ entries, resolved from
`fanshi-gptel-project-rules' by `fanshi/gptel--enforce-project-rules'.")

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-agents-rules-resolved nil
    "Non-nil once this session's project rules have been looked up.
Cache flag, set whether or not rules were found, so a preset with no rules does not
re-read the file per call.")

  (put 'fanshi-gptel-project-rules 'safe-local-variable
       ;; Structural check only; the nested grammar is validated at use sites.
       (lambda (x)
         (and (consp x)
              (not (sequencep x))
              (seq-every-p
               (lambda (e)
                 (and (consp e)
                      (symbolp (car e))
                      (listp (cdr e))
                      (= 0 (mod (length (cdr e)) 2))))
               x))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-agents-section-body (file preset)
    "PRESET's section body in org FILE, or nil when absent/empty."
    (when-let* ((file (expand-file-name file))
                ((file-exists-p file)))
      (with-temp-buffer
        (delay-mode-hooks (org-mode))
        (insert-file-contents file)
        (goto-char (point-min))
        (let ((heading (concat "^\\* +" (regexp-quote (symbol-name preset))
                               "[ \t]*$"))
              (search-invisible nil))
          (when (re-search-forward heading nil t)
            (org-back-to-heading)
            (forward-line 1)
            (let ((start (point)))
              (org-end-of-subtree)
              (string-trim (buffer-substring-no-properties start (point)))))))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-agents-insert-project-instructions (preset)
    "Append PRESET's project instructions to the current buffer.
Under a ~# Project Instructions~ heading; no matching section leaves the buffer
untouched."
    (when-let* ((root (or (ignore-errors (projectile-project-root))
                          (fanshi/jj-workspace-root)))
                (file (expand-file-name fanshi/gptel-agents-file-name root))
                ((file-exists-p file))
                (result (fanshi/gptel-agents-section-body file preset))
                ((not (string-empty-p result))))
      (goto-char (point-max))
      (insert "\n\n# Project Instructions\n\n" result)))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--project-rules-for-presets (presets)
    "The rules PRESETS activate, resolved from the current buffer.
Reads ~fanshi-gptel-project-rules~ out of the project root's ~.dir-locals.el~
directly, so the lookup does not depend on local variables having been applied."
    (let ((root (or (ignore-errors (projectile-project-root))
                    (fanshi/jj-workspace-root))))
      (when-let* ((root root)
                  (file (expand-file-name ".dir-locals.el" root))
                  ((file-exists-p file))
                  (alist (condition-case err
                             (let ((contents (with-temp-buffer
                                               (insert-file-contents file)
                                               (buffer-string))))
                               (cdr (assq 'fanshi-gptel-project-rules
                                          (cdr (assq nil (read contents))))))
                           (error (message "Invalid %s: %s" file err)
                                  nil)))))
      (when (consp alist)
        (let (acc)
          (dolist (preset presets)
            (when-let ((rules (cdr (assq preset alist))))
              (setq acc (append acc rules))))
          acc))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--project-rule-violation (rules tool-name args)
    "Ban reason if a call of TOOL-NAME/ARGS violates a rule in RULES.
TOOL-NAME matches each rule's tool symbol case-insensitively."
    (cl-loop for (tool-key . arg-map) on rules by #'cddr
             when (and (symbolp tool-key)
                       (string-equal (downcase (symbol-name tool-key))
                                     (downcase tool-name)))
             do (cl-loop for (arg-key . file-rules) on arg-map by #'cddr
                         for file = (plist-get args arg-key)
                         when (stringp file)
                         do (dolist (entry (if (consp (car-safe file-rules))
                                               file-rules
                                             (list file-rules)))
                              (let ((regexp (if (consp entry) (car entry) entry))
                                    (reason (if (consp entry) (cdr entry) nil)))
                                (when (and (stringp regexp)
                                           (string-match-p regexp file))
                                  (return-from fanshi/gptel--project-rule-violation
                                               (or reason
                                                   (format "%s on %s is not allowed" tool-name file))))))))
    nil)

  (defun fanshi/gptel--enforce-project-rules (tool-call)
    "`gptel-pre-tool-call-functions' hook: block TOOL-CALL on a project rule.
Resolves the project's rules once per session into `fanshi/gptel-agents-rules' and
matches the call against them, `(:block REASON)' when banned.

Calls with no applied presets are not enforced, and only the tool NAME is matched."
    (let* ((buffer (plist-get tool-call :buffer))
           (name (plist-get tool-call :name))
           (args (plist-get tool-call :args)))
      (when (buffer-live-p buffer)
        (let ((presets (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/last-gptel-presets buffer)))
          (unless (or (null presets)
                      (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/gptel-agents-rules-resolved buffer))
            (let ((rules (fanshi/gptel--project-rules-for-presets presets)))
              (with-current-buffer buffer
                (setq fanshi/gptel-agents-rules rules)
                (setq fanshi/gptel-agents-rules-resolved t)))))
        (when-let* ((rules (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/gptel-agents-rules buffer))
                    (reason (and name
                                 (fanshi/gptel--project-rule-violation
                                  rules name args))))
          `(:block ,(format "Blocked by project rule: %s" reason))))))

  ;; Depth 90: after the arg validator (80), so a ban overrides the `(:confirm nil)'
  ;; it returns for a tool in `fanshi/safe-tool'. Explicit because the validator's
  ;; `add-hook' is in another `after! gptel' block.
  (add-hook 'gptel-pre-tool-call-functions
            #'fanshi/gptel--enforce-project-rules 90))
#+end_src
