*** Coding
**** Project-specific coding tools
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defvar fanshi/project-tools-map
  '((doom . ("elisp")))
  "Alist of projectile project types to extra tool names for coding.")

(defun fanshi/append-coding-project-tools (tools dir)
  "Append project(in DIR)-specific tools to TOOLS."
  (when-let* ((extra-tools (alist-get
                            (or
                             (buffer-local-value 'projectile-project-type (dired-noselect dir))
                             (projectile-project-type dir))
                            fanshi/project-tools-map nil nil #'equal)))
    (append tools extra-tools)))
#+end_src
**** Post-response code checkers
On a failed check the fix is *not* sent as a follow-up in the current conversation —
that would resend the whole (often huge) context for what is usually a local syntax
error. Instead a fresh `gptel-agent' buffer is spawned in the same project, prompted
with only:
- the failing ~cmd~, the file the error was found in, and the source file to fix,
  with the error wrapped in an ~#+begin_example~ block
- the current jj diff (in a jj repo), via `fanshi/call-jj'
- the fixer inherits the spawning buffer's retry count, so a chain of failing fixers
  stays bounded

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel--edited-files nil
    "Files edited during the current response cycle.
A failing one stays, so it is re-checked after the next response.")

  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-code-check-auto-fix-max-retries 3
    "Auto-fix retries before giving up and just showing the error. 0 disables."
    :type 'integer
    :group 'gptel)

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel--code-check-fix-count 0
    "Auto-fix retries in the current turn.")

  (defun fanshi/gptel--track-edited-files-a (fn &rest args)
    "Record a write tool's file (its first arg) as edited."
    (let ((result (apply fn args)))
      (when-let* ((file-path (car args)))
        (add-to-list 'fanshi/gptel--edited-files file-path))
      result))

  (advice-add #'gptel-agent--write-file :around #'fanshi/gptel--track-edited-files-a)
  (advice-add #'gptel-agent--edit-files :around #'fanshi/gptel--track-edited-files-a)

  (defun fanshi/gptel--check-elisp-syntax (file)
    "Syntax-check FILE with `scan-sexps'. Error string on a scan error."
    (with-temp-buffer
      (insert-file-contents file)
      (delay-mode-hooks (emacs-lisp-mode))
      (let ((parse-sexp-ignore-comments t))
        (condition-case err
            (while-let ((end (scan-sexps (point) 1)))
              (goto-char end))
          (scan-error
           (let ((pos (nth 3 err)))
             (format "%s at line %d, column %d."
                     (nth 1 err)
                     (save-excursion (goto-char pos) (line-number-at-pos))
                     (save-excursion (goto-char pos) (current-column)))))))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--code-check-auto-fix (failure)
    "Auto-fix a code check FAILURE in a fresh gptel-agent buffer.
FAILURE is the plist from `fanshi/gptel--post-response-check/doom' with keys :cmd,
:file (where the error was found, e.g. the tangled .el), :source (the Org file to
fix) and :error.

Rather than re-sending the whole conversation, spawn a `gptel-agent' buffer in the
same project, primed with the coding preset, the failure details, and — in a jj repo
— the current `jj diff'."
    (incf fanshi/gptel--code-check-fix-count)
    (message "Gptel code check failed (attempt %d/%d), spawning fixer agent..."
             fanshi/gptel--code-check-fix-count
             fanshi/gptel-code-check-auto-fix-max-retries)
    (let* ((err (plist-get failure :error))
           (err-block (if (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
                          (format "#+begin_example\n%s\n#+end_example" err)
                        (format "```\n%s\n```" err)))
           (formated-err (format "In %s:\n%s" (plist-get failure :file) err-block))
           (prompt
            (format
             "Command(%s) failed.\n\n%s\n\nPlease fix the error in the source file(%s) then report the fix."
             (plist-get failure :cmd)
             formated-err
             (plist-get failure :source))))
      (let ((buf (fanshi/gptel-agent "")))
        (with-current-buffer buf
          (setq-local fanshi/gptel--code-check-fix-count fanshi/gptel--code-check-fix-count)
          ;; Apply the @coding preset, right after the prompt prefix.
          (goto-char (point-min))
          (let ((prompt-prefix (alist-get gptel-default-mode gptel-prompt-prefix-alist)))
            (re-search-forward (rx (literal prompt-prefix))))
          (insert "@coding ")
          (goto-char (point-max))
          (insert (format "%s\n" prompt))
          (when-let ((jj-diff (ignore-errors
                                (fanshi/call-jj
                                 (or (buffer-local-value 'default-directory buf)
                                     default-directory)
                                 "diff"))))
            (insert (concat
                     "\nFor context, the current jj diff is:\n\n"
                     jj-diff)))
          (gptel-send)))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--check-tangled-file (file)
    "Tangle Org FILE and syntax-check the output.
Nil when clean, else see `fanshi/gptel--post-response-check/doom'."
    (when (and (equal (file-name-extension file) "org")
               (file-exists-p file))
      (let* ((cmd (format "org-babel tangled file %S" file))
             (tangled (org-babel-tangle-file file))
             (bad (cl-loop for f in tangled
                           for err = (fanshi/gptel--check-elisp-syntax f)
                           thereis (and err (cons f err)))))
        (cond
         (bad
          (list :cmd cmd :file (car bad) :source file :error (cdr bad)))
         ((and (null tangled)
               (equal file (expand-file-name "config.org")))
          (list :cmd cmd :file file :source file
                :error "oops: no tangled file output produced from doom config"))))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--post-response-check/doom ()
    "Tangle and syntax-check `config.org' plus each edited \".org\" file.
Nil when all pass, else the first failure's plist. Passing files are dropped from
`fanshi/gptel--edited-files'; failing ones stay for the next check."
    (let ((files (and fanshi/gptel--edited-files
                      (delete-dups
                       (cons (expand-file-name "config.org")
                             (mapcar #'expand-file-name
                                     fanshi/gptel--edited-files)))))
          (checked nil)
          (failures nil))
      (dolist (file files)
        (if-let* ((failure (fanshi/gptel--check-tangled-file file)))
            (push failure failures)
          (push file checked)))
      (setq fanshi/gptel--edited-files
            (cl-remove-if (lambda (f) (member (expand-file-name f) checked))
                          fanshi/gptel--edited-files))
      (unless failures
        (setq-local fanshi/gptel--code-check-fix-count 0))
      (car (last failures)))))

***** fanshi/gptel--hf-to-capabilites-and-mimes
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle no
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel--hf-to-capabilites-and-mimes (hf)
    "(CAPABILITIES . MIME-TYPES) inferred from HuggingFace tag HF."
    (pcase hf
      ('nil (error "impossible: nil hf"))
      ("" (error "impossible: empty string hf"))
      ;; Multimodal
      ((or (pred (string-match-p "Qwen3\\.6"))
           (pred (string-match-p "gemma-4")))
       (cons '(media tool-use json url) '("image/jpeg" "image/png" "image/gif" "image/webp")))
      ;; Coding
      ((pred (string-match-p "coder")) (cons '(tool-use json) '()))
      (_ (cons (list) (list))))))
#+end_src
***** fanshi/gptel--load-models-from-ini
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle no
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel--load-models-from-ini (backend)
    "Parse the INI file for BACKEND ('Llama or 'Mlx) into gptel model specs.
'Llama: section name is the model symbol, :hf tag infers capabilities.
'Mlx: the :hf tag value is the model symbol."
    (with-temp-buffer
      (insert-file-contents-literally (expand-file-name (concat (symbol-name backend) "-model.ini") doom-user-dir))
      (goto-char (point-min))
      (let ((models '()))
        (while-let ((_ (not (eobp)))
                    (line (buffer-substring-no-properties (line-beginning-position) (line-end-position))))
          (if (string-match "^\\[\\([^]]+\\)\\]$" line)
              (let ((section (intern (match-string 1 line))) (props))
                (forward-line 1)
                (while-let ((_ (not (eobp)))
                            (line (buffer-substring-no-properties (line-beginning-position) (line-end-position)))
                            (_ (not (string-match "^\\[\\([^]]+\\)\\]$" line))))
                  (when (string-match "^\\([a-z-]+\\)\\s-*=\\s-*\\(.*\\)$" line)
                    (let ((key (pcase (match-string 1 line)
                                 ("temp" :temperature)
                                 ("ctx-size" :context-window)
                                 (str (intern (concat ":" (string-replace "-" "_" str))))))
                          (val (pcase (match-string 2 line)
                                 ((and (pred (string-match-p "^[0-9.]+$")) str) (string-to-number str))
                                 (str str))))
                      (if (memq key '(:temperature :top_p :top_k :min_p :presence_penalty))
                          (let ((request-params (plist-put (plist-get props :request-params) key val)))
                            (setf (plist-get props :request-params) request-params))
                        (setq props (plist-put props key val)))))
                  (forward-line 1))
                (let (;; For Mlx, the :hf tag value is the model symbol.
                      (model-symbol  (or (and (eq backend 'Mlx) (intern (plist-get props :hf)))
                                         section))
                      (caps-mime (fanshi/gptel--hf-to-capabilites-and-mimes (plist-get props :hf))))
                  (push (cons model-symbol (-> props (plist-put :input-cost 0)
                                               (plist-put :output-cost 0)
                                               (plist-put :capabilities (car caps-mime))
                                               (plist-put :mime-types (cdr caps-mime))))
                        models)))
            (forward-line 1)))
        (nreverse models)))))

** Prompt rewrite for gptel-mode
*** fanshi/gptel--rewrite-directive-default
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel--rewrite-directive-default ()
    "Default rewrite directive: prompt-engineer in gptel buffers, else per-language."
    (cond
     (gptel-mode
      (concat "You are a prompt engineer.  "
              "Your take a user's draft prompt and *IMMEDIATELY* rewrite it into a superior prompt. \n"
              "- Generate ONLY the prompt as output"
              "- Preserve the user's original intent"
              "- Do not ask for further clarification, and make "
              "any assumptions you need to follow instructions."))
     ((-some-->
          (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
        (eq 'src-block (org-element-type (org-element-context)))
        (org-element-property :language (org-element-at-point))
        (format (concat "You are a %s programmer.  "
                        "Follow my instructions and refactor %s code I provide.\n"
                        "- Generate ONLY %s code as output, without "
                        "any explanation or markdown code fences.\n"
                        "- Generate code in full, do not abbreviate or omit code.\n"
                        "- Do not produce intermediate text or report on your progress.\n"
                        "- Do not ask for further clarification, and make "
                        "any assumptions you need to follow instructions.")
                it it it)))))
  (advice-add 'gptel--rewrite-directive-default :before-until 'fanshi/gptel--rewrite-directive-default))

** Allow user to reject tool call with a reason
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel--reject-tool-calls-a (&optional tool-calls _ov)
    "Ask for a reason when rejecting TOOL-CALLS, and pass it to the model."
    (cl-loop for (_tool-spec _arg-plist process-tool-result) in tool-calls
             do (if (not (called-interactively-p 'any)) ;; timer fired, no user input
                    (funcall process-tool-result "User probably AFK, please try finding another way to achieve whatever the tool call was intended")
                  (pcase (read-from-minibuffer "Reason to reject the tool call: ")
                    ((and (pred (not string-empty-p)) reason) (funcall process-tool-result (concat "User rejected tool call: " reason)))
                    (_)))))
  (advice-add 'gptel--reject-tool-calls :after #'fanshi/gptel--reject-tool-calls-a))
#+end_src
** Save gptel-agent chat to .gptel-agent under main project root with name from task description
The chat is saved as =.gptel-agent/<buffer-name>-<timestamp>.org= in the *main*
project root. Resolving that root has two traps:

- A failed =jj workspace root= writes stderr into the chat buffer and returns the
  empty string, so =ignore-errors= catches nothing. Hence the requirement of a
  non-blank absolute path before anything is created.
- From inside a sub-workspace, =jj workspace root= returns the sub-workspace's own
  root, so jj agent buffers record the main root in `fanshi/gptel-jj-origin-root'
  at creation time.

*** fanshi/gptel-agent--auto-assign-filename
#+begin_src elisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent--auto-assign-filename ()
    "Give a gptel-agent buffer a file under .gptel-agent/ in the main project root.
For jj sessions the root comes from `fanshi/gptel-jj-origin-root', otherwise
`default-directory'."
    (unless buffer-file-name
      (when (string-prefix-p "*gptel-agent:" (buffer-name))
        (if-let* ((base-dir (let ((r (or fanshi/gptel-jj-origin-root
                                         (fanshi/jj-workspace-root))))
                              (and (not (string-blank-p r))
                                   (file-name-absolute-p r)
                                   r))))
            (if-let* ((dir (expand-file-name ".gptel-agent/" base-dir))
                      (timestamp (format-time-string "%Y%m%d-%H%M%S"))
                      (filename (concat
                                 (->> (buffer-name)
                                      (s-chop-suffix "*")
                                      (s-chop-prefix "*gptel-agent:")
                                      (replace-regexp-in-string "[,;'\"`|]" ""))
                                 "-" timestamp ".org"))
                      ;; A renamed buffer gets a fresh timestamp, not two.
                      (filename (if (string-match-p
                                     (rx (seq "-" (= 8 digit) "-" (= 6 digit) ".org" eol))
                                     filename)
                                    (replace-regexp-in-string
                                     (rx (seq "-" (= 8 digit) "-" (= 6 digit) ".org" eol))
                                     (concat "-" timestamp ".org")
                                     filename)
                                  filename)))
                (progn
                  (make-directory dir t)
                  (setq buffer-file-name
                        (expand-file-name filename dir))
                  (rename-buffer (file-name-nondirectory buffer-file-name) t))
              (message "failed to assign file name to the gptel-buffer"))
          (message "failed to resolve the main project root"))))))

    (problem-solve . ,(rx "Executive Summary" (+ anychar) "Key Findings" (+ anychar) "Sources" (+ anychar) "Conclusion")))

** Fix gptel--transform-apply-preset to handle the case where we resume from the middle of a agent response.
Three fixes over the original:
- A single `text-property-search-backward' is not enough when resuming from the
  middle of an agent response, so keep searching backward past headings to find the
  actual user prompt.
- Presets set `gptel-tools' and `gptel-model' in the temp *prompt buffer*, but
  downstream hooks read the *original* buffer, so propagate them back via
  `fanshi/last-presets-gptel-tools' and `fanshi/last-presets-gptel-model'.
- Strip preset markers left in *previous* prompts, so stale directives do not
  pollute the prompt cache.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defvar-local fanshi/last-presets-gptel-tools nil
    "Tools equipped by the last preset applied here.
Fallback for the available-tool check, ahead of `gptel-tools'.")

  (defvar-local fanshi/last-gptel-presets nil
    "Preset names applied by the last prompt, e.g. (coding editor).")

  (defvar-local fanshi/last-presets-gptel-model nil
    "Model selected by the last preset applied here.
Fallback for the active-model check, ahead of `gptel-model'.")

  (defun fanshi/process-preset-til-end (&optional end only-remove)
    "Process @preset markers from point to END (default point-max).
Deletes each known @NAME marker, and applies it buffer-locally unless ONLY-REMOVE.
Markers must be preceded by whitespace or start-of-buffer. Errors if more than one
preset in the range has a report pattern.

Value is (REPORT-PATTERN . PRESETS-APPLIED)."
    (let ((presets-applied))
      (while (re-search-forward (rx "@" (group (+ (not (any blank))))) end t)
        (when (or (= (match-beginning 0) (point-min))
                  (memq (char-syntax (char-before (match-beginning 0))) '(32 62)))
          (when-let* ((name (match-string-no-properties 1))
                      (preset (or (gptel-get-preset (intern-soft name))
                                  (gptel-get-preset name))))
            (delete-region (match-beginning 0) (match-end 0))
            (push (intern-soft name) presets-applied)
            (unless only-remove
              (gptel--apply-preset preset
                                   (lambda (sym val)
                                     (set (make-local-variable sym) val)))))))
      (when (and gptel-mode fanshi/gptel--auto-continued)
        (setq-local gptel--request-params
                    (-> gptel--request-params
                        (plist-put :enable_thinking :json-false)
                        (plist-put :chat_template_kwargs (:enable_thinking :json-false :preserve_thinking t)))))
      (let ((matching-presets (cl-remove-if-not (lambda (p)
                                                  (alist-get p fanshi/gptel-report-patterns nil nil #'equal))
                                                presets-applied)))
        (when (> (length matching-presets) 1)
          (user-error "Aborted: multiple presets with report patterns: %s" matching-presets))
        (cons
         (and matching-presets (alist-get (car matching-presets) fanshi/gptel-report-patterns nil nil #'equal))
         (nreverse presets-applied)))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--transform-apply-preset-a (fsm)
    "Apply the @preset markers in the last prompt, then propagate them to FSM's buffer.

Presets set `gptel-tools' and `gptel-model' in the *prompt* buffer, but downstream
hooks read the *original* buffer, so the values are copied back. Markers in earlier
turns are stripped too, so stale directives do not reach the prompt cache."
    (when gptel--known-presets
      (let ((preset-applied
             (if-let ((prompt-prefix (alist-get gptel-default-mode gptel-prompt-prefix-alist)))
                 (let ((last-prompt-preset-applied))
                   (goto-char (point-max))
                   (while-let ((_ (not (bobp)))
                               (last-matched-point (point))
                               (match (text-property-search-backward 'gptel nil t)))
                     (save-excursion
                       (when (re-search-forward
                              (rx (and
                                   point
                                   (* (any blank ?\n))
                                   (literal prompt-prefix)))
                              (prop-match-end match) t 1)
                         (pcase-let ((`(,preset-report-pattern . ,current-prompt-presets)
                                      (fanshi/process-preset-til-end
                                       (prop-match-end match)
                                       last-prompt-preset-applied)))
                           (when current-prompt-presets
                             (or last-prompt-preset-applied (setq last-prompt-preset-applied current-prompt-presets)))
                           (when preset-report-pattern
                             (while-let ((_ (< (point) last-matched-point))
                                         (response-match (text-property-search-forward 'gptel 'response t)))
                               (when (re-search-forward preset-report-pattern (prop-match-end response-match) t 1)
                                 (delete-region (+ (prop-match-end match) 1) (- (match-beginning 0) 1)))))))))
                   last-prompt-preset-applied)
               (goto-char (point-min))
               (cdr (fanshi/process-preset-til-end)))))
        ;; Propagate the preset's tools/model/preset list to the original buffer.
        (if-let* ((original-buffer (plist-get (gptel-fsm-info fsm) :buffer))
                  (_ (buffer-live-p original-buffer)))
            (let ((tools gptel-tools)
                  (model gptel-model))
              (with-current-buffer original-buffer
                (when preset-applied
                  (set (make-local-variable 'fanshi/last-gptel-presets) preset-applied))
                (when tools
                  (set (make-local-variable 'fanshi/last-presets-gptel-tools) tools))
                (when model
                  (set (make-local-variable 'fanshi/last-presets-gptel-model) model))))
          (display-warning '(gptel transform)
                           (format "fanshi/gptel--transform-apply-preset-a: propagate preset to original buffer %s failed" original-buffer))))))
  (advice-add 'gptel--transform-apply-preset :override  #'fanshi/gptel--transform-apply-preset-a))

(defvar fanshi/gptel-last-agent-buffer nil
  "Last visited gptel-agent buffer.")

(defvar-local fanshi/gptel-context-files nil
  "Files allowed through the sandbox outside the project root, set by
`gptel-context-add'.  Honored by
`fanshi/restrict-to-projectile-files-plus-context-a' and
`fanshi/nono-context-allow-read-file-args-list'.")

(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel--track-agent-buffer (&optional _window)
    "Record the current buffer as the last visited agent buffer."
    (setq fanshi/gptel-last-agent-buffer (current-buffer)))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--setup-track-agent-buffer (&optional _rest)
    "Track the agent buffer on window buffer changes, not just the mode hook."
    (fanshi/gptel--track-agent-buffer)
    (add-hook 'window-buffer-change-functions #'fanshi/gptel--track-agent-buffer nil t))

  (add-hook! 'gptel-mode-hook :append #'fanshi/gptel--setup-track-agent-buffer)

  (defun fanshi/gptel-context-add-a (orig-fun &rest args)
    "Add context as a link in the last visited agent buffer, and allow its file."
    (unless (buffer-live-p fanshi/gptel-last-agent-buffer)
      (user-error "%s is not alive to add context link to." fanshi/gptel-last-agent-buffer))
    (call-interactively #'org-store-link)
    (let ((fname buffer-file-name))
      (with-current-buffer fanshi/gptel-last-agent-buffer
        (when fname (add-to-list 'fanshi/gptel-context-files fname))
        (goto-char (point-max))
        (org-insert-link)
        (current-buffer))))

  (advice-add 'gptel-context-add :override #'fanshi/gptel-context-add-a))

(defvar-local fanshi/prevent-duplicate-gptel-send t)
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel-send--prevent-duplicate-a (&optional arg)
    "Block `gptel-send' while a request is already in progress in this buffer."
    (let ((buffer (current-buffer)))
      (when (and fanshi/prevent-duplicate-gptel-send (not gptel-org-branching-context)
                 (cl-find-if (lambda (entry)
                               (eq (plist-get (gptel-fsm-info (cadr entry)) :buffer)
                                   buffer))
                             gptel--request-alist))
        (message "A gptel request is already in progress in %s. Use `gptel-abort' to cancel it first, or wait for it to complete." buffer)
        t)))
  (advice-add 'gptel-send :before-until #'fanshi/gptel-send--prevent-duplicate-a))

** Multi parallel requests in the buffer
*** fanshi/gptel-multisend
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defun fanshi/gptel-multisend (&optional ARG)
  "Send ARG repetitions of the current org subtree using gptel.

ARG is the number of *additional* sends (default 1). Each repetition is cloned and
sent in turn, advancing to the next sibling heading after each send. Enables
`gptel-org-branching-context' so cloned subtrees keep their own history."
  (interactive "P")
  (let ((gptel-org-branching-context t)
        ;; NOTE: gptel--stream-convert-markdown->org handles multiple requests in
        ;; one buffer poorly.
        (gptel-org-convert-response nil))
    (org-clone-subtree-with-time-shift ARG)
    (cl-loop repeat (1+ (if (numberp ARG) ARG 1))
             do (progn
                  (org-end-of-subtree)
                  (gptel-send)
                  (org-next-visible-heading 1)))))
#+end_src
*** gptel-abort aborts the request at point instead of the first one it found
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel-abort-at-point-a (buf)
    "Abort the gptel request at point in BUF, or nil."
    (when-let*
        (((eq buf (current-buffer)))
         (pt (point))
         (req-at-point (cl-find-if (lambda (req)
                                     (let* ((fsm-info (gptel-fsm-info (cadr req)))
                                            (pos (plist-get fsm-info :position)))
                                       (and
                                        (eq buf (plist-get fsm-info :buffer))
                                        (when-let ((bounds (save-excursion
                                                             (goto-char pos)
                                                             (gptel--get-response-bounds))))
                                          (and (< (car bounds) pt) (> (cdr bounds) pt))))))
                                   gptel--request-alist))
         (proc (car req-at-point))
         (fsm (cadr req-at-point))
         (fsm-info (gptel-fsm-info fsm))
         (abort-fn (caddr req-at-point))
         (cb (plist-get fsm-info :callback))
         ((functionp cb)))
      ;; Mark before the callback: on a `:before-until' miss the original
      ;; `gptel-abort' runs, and marking there would arm a stale flag.
      (fanshi/gptel-mark-turn-aborted buf)
      (with-demoted-errors "Callback error: %S" (funcall cb 'abort fsm-info))
      (funcall abort-fn)
      (setf (alist-get proc gptel--request-alist nil 'remove) nil)
      (gptel--fsm-transition fsm 'ABRT)
      (message "Stopped gptel request at point in buffer %S"
               (buffer-name buf))
      t))

  (advice-add 'gptel-abort :before-until #'fanshi/gptel-abort-at-point-a))
#+end_src
** fanshi/speak
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defun fanshi/speak (&optional text model voice)
  "Speak TEXT via the oMLX TTS endpoint.

Speaks the region if the mark is active, else prompts. MODEL (default \"qwen3-tts\")
and VOICE override the defaults."
  (interactive)
  (let ((text (or text
                  (and (use-region-p) (buffer-substring-no-properties (region-beginning) (region-end)))
                  (read-string "Speak: "))))
    (unless (stringp text)
      (user-error "No text to speak"))
    (let* ((model (or model "Qwen3-TTS-12Hz-1.7B-CustomVoice"))
           (pass-key (when-let ((key (+pass-get-secret "omlx/1")))
                       key))
           (url-request-method "POST")
           (url-request-extra-headers
            `(("Content-Type" . "application/json")
              ,@(when pass-key
                  `(("Authorization" . ,(concat "Bearer " pass-key))))
              ,@url-request-extra-headers))
           (url-request-data (json-encode `(("model" . ,model)
                                            ("input" . ,(string-trim text))
                                            ;; NOTE: Qwen3-TTS's voice 'serena', 'vivian', 'uncle_fu', 'ryan', 'aiden', 'ono_anna', 'sohee', 'eric', 'dylan'
                                            ("voice" . ,(or voice "ono_anna")))))
           (tmp-file (make-temp-file "omlx-tts-" nil ".wav")))
      (url-retrieve (concat "http://127.0.0.1:8000/v1/audio/speech")
                    (lambda (status)
                      (if (eq (car status) :error)
                          (message "fanshi/speak: oMLX tts request failed")
                        (goto-char url-http-end-of-headers)
                        (while (looking-at "\r?\n")
                          (delete-char (if (looking-at "\r\n") 2 1)))
                        (write-region (point) (point-max) tmp-file nil 'quiet)
                        (start-process "afplay" nil "afplay" tmp-file)
                        (message "fanshi/speak: playing %s" tmp-file)))))))
#+end_src
** fanshi/speak-stream
#+begin_src elisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(defun fanshi/speak-stream (&optional text model voice)
  "Stream TEXT to the oMLX TTS endpoint and play it via ffplay.

Speaks the region if the mark is active, else prompts. MODEL and VOICE override the
defaults."
  (interactive)
  (let ((text (or text
                  (and (use-region-p) (buffer-substring-no-properties (region-beginning) (region-end)))
                  (read-string "Speak: "))))
    (unless (stringp text)
      (user-error "No text to speak"))
    (let* ((model (or model "Qwen3-TTS-12Hz-1.7B-Base-bf16"))
           (pass-key (when-let ((key (+pass-get-secret "omlx/2")))
                       key))
           (json-body (json-encode `(("model" . ,model)
                                     ("input" . ,(string-trim text))
                                     ("voice" . ,(or voice "Ono_Anna" "Sohee"))
                                     ("stream" . t))))
           (url "http://127.0.0.1:8000/v1/audio/speech")
           (cmd (concat "curl -s -X POST --no-buffer "
                        "-H 'Content-Type: application/json' "
                        (or (when pass-key
                              (format "-H 'Authorization: Bearer %s' " pass-key))
                            "")
                        (format "-d '%s' %s | ffplay -nodisp -autoexit -fflags nobuffer -probesize 64 -analyzeduration 0 -"
                                json-body url))))
      (start-process "tts-stream" nil "sh" "-c" cmd))))
#+end_src
** Local GPTEL request waiting for main models
To avoid loading multiple main models into restricted RAM, main-model requests never
run in parallel — even for the same model. A request that cannot run immediately is
queued as a TODO task in the AI tasks org file (the file the nightly processor reads)
and left there: the agent picks it up and replays it by priority. The org file is the
only queue, so it survives Emacs restarts for free.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defvar fanshi/gptel-send-mutex (make-mutex "fanshi/gptel-send-mutex"))

(defvar fanshi/main-models (list
                            'Jundot--Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-oQ4e-mtp
                            'mensaprodigy--Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-REAP-384-oQ5e-BF16-MTP-PLE
                            'mlx-community--Qwen3.8-27B-bf16
                            'mlx-community--gemma-4-31b-it-bf16)
  "Models that should not run concurrently.")

(defvar fanshi/gptel--replaying-queued-request nil
  "Bound non-nil around a replayed queued request's `gptel-send'.
Lets such sends through `fanshi/gptel-request-maybe-waiting-main-model-a' without
queueing.")

(after! org-gtd
  (defun fanshi/gptel--find-queued-task-id ()
    "Org-ID of the first TODO [gptel] task in the AI tasks file, or nil.
The org file is the queue, so this guards against queueing a second request while one
is already queued."
    (let ((file (org-gtd--path "org-gtd-ai-tasks")))
      (when (file-exists-p file)
        (with-temp-buffer
          (insert-file-contents file)
          (org-mode)
          (save-excursion
            (goto-char (point-min))
            (when (re-search-forward
                   "^\\* \\(?:#[A-Z0-9]+ +\\)?TODO \\(?:\\[#[^]]+\\] +\\)?\\[gptel\\] "
                   nil t)
              (org-entry-get (point) "ID")))))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--queue-request-as-task (prompt model backend-name project-dir)
    "Queue PROMPT as a TODO task in the AI tasks org file.
Stores the request parameters as org properties (GPTEL_MODEL, GPTEL_BACKEND,
SCOPED_PROJECT) and the prompt in a #+BEGIN_EXAMPLE block, so it survives an Emacs
restart."
    (let ((heading (string-trim prompt)))
      (when (string-prefix-p (gptel-prompt-prefix-string) heading)
        (setq heading (substring heading (length (gptel-prompt-prefix-string)))))
      (fanshi/ai-task--capture
       (concat "[gptel] " (s-truncate 60 (replace-regexp-in-string "[\n\r]" " " heading)))
       (concat "#+BEGIN_EXAMPLE\n" prompt "\n#+END_EXAMPLE")
       (list (cons "PRIORITY" "A")
             (cons "GPTEL_MODEL" (symbol-name model))
             (cons "GPTEL_BACKEND" backend-name)
             (when project-dir (cons "SCOPED_PROJECT" project-dir)))))))

(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel--cleanup-dead-requests ()
    "Drop `gptel--request-alist' entries whose :buffer is dead.
Number removed."
    (interactive)
    (let ((removed 0))
      (cl-loop for entry in (copy-sequence gptel--request-alist)
               for fsm-info = (gptel-fsm-info (cadr entry))
               for buf = (plist-get fsm-info :buffer)
               when (and buf (not (buffer-live-p buf)))
               do (let ((proc (car entry)))
                    (setf (alist-get proc gptel--request-alist nil 'remove) nil)
                    (cl-incf removed)))
      (when (> removed 0)
        (message "Cleaned up %d dead gptel request(s) from gptel--request-alist" removed))
      removed))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--get-active-main-model ()
    "The main model running in a gptel request, or nil.
Terminal requests (DONE/ERRS/ABRT) do not count: gptel removes them from
`gptel--request-alist' only after `gptel-post-response-functions' runs. Dead buffers
are cleaned up first."
    (fanshi/gptel--cleanup-dead-requests)
    (cl-loop for req in gptel--request-alist
             for info = (gptel-fsm-info (cadr req))
             for state = (gptel-fsm-state (cadr req))
             for model = (plist-get info :model)
             when (and (member model fanshi/main-models)
                       (not (memq state '(DONE ERRS ABRT))))
             return model))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--preset-model-in-prompt ()
    "The model set by @preset markers in the prompt, or nil.
Resolves the presets in a throwaway buffer via `fanshi/process-preset-til-end'."
    (when-let ((prompt-buffer (gptel-org--create-prompt-buffer (point))))
      (with-current-buffer prompt-buffer
        (goto-char (point-min))
        (ignore-errors (fanshi/process-preset-til-end)))
      (buffer-local-value 'gptel-model prompt-buffer)))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-request-maybe-waiting-main-model-a (orig-f &rest args)
    "Keep main model requests strictly sequential. Advice for `gptel-send'.
If a main model is already running — even the same one — the request is queued as a
TODO task in the AI tasks org file instead of sent. The effective model comes from an
@preset in the prompt, else `fanshi/last-presets-gptel-model', else `gptel-model'."
    (let ((effective-model
           (or (fanshi/gptel--preset-model-in-prompt)
               fanshi/last-presets-gptel-model
               gptel-model)))
      (with-mutex fanshi/gptel-send-mutex
        (if fanshi/gptel--replaying-queued-request
            (apply orig-f args)
          (if-let* ((backend (and gptel-backend (not (plist-get gptel-backend :protocol "https"))))
                    (main-model-p (member effective-model fanshi/main-models))
                    (active-model (fanshi/gptel--get-active-main-model)))
              (pcase (completing-read
                      (format "Main model %s is already running. %s is requested. Choice: "
                              active-model effective-model)
                      '("Schedule later" "Use active model" "Cancel")
                      nil t)
                ("Schedule later"
                 (if-let ((queued-id (fanshi/gptel--find-queued-task-id)))
                     (message "Request not sent: task %s is already queued. The prompt stays in this buffer; send it again once the agent has picked it up."
                              queued-id)
                   (let ((task-id
                          (fanshi/gptel--queue-request-as-task
                           (buffer-substring (point-min) (point))
                           effective-model
                           (gptel-backend-name gptel-backend)
                           (and (string-prefix-p "*gptel-agent:" (buffer-name))
                                default-directory))))
                     (message "Request queued as task %s. The AI agent will pick it up from the tasks file."
                              task-id))))
                ("Use active model"
                 (let ((gptel-model active-model))
                   (message "Using currently active model %s" active-model)
                   (apply orig-f args)))
                (_ (error "Request cancelled")))
            ;; No main model running, proceed normally
            (apply orig-f args))))))


  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel--replay-callback nil
    "Callback for a replayed queued request, called with a result string when the
response finishes.")

  (defun fanshi/gptel--replay-done (_start _end)
    "Finish the replayed request in this buffer and call its callback."
    (when fanshi/gptel--replay-callback
      (let ((cb fanshi/gptel--replay-callback))
        (setq-local fanshi/gptel--replay-callback nil)
        (remove-hook 'gptel-post-response-functions #'fanshi/gptel--replay-done t)
        (funcall cb (format "Queued gptel request in buffer '%s' finished (state: %s)."
                            (buffer-name)
                            (symbol-name (or (gptel-fsm-state gptel--fsm-last) 'unknown)))))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--replay-buffer-killed ()
    "Fire the replay callback if this buffer dies before the response finishes."
    (when fanshi/gptel--replay-callback
      (let ((cb fanshi/gptel--replay-callback))
        (setq fanshi/gptel--replay-callback nil)
        (funcall cb (format "Replay buffer '%s' was killed before the response finished; the request was aborted."
                            (buffer-name))))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--replay-queued-request (cb task-id)
    "Replay queued request task TASK-ID; call CB when its response finishes.
Reads the prompt from the task body's #+BEGIN_EXAMPLE block and GPTEL_MODEL /
GPTEL_BACKEND / SCOPED_PROJECT from its properties, creates a fresh gptel (or
gptel-agent) buffer, and sends it. The task is marked DELEGATED as soon as the
request is sent, even if it later fails."
    (let ((task-marker (org-id-find task-id 'marker)))
      (unless task-marker
        (funcall cb (format "Task %s not found" task-id))
        (return-from fanshi/gptel--replay-queued-request))
      (save-excursion
        (goto-char task-marker)
        (let* ((subtree (save-excursion
                          (org-back-to-heading)
                          (let ((end (org-end-of-subtree)))
                            (buffer-substring-no-properties (point) end))))
               (prompt (when (string-match "\n#\\+BEGIN_EXAMPLE\n" subtree)
                         (let ((start (match-end 0))
                               (end (string-match "\n#\\+END_EXAMPLE" subtree start)))
                           (string-trim (substring subtree start (or end (length subtree)))))))
               ;; `org-entry-get' returns \"\" for a missing property, and
               ;; `intern' of that is truthy.
               (model-str (org-entry-get (point) "GPTEL_MODEL"))
               (backend-str (org-entry-get (point) "GPTEL_BACKEND"))
               (model (and model-str (intern model-str)))
               (backend (and backend-str
                             (alist-get backend-str gptel--known-backends nil nil #'equal)))
               (project-dir (org-entry-get (point) "SCOPED_PROJECT"))
               buf)
          (unless (and prompt model backend)
            (funcall cb (format "Task %s is missing the prompt or a valid GPTEL_MODEL/GPTEL_BACKEND" task-id))
            (return-from fanshi/gptel--replay-queued-request))
          (setq buf
                (if project-dir
                    (gptel-agent project-dir 'gptel-agent)
                  (get-buffer-create (generate-new-buffer-name "*gptel-queued*"))))
          (with-current-buffer buf
            (gptel-mode 1)
            (setq-local gptel-model model
                        gptel-backend backend
                        fanshi/gptel--replay-callback cb)
            (add-hook 'gptel-post-response-functions #'fanshi/gptel--replay-done nil t)
            (add-hook 'kill-buffer-hook #'fanshi/gptel--replay-buffer-killed nil t)
            ;; Clear whatever the buffer was created with before inserting the
            ;; stored prompt, which carries its own prefix.
            (delete-region (point-min) (point-max))
            (insert prompt)
            (goto-char (point-max))
            (display-buffer buf)
            (let ((ok
                   (condition-case err
                       (progn
                         (let ((fanshi/gptel--replaying-queued-request t))
                           (gptel-send))
                         t)
                     (error
                      (remove-hook 'gptel-post-response-functions #'fanshi/gptel--replay-done t)
                      (setq-local fanshi/gptel--replay-callback nil)
                      (funcall cb (format "Replay of task %s failed to start: %s"
                                          task-id (error-message-string err)))
                      nil)))))
            (when ok
              (org-todo "DELEGATED")
              (org-entry-put (point) "DELEGATED_TO" (symbol-name model))
              (org-entry-put (point) "DELEGATED_AT" (format-time-string "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"))
              (save-buffer)))))))

  (gptel-make-tool
   :name "ReplayGptelTask"
   :function #'fanshi/gptel--replay-queued-request
   :description "Replay a queued gptel request task (heading starts with \"[gptel]\").
Reads the prompt and GPTEL_MODEL / GPTEL_BACKEND / SCOPED_PROJECT from the task, opens a gptel buffer in the project (or a plain gptel buffer without one), sends the request, and marks the task DELEGATED as soon as the request is sent.
The call returns only after the response finishes; the result reports the final state. A request that fails after being sent stays marked DELEGATED and is not retried.
Replay the next [gptel] task only after this one is done — never run two [gptel] requests at the same time.
TASK-ID: org-id of the [gptel] task to replay."
   :args '((:name "task-id" :type string
            :description "org-id of the [gptel] task to replay"))
   :category "agents"
   :async t)

  (advice-add 'gptel-send :around #'fanshi/gptel-request-maybe-waiting-main-model-a))
#+end_src
** Agent chain
Orchestrate multiple gptel presets sequentially, with fan-out for parallel
exploration. Each step is a preset plus a prompt template with {{key}} substitution
from prior results. Uses gptel-request directly (no tool access — suitable for
analysis/planning chains).
*** fanshi/gptel-chain
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle no :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel-chain--substitute (template context)
    "Replace {{KEY}} in TEMPLATE with CONTEXT's values (an alist)."
    (let ((result template))
      (dolist (pair context)
        (let* ((key (car pair))
               (placeholder (format "{{%s}}" key)))
          (setq result (replace-regexp-in-string
                        (regexp-quote placeholder)
                        (or (cdr pair) "")
                        result t t))))
      result))

  (cl-defun fanshi/gptel-chain (steps &key context callback on-step)
    "Execute STEPS as a sequential chain of gptel requests.

Each step in STEPS is a plist with:
  :name        — string key for storing the result
  :preset      — preset symbol (e.g. 'research 'product)
  :prompt      — prompt template with {{key}} placeholders
  :fan-out     — (optional) N parallel requests
  :consolidate — (optional) prompt merging fan-out results, via {{responses}}
  :model       — (optional) override model
  :backend     — (optional) override backend
  :system      — (optional) override system prompt
  :request-params — (optional) override request params

CONTEXT is an initial (KEY . VALUE) alist for template substitution.
CALLBACK gets the final context; ON-STEP gets (STEP-INDEX STEP-NAME RESULT)."
    (let ((ctx (or context '()))
          (remaining steps)
          (idx 0)
          (cb callback)
          (on-step-fn on-step))

      (cl-labels ((log (msg) (message "gptel-chain: %s" msg))

                  (resolve-preset-info (step)
                    "Model/backend/system/params from STEP's preset."
                    (let* ((pname (plist-get step :preset))
                           (preset (gptel-get-preset pname)))
                      (unless preset
                        (error "gptel-chain: Unknown preset '%s'" pname))
                      (let ((pp (gptel--preset-plist preset)))
                        (list :model (or (plist-get step :model)
                                         (and pp (plist-get pp :model))
                                         gptel-model)
                              :backend (or (plist-get step :backend)
                                           (and pp (plist-get pp :backend))
                                           gptel-backend)
                              :system (or (plist-get step :system)
                                          (and pp
                                               (let ((s (plist-get pp :system)))
                                                 (if (functionp s) (funcall s) s))))
                              :params (or (plist-get step :request-params)
                                          (and pp (plist-get pp :request-params)))))))

                  (advance (result)
                    "Store RESULT and move to next step."
                    (let ((sname (plist-get (car remaining) :name)))
                      (push (cons sname result) ctx)
                      (log (format "Step '%s' complete" sname))
                      (when on-step-fn
                        (funcall on-step-fn idx sname result)))
                    (setq remaining (cdr remaining)
                          idx (1+ idx))
                    (if remaining
                        (exec-step (car remaining))
                      (when cb (funcall cb ctx))))

                  (fan-out-done (responses)
                    "Consolidate or concatenate the fan-out RESPONSES."
                    (let* ((step (car remaining))
                           (consolidate (plist-get step :consolidate))
                           (info (resolve-preset-info step)))
                      (if consolidate
                          (let ((prompt (fanshi/gptel-chain--substitute
                                         consolidate
                                         `(("responses" . ,(string-join responses "\n\n---\n\n"))))))
                            (log "Consolidating fan-out results...")
                            (gptel-request prompt
                              :model (plist-get info :model)
                              :backend (plist-get info :backend)
                              :system (plist-get info :system)
                              :request-params (plist-get info :params)
                              :callback (lambda (resp)
                                          (if (stringp resp)
                                              (advance resp)
                                            (log "Consolidation returned non-string")
                                            (advance "")))))
                        (advance (string-join responses "\n\n---\n\n")))))

                  (exec-step (step)
                    "Execute a single STEP."
                    (let* ((name (plist-get step :name))
                           (info (resolve-preset-info step))
                           (prompt (fanshi/gptel-chain--substitute
                                    (plist-get step :prompt) ctx))
                           (fan-out (plist-get step :fan-out)))
                      (log (format "Step %d: %s" idx name))
                      (if (and fan-out (> fan-out 1))
                          ;; Fan-out: N parallel requests
                          (let ((responses '())
                                (done 0)
                                (total fan-out))
                            (dotimes (_ fan-out)
                              (gptel-request prompt
                                :model (plist-get info :model)
                                :backend (plist-get info :backend)
                                :system (plist-get info :system)
                                :request-params (plist-get info :params)
                                :callback (lambda (resp)
                                            (when (stringp resp)
                                              (push resp responses))
                                            (cl-incf done)
                                            (when (>= done total)
                                              (fan-out-done (nreverse responses))))))
                            ;; Proceed with partial results after 3 min
                            (run-at-time 180 nil
                                         (lambda ()
                                           (when (< done total)
                                             (log (format "Fan-out timeout, %d/%d responses" done total))
                                             (fan-out-done (nreverse responses))))))
                        ;; Single request
                        (gptel-request prompt
                          :model (plist-get info :model)
                          :backend (plist-get info :backend)
                          :system (plist-get info :system)
                          :request-params (plist-get info :params)
                          :callback (lambda (resp)
                                      (if (stringp resp)
                                          (advance resp)
                                        (log (format "Step '%s' returned non-string: %s" name resp))
                                        (advance "")))))))

                  (when remaining
                    (exec-step (car remaining)))))))

  ;; Convenience: run a pre-defined chain
  (defun fanshi/gptel-chain-run (chain-var &rest context)
    "Run the chain in CHAIN-VAR with CONTEXT, an alist for template substitution."
    (interactive)
    (fanshi/gptel-chain (symbol-value chain-var) :context context))

  ;; Pre-defined chains
  (defvar fanshi/chain-research-to-plan
    '((:name "research" :preset research
       :prompt "Research the following topic thoroughly. Provide key findings, trends, and open questions.\n\nTopic: {{topic}}"
       :fan-out 3
       :consolidate "You received 3 independent research reports on the same topic. Synthesize them into a single comprehensive summary. Highlight areas of agreement, disagreement, and unique insights from each.\n\nReports:\n{{responses}}")
      (:name "plan" :preset product
       :prompt "Based on the research findings below, decompose this into a structured project plan with user stories and acceptance criteria.\n\nResearch:\n{{research}}"))
    "Chain: fan-out research → consolidate → project plan.")

  (defvar fanshi/chain-problem-to-design
    '((:name "analysis" :preset problem-solve
       :prompt "Analyze the following problem. Identify root causes, constraints, and propose 2-3 solution approaches with tradeoffs.\n\nProblem: {{problem}}")
      (:name "design" :preset coding
       :prompt "Based on this problem analysis, design a technical solution. Include architecture overview, key components, and implementation plan.\n\nAnalysis:\n{{analysis}}"))
    "Chain: problem analysis → technical design."))
#+end_src
** fanshi/gptel-abort-all-in-buffer
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel-abort-all-in-buffer (&optional buffer)
    "Abort all active GPTel requests in BUFFER (default: current).
Number aborted."
    (interactive (list (current-buffer)))
    (let ((buffer (or buffer (current-buffer)))
          (aborted 0))
      (fanshi/gptel-mark-turn-aborted buffer)
      (with-demoted-errors "gptel-abort-all: %s"
        ;; Copy: entries are removed while iterating.
        (cl-loop for entry in (copy-sequence gptel--request-alist)
                 when (eq (plist-get (gptel-fsm-info (cadr entry)) :buffer)
                          buffer)
                 do (let* ((proc (car entry))
                           (fsm (cadr entry))
                           (fsm-info (gptel-fsm-info fsm))
                           (abort-fn (cddr entry))
                           (cb (plist-get fsm-info :callback)))
                      (when (functionp cb)
                        (with-demoted-errors "Callback error: %S"
                          (funcall cb 'abort fsm-info)))
                      (funcall abort-fn)
                      (setf (alist-get proc gptel--request-alist nil 'remove) nil)
                      (gptel--fsm-transition fsm 'ABRT)
                      (cl-incf aborted)))
        (if (> aborted 0)
            (progn
              (message "Aborted %d gptel request(s) in %s." aborted buffer)
              (fanshi/gptel-mark-turn-aborted buffer))
          (progn
            (with-current-buffer buffer
              (setq-local fanshi/gptel--aborted nil
                          fanshi/gptel-loop--aborted nil))
            (message "No active gptel request(s) to abort in %s." buffer)))
        aborted)))
  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent--setup-abort-all ()
    "Abort this buffer's requests when it is killed."
    (add-hook! 'kill-buffer-hook :local #'fanshi/gptel-abort-all-in-buffer))
  (add-hook! gptel-mode-hook :append #'fanshi/gptel-agent--setup-abort-all))
#+end_src
** Fix lingering entries in gptel--request-alist
Gptel removes a request's entry from `gptel--request-alist' only *after* running the
response callback. If anything in that hook chain signals, the sentinel's cleanup
never runs and the entry lingers until `gptel-abort' is called.

The FSM's terminal state (DONE/ERRS/ABRT) is set *before* the buffer shows "Ready",
so sweeping out terminal entries from a post-response hook is safe.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel--cleanup-finished-requests (_beg _end)
    "Drop `gptel--request-alist' entries in a terminal FSM state.
Leftovers appear when a post-response handler errors and interrupts gptel's own
sentinel cleanup."
    (cl-loop for entry in (copy-sequence gptel--request-alist)
             for state = (gptel-fsm-state (cadr entry))
             when (memq state '(DONE ERRS ABRT))
             do (setf (alist-get (car entry) gptel--request-alist nil 'remove) nil)))
  (add-hook 'gptel-post-response-functions #'fanshi/gptel--cleanup-finished-requests))
#+end_src
** Guard gptel-curl process handlers against stale requests
`gptel-curl--stream-filter', `gptel-curl--stream-cleanup' and
`gptel-curl--sentinel' look up the request FSM with
`(car (alist-get process gptel--request-alist))' without checking the entry exists.
If it was already removed while the process still delivers buffered data, or its
sentinel still fires, they call `gptel-fsm-info' on nil:

#+begin_example
error in process filter: Wrong type argument: gptel-fsm, nil
error in process sentinel: gptel-curl--stream-cleanup: Wrong type argument: gptel-fsm, nil
#+end_example

Once the entry is gone there is nothing to do, so skip the handler and kill the
process buffer.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel-curl--stale-request-p (process &rest _args)
    "Skip the handler when PROCESS has no entry in `gptel--request-alist'."
    (unless (alist-get process gptel--request-alist)
      (when-let ((buf (process-buffer process)))
        (when (buffer-live-p buf)
          (kill-buffer buf)))
      t))
  (advice-add 'gptel-curl--stream-filter :before-until #'fanshi/gptel-curl--stale-request-p)
  (advice-add 'gptel-curl--stream-cleanup :before-until #'fanshi/gptel-curl--stale-request-p)
  (advice-add 'gptel-curl--sentinel :before-until #'fanshi/gptel-curl--stale-request-p))
#+end_src
** fanshi/gptel-agent
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(map! :map doom-leader-open-map "l l" #'fanshi/gptel-agent)
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent (task-description)
    "Start a gptel-agent session for TASK-DESCRIPTION.
In the main workspace of a jj repo, a sub-workspace is created for the task;
otherwise a plain gptel-agent buffer in the current project."
    (interactive "sTask Description: ")
    (if-let* ((jj-dir (fanshi/jj-workspace-root default-directory)))
        (if (string-blank-p task-description)
            (with-current-buffer (gptel-agent jj-dir 'gptel-agent)
              (setq-local fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace jj-dir)
              (when (eq 'doom (projectile-project-type))
                ;; Let the doomemacs config agent read modules & repos.
                (setq-local fanshi/gptel-context-files
                            `(,doom-modules-dir
                              ,(expand-file-name "straight/repos" doom-local-dir))))
              (current-buffer))
          ;; create a new jj workspace.
          (let* ((name (->> task-description
                            (s-replace "/" "-")
                            (s-replace " " "-")
                            (s-replace ":" "-")
                            (concat (projectile-project-name default-directory) "-")
                            (downcase)
                            (replace-regexp-in-string "[,;'\"`|]" "")
                            (replace-regexp-in-string "-+" "-")
                            (string-remove-prefix "-")
                            (string-remove-suffix "-")))
                 (dir (file-name-as-directory (expand-file-name name fanshi/breeding-ground-dir))))
            (fanshi/call-jj jj-dir "workspace" "add" "--name" name dir)
            (let ((buf (gptel-agent dir 'gptel-agent)))
              (with-current-buffer buf
                (goto-char (point-max))
                (insert task-description)
                (setq-local fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace dir)
                (setq-local fanshi/gptel-jj-origin-root jj-dir)
                (when (eq 'doom (projectile-project-type))
                  ;; Let the doomemacs config agent read modules & repos.
                  (setq-local fanshi/gptel-context-files
                              `(,doom-modules-dir
                                ,(expand-file-name "straight/repos" doom-local-dir))))
                (when
                    ;; NOTE: only forget breeding-ground workspaces — safe over sorry.
                    (s-prefix-p (expand-file-name fanshi/breeding-ground-dir) fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace)
                  (add-hook 'kill-buffer-hook
                            (lambda ()
                              (dolist (buf (buffer-list))
                                (when (or
                                       ;; Kill child buffers
                                       (eq (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/gptel-agent-parent buf) (current-buffer))
                                       ;; Kill buffers visiting files in the workspace
                                       (and-let* ((fname (buffer-local-value 'buffer-file-name buf))
                                                  ((stringp fname))
                                                  ((s-prefix-p fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace fname)))))
                                  (kill-buffer buf)))
                              (fanshi/call-jj fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace "workspace" "forget"
                                              (format "%s" name))
                              (ignore-errors (system-move-file-to-trash fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace))
                              (message "Auto-cleaned workspace: %s" fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace)
                              (projectile-switch-project-by-name fanshi/gptel-jj-origin-root))
                            nil t))
                ;; `--message=' not `-m "..."': `fanshi/call-jj' runs jj via
                ;; `call-process' (no shell), so embedded quotes would be passed
                ;; to jj literally and end up inside the commit description.
                (fanshi/call-jj dir "desc" (format "--message=%s" task-description)))
              buf)))
      (message "jj workspace root unresolvable in %s (jj not on PATH, or not a jj repo); starting a plain gptel-agent"
               default-directory)
      (with-current-buffer (gptel-agent default-directory 'gptel-agent)
        (goto-char (point-max))
        (insert task-description)
        (current-buffer)))))
#+end_src
** fanshi/gptel--insert-preset
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel--insert-preset (preset-name)
    "Insert the '@PRESET-NAME' marker into the current buffer.

Split across two insertions on purpose: a literal '@foo' here would be picked up as
a preset marker when this Org source is exported or fed to an agent."
    (insert " @" preset-name " ")))
#+end_src
** fanshi/gptel-code-review-jj-diff
Start a gptel-agent session reviewing the current jj changes. The prompt ends with a
=bash= block holding `fanshi/gptel-code-review-jj-diff-cmd'.

- No prefix arg: the command is run in the workspace, its output appended to the prompt,
  and the request sent.
- Prefix arg: nothing is run or sent; the buffer is left with the command block in place
  so the instruction and command can be edited, then the block run and sent (~C-c RET~).

The buffer's =default-directory= is set to the workspace so the agent's own =jj= commands
resolve against the reviewed change, and the auto-commit hook is disarmed so =@= stays put.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-code-review-jj-diff-cmd
    '("jj" "diff" "--color=never" "-r" "\"heads(::@&~empty())\"")
    "Diff command `fanshi/gptel-code-review-jj-diff' puts in the prompt's bash block
and runs. Defaults to the latest non-empty ancestor of `@'."
    :type '(repeat string)
    :group 'gptel)

  (defun fanshi/insert-jj-diff-code-block ()
    "FIXME: finish the doc here"
    (insert "\n\n#+begin_src bash :results output :wrap src diff\n"
            (string-join fanshi/gptel-code-review-jj-diff-cmd " ")
            "\n#+end_src"))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-code-review-jj-diff (&optional no-run)
    "Start a gptel-agent session code reviewing the current jj changes.
Runs in the *current* workspace (a fresh sub-workspace has an empty working copy),
with the jj auto-commit hook disarmed so the reviewed `@' stays put.

The prompt ends with a bash block to return the jj diff.
Without a prefix arg it is run in the workspace, its output appended to the prompt as
the diff under review, and the request sent. With a prefix arg NO-RUN, nothing is run
or sent: the buffer is left with the command block in place, so the instruction and
command can be edited before running the block and sending with ~C-c RET~."
    (interactive (list current-prefix-arg))
    (if-let* ((jj-dir (fanshi/jj-workspace-root default-directory))
              (default-directory jj-dir))
        (with-current-buffer (fanshi/gptel-agent "")
          (setq-local fanshi/gptel-jj-auto-commit-enabled nil)
          (goto-char (point-min))
          (forward-char)
          (fanshi/gptel--insert-preset "exp")
          (insert "Please code review this change")
          (fanshi/insert-jj-diff-code-block)
          (if no-run
              (message "Review prompt ready in %s — edit, run the diff block, then C-c RET"
                       (buffer-name (current-buffer)))
            (org-babel-execute-src-block)
            (goto-char (point-max))
            (gptel-send))
          (current-buffer))
      (error "Not inside a jj workspace: no workspace root for %s" default-directory))))
#+end_src
** jj auto commit
After each gptel response in a buffer bound to a dedicated jj sub-workspace (see
`fanshi/gptel-agent'), commit the working copy so every response leaves one
reviewable commit:
- if the working-copy commit already has a description, run ~jj new~ (the changes stay
  on the previous commit, which keeps that description)
- otherwise generate a commit message from the ~jj diff~ with an LLM
  (~gptel-request~) and run ~jj ci --message="<message>"~, unwrapping any quotes
  the model wrapped it in
- if the diff is empty, do nothing

Safety:
- only buffers with a non-nil buffer-local `fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace' are affected
- `jj workspace root' must equal the recorded workspace dir before anything runs, so
  a stale or moved buffer can never commit in the wrong workspace
- while a response auto-continues, the commit is deferred to the final response
- deferred via `run-at-time' and wrapped in `condition-case': the post-response hook
  runs inside gptel's process filter, where calling the asynchronous `gptel-request'
  corrupts the new request's process filter
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-jj-origin-root nil
    "Main workspace root this sub-workspace buffer originated from.
A lookup from here would return the sub-workspace's own root.")

  (defvar-local fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace nil
    "If non-nil, the jj sub-workspace this gptel buffer is bound to.
Enables post-response auto-commit.")

  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-jj-auto-commit-enabled t
    "Auto-commit the jj working copy after each gptel response in buffers with a
non-nil `fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace'."
    :type 'boolean
    :group 'gptel)

  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-jj-auto-commit-diff-limit 8000
    "Max characters of `jj diff' sent to the LLM for commit-message generation."
    :type 'integer
    :group 'gptel)

  (defconst fanshi/gptel-jj-auto-commit-msg "gptel: auto-commit"
    "Commit message used when the LLM message generator returns nothing.")

  (defun fanshi/gptel--jj-workspace-check (dir)
    "Whether the current buffer is still bound to jj sub-workspace DIR.
Runs `jj workspace root' in DIR and requires it to equal DIR, so a stale buffer
(moved, deleted, never a workspace) can never commit in the wrong workspace."
    (let ((root (fanshi/jj-workspace-root dir)))
      (and root (string= root (file-name-as-directory dir)))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--jj-working-copy-description ()
    "The working-copy commit's description, or nil if none."
    (let ((desc (ignore-errors
                  (fanshi/call-jj fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace
                                   "log" "-r" "@" "-T" "description" "--no-graph"))))
      (and desc (not (string-blank-p desc)) (string-trim desc))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--jj-generate-commit-message (diff callback)
    "Ask the LLM for a commit message for jj diff DIFF.
CALLBACK gets the message, or nil on failure."
    (condition-case err
        (gptel-request
            (format "Write a concise commit message (imperative mood, max 72 chars) for this jj diff.\nReturn ONLY the message text, no quotes, no explanation.\n\n#+begin_example\n%s\n#+end_example"
                    diff)
          :system "You write concise commit messages."
          :callback (lambda (response _)
                      (if (stringp response)
                          (let* ((msg (string-trim response))
                                 ;; models wrap the message in quotes anyway; the interior
                                 ;; must be quote-free so fix "a" and "b" stays intact
                                 (msg (replace-regexp-in-string
                                       "\\`[\"'`]\\([^\"'`]*\\)[\"'`]\\'" "\\1" msg)))
                            (funcall callback
                                     (if (not (string-blank-p msg))
                                         (substring msg 0 (min 200 (length msg)))
                                       nil)))
                        (funcall callback nil))))
      (error (funcall callback nil))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--jj-auto-commit (&rest _args)
    "Post-response hook: auto-commit the jj working copy of a sub-workspace buffer.
Deferred via `run-at-time', so `gptel-request' is not called from the process
filter."
    (when (and fanshi/gptel-jj-auto-commit-enabled
               fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace
               (not fanshi/gptel--auto-continued))
      (let ((buf (current-buffer))
            (ws fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace))
        (run-at-time 0 nil
                     (lambda ()
                       (when (buffer-live-p buf)
                         (with-current-buffer buf
                           (condition-case err
                               (fanshi/gptel--jj-do-auto-commit ws)
                             (error
                              (message "jj auto-commit failed: %s"
                                       (error-message-string err)))))))))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--jj-do-auto-commit (ws)
    "Auto-commit workspace WS. Called from a timer, so `gptel-request' is safe."
    (when (fanshi/gptel--jj-workspace-check ws)
      (let ((diff (ignore-errors (fanshi/call-jj ws "diff"))))
        (when (and diff (not (string-blank-p diff)))
          (if-let ((desc (fanshi/gptel--jj-working-copy-description)))
              (progn
                (fanshi/call-jj ws "new")
                (message "jj auto-commit: committed working copy (kept description: %s)" (string-trim desc)))
            (fanshi/gptel--jj-generate-commit-message
             (substring diff 0 (min fanshi/gptel-jj-auto-commit-diff-limit
                                    (length diff)))
             (lambda (msg)
               (condition-case err
                   (let ((ci-msg (replace-regexp-in-string
                                  "[\r\n]+" " "
                                  (or msg fanshi/gptel-jj-auto-commit-msg))))
                     ;; `--message=' not `-m': a generated message could start with a
                     ;; hyphen, which clap would read as a flag.
                     (fanshi/call-jj ws "ci" (format "--message=%s" ci-msg))
                     (message "jj auto-commit: committed working copy (%s)" ci-msg))
                 (error
                  (message "jj auto-commit failed: %s"
                           (error-message-string err)))))))))))

  ;; Weight 110: after the auto-continue hook (100), so
  ;; `fanshi/gptel--auto-continued' is settled before we commit.
  (add-hook 'gptel-post-response-functions #'fanshi/gptel--jj-auto-commit 110))
#+end_src
** Meta request on previous conversations
Start a conversation based on a previous gptel-agent conversation — to judge,
summarise, verify or find mistakes in the result.

`fanshi/gptel-meta-request' derives a clean transcript from the
prompt buffer that gptel would send with reasoning block headers and tool results stripped.

The transcript goes in as alternating "User"/"Assistant" sub-headings, so the
meta-agent can distinguish turns without a code fence.

When the source agent is bound to a jj sub-workspace, a =bash= block with the diff command
for the agent's own work is appended after the transcript, the same way as in
`fanshi/gptel-code-review-jj-diff'.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/prepare-meta-request-transcript (source-buf meta-buf)
    "Extract a clean transcript of SOURCE-BUF into META-BUF.

META-BUF is the meta-agent buffer whose framing text has already been inserted: the
transcript's User/Assistant sub-headings are appended at its end.  Build a throwaway
prompt buffer via `gptel--create-prompt-buffer' (so gptel's tool/reasoning block headers
are stripped and tool results unescaped), parse the conversation turns from it, and append
the transcript to META-BUF.  SIGNALS a `user-error' when SOURCE-BUF is not live, or when
no conversation turn is found (after showing META-BUF, which holds the framing text)."
    (unless (buffer-live-p source-buf)
      (user-error "No live source conversation buffer to build the meta request from"))
    (let ((prefix (alist-get 'org-mode gptel-prompt-prefix-alist))
          (transcript-buf (with-current-buffer source-buf
                            (save-excursion
                              (goto-char (point-max))
                              (gptel--create-prompt-buffer)))))
      (unwind-protect
          (with-current-buffer meta-buf
            (goto-char (point-max))
            (insert "\n*" prefix "Transcript:\n")
            (let ((user-prompt-heading-regex (rx point (literal prefix) (* blank) (* (and "@" (1+ (any word ?-)) (+ blank)))))
                  (active-speaker)
                  (pos-next 1))
              (while-let ((pos pos-next))
                (setq pos-next (next-single-property-change pos 'gptel transcript-buf))
                (let ((prop (get-text-property pos 'gptel transcript-buf)))
                  (cond
                   ((null prop)
                    (when-let ((prompt-start (with-current-buffer transcript-buf
                                               (goto-char pos)
                                               (re-search-forward user-prompt-heading-regex nil t))))
                      (unless (eq active-speaker 'user)
                        (setq active-speaker 'user)
                        (insert "\n**" prefix "User:\n"))
                      (let ((pt (point)))
                        (insert-buffer-substring-no-properties transcript-buf prompt-start pos-next)
                        (dotimes (_ 3) (org-map-region 'org-demote pt (point))))))
                   ((eq 'response prop)
                    (unless (eq active-speaker 'assistant)
                      (setq active-speaker 'assistant)
                      (insert "\n**" prefix "Assistant:\n"))
                    (let ((pt (point)))
                      (insert-buffer-substring-no-properties transcript-buf pos pos-next)
                      (dotimes (_ 3) (org-map-region 'org-demote pt (point)))))
                   ;; NOTE: skip tool-call
                   ((and (consp prop) (eq 'tool (car prop))) nil))))))
        (kill-buffer transcript-buf))))


  (defun fanshi/gptel-meta-request (task source-buf &optional no-run)
    "Start a gptel-agent conversation based on a previous one.

TASK is the meta request, e.g. \"Summarise the result\". SOURCE-BUF is the previous
conversation, defaulting to the last visited gptel-agent buffer. The transcript is
derived from `gptel--create-prompt-buffer', so it is the same text gptel would send
(gptel's tool/reasoning block headers stripped, tool results unescaped).  SIGNALS a
`user-error' when SOURCE-BUF is not a live buffer, or when no conversation turn could be
extracted from it.

When SOURCE-BUF is bound to a jj sub-workspace (see `fanshi/gptel-agent'), a
bash block with the diff of the agent's work is appended after the transcript and run
before sending. A sub-agent source buffer has no workspace of its own, so its parent's
is used; a source with no workspace at all gets no diff block.

The new agent buffer is left for review; without prefix arg NO-RUN, the block is run
in place and the request sent immediately. With NO-RUN nothing is run or sent."
    (interactive
     (list (read-string "Meta task (e.g. summarise, verify, judge the result): " "Summarise")
           (get-buffer
            (if (and (derived-mode-p 'org-mode) (eq gptel-mode t)) (current-buffer)
              (read-buffer "Conversation buffer: "
                           (buffer-name fanshi/gptel-last-agent-buffer)
                           t
                           (lambda (b)
                             (and-let* ((buf (get-buffer (or (car-safe b) b)))
                                        ((buffer-local-value 'gptel-mode buf))
                                        ((eq (buffer-local-value 'major-mode buf)
                                             'org-mode)))
                               t)))))
           current-prefix-arg))
    ;; `source-buf' can be nil (minibuffer exited on a name whose buffer is gone).
    (unless (buffer-live-p source-buf)
      (user-error "No live source conversation buffer to build the meta request from"))
    (with-current-buffer
        (let ((mark-active nil) (transient-mark-mode nil)) ;; Keep `gptel-agent' from copying an active region into the new buffer.
          (fanshi/gptel-agent ""))
      ;; Auto-commit stays off: the meta session only reads and analyses.
      (setq-local fanshi/gptel-jj-auto-commit-enabled nil)
      (insert " " task "\n")
      (fanshi/prepare-meta-request-transcript source-buf (current-buffer))
      ;; `gptel-send' sends the current subtree, so the send point is the meta turn's heading,
      ;; not the end of the buffer. Demote the task heading one level above the transcript's so
      ;; the whole transcript falls inside the sent subtree.
      (when-let ((source-ws (or (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace source-buf)
                                (let ((parent (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/gptel-agent-parent
                                                                  source-buf)))
                                  (and parent (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/gptel-jj-workspace parent))))))
        ;; The diff runs in the source's workspace: the meta session's own directory is the
        ;; invoking buffer's project, not the source's.
        (let ((default-directory source-ws))
          (insert "\n*" (alist-get 'org-mode gptel-prompt-prefix-alist) "Diff:\n")
          (fanshi/insert-jj-diff-code-block)
          (unless no-run (org-babel-execute-src-block))))
      (if no-run
          (message "Meta request ready in %s — review, then C-c RET to send"
                   (buffer-name (current-buffer)))
        (goto-char (point-max))
        (gptel-send))
      (current-buffer))))
#+end_src
** Sub-agent buffers
Each sub-agent buffer stores a pointer to its parent in `fanshi/gptel-agent-parent'.
Children are found by scanning buffers for that pointer, so the parent needs no list
of them. Sub-agent properties live as buffer-local variables on the sub-agent buffer.

*** Local variables for sub-agent metadata
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defvar-local fanshi/gptel-agent-parent nil
  "The parent gptel-agent buffer that created this buffer, or nil.")

(defvar-local fanshi/gptel-agent-task nil
  "Task description string for this sub-agent buffer.")

(defvar-local fanshi/gptel-agent-intention nil
  "Intention string for this sub-agent buffer.")

(defvar-local fanshi/gptel-agent-verification nil
  "Verification criteria string for this sub-agent buffer.")

(defvar-local fanshi/gptel-agent-task-callback nil
