  "Main callback for this sub-agent buffer, called with the result on completion.")
#+end_src

*** Override gptel-agent--task to spawn its own gptel buffer
Instead of the inline overlay approach, spawn a dedicated gptel buffer per sub-agent
task, integrating with `fanshi/gptel-agent-parent' and the marginalia annotation.

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel-agent
  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent--task (main-cb agent-preset description prompt)
    "Spawn a dedicated gptel buffer for a sub-agent task.

MAIN-CB gets the result when done. AGENT-PRESET names the preset, DESCRIPTION the
task, PROMPT the detailed prompt."
    (if (gptel-get-preset (intern-soft agent-preset))
        ;; FIXME: also check whether the sub-agent may be called by this agent.
        (let* ((parent-buf (current-buffer))
               (sub-agent-buf (fanshi/gptel-agent description)))
          (condition-case err
              (with-current-buffer sub-agent-buf
                (setq-local fanshi/gptel-agent-parent parent-buf
                            fanshi/gptel-agent-task description
                            fanshi/gptel-agent-task-callback main-cb
                            fanshi/gptel-auto-continue-enabled nil)
                (goto-char (point-min))
                (let ((prompt-prefix (alist-get gptel-default-mode gptel-prompt-prefix-alist)))
                  (re-search-forward (rx (literal prompt-prefix))))
                (insert "@" agent-preset " ")
                (goto-char (point-max))
                (insert "\n" prompt "\n")
                (goto-char (point-max))
                (add-hook 'gptel-post-response-functions
                          #'fanshi/gptel-agent--sub-agent-done nil t)
                (gptel-send))
            (error (funcall main-cb (format "Error: Sub-agent task failed to start: %s" err))))
          (message "Spawned sub-agent %s: %s" agent-preset (buffer-name sub-agent-buf)))
      (funcall main-cb
               (format "Error: Sub-agent task failed. Agent %s not found" agent-preset))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-agent--sub-agent-done (beg end)
    "Post-response hook: call the main callback when the sub-agent is done."
    (when (and fanshi/gptel-agent-task-callback
               fanshi/gptel-agent-parent
               (buffer-live-p fanshi/gptel-agent-parent))
      (if (eq beg end)
          (funcall fanshi/gptel-agent-task-callback
                   (format "Error: Sub-agent task failed. %S"
                           (if-let* ((fsm-info (gptel-fsm-info gptel--fsm-last))
                                     (tool-use (plist-get fsm-info :tool-use))
                                     (err (plist-get fsm-info :error)))
                               err
                             "empty response")))
        (funcall fanshi/gptel-agent-task-callback
                 (format "Result for the task:\n\n%s"
                         (string-trim (buffer-substring-no-properties beg end)))))
      (setq-local fanshi/gptel-agent-task-callback nil)
      (remove-hook 'gptel-post-response-functions
                   #'fanshi/gptel-agent--sub-agent-done t)))
  (advice-add 'gptel-agent--task :override #'fanshi/gptel-agent--task))
#+end_src
*** Adapt Agent tool to use valid preset names
The original Agent tool's =subagent_type= enum is ["researcher" "introspector"],
which are not gptel preset names. Redefine it with presets that exist.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel-agent
  (when-let* ((agent-tool (gptel-get-tool "Agent")))
    (aset agent-tool 4
          (mapcar (lambda (arg)
                    (if  (string= (plist-get arg :name) "subagent_type")
                        (plist-put arg :enum ["research" "coding" "problem-solve"])
                      arg
                      ))
                  (aref agent-tool 4)))
    (setf
     (alist-get "Agent"
                (alist-get (gptel-tool-category (gptel-get-tool "Agent")) gptel--known-tools nil nil #'equal)
                nil nil #'equal)
     agent-tool)))
#+end_src
*** Advice +llm/open-in-same-window to narrow to sub-agents
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/+llm/open-in-same-window--sub-agents-a ()
    "`+llm/open-in-same-window' advice: with a prefix arg, offer only the live gptel
buffers whose `fanshi/gptel-agent-parent' is the current buffer."
    (let ((parent-buf (current-buffer)))
      (when current-prefix-arg
        (if-let* ((chosen (read-buffer "Select sub gptel-agent buffer: " nil t
                                       (lambda (b)
                                         (and-let* (;; NOTE: https://github.com/karthink/gptel/issues/450
                                                    (buf (get-buffer (or (car-safe b) b)))
                                                    ((buffer-local-value 'gptel-mode buf))
                                                    (parent (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/gptel-agent-parent buf))
                                                    ((eq parent parent-buf))))))))
            (let ((gptel-display-buffer-action '(display-buffer-same-window)))
              (gptel chosen nil nil t))
          (user-error "No sub-agent buffers found/selected for current buffer")))))

  (advice-add '+llm/open-in-same-window :before-until
              #'fanshi/+llm/open-in-same-window--sub-agents-a))
#+end_src
** Marginalia annotation for gptel buffers
Annotate gptel buffers in marginalia with their last status (Ready, Typing, Error),
model, token usage and tools, so each session's state is visible at a glance during
buffer completion.

*** Capture status from gptel--update-status
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defvar-local fanshi/gptel--status `("Ready" . 'success)
  "Last status message set by `gptel--update-status' in this buffer.")

(defun fanshi/gptel--update-status-a (msg &optional face)
  "Capture MSG and FACE from `gptel--update-status' into `fanshi/gptel--status'."
  (when gptel-mode
    (setq-local fanshi/gptel--status `(,(string-trim msg) . ,face))))

(advice-add 'gptel--update-status :after #'fanshi/gptel--update-status-a)
#+end_src

*** Marginalia integration
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! marginalia
  (defun fanshi/gptel--annotate (candidate)
    "Annotate CANDIDATE with its gptel status, preset, model, tools and token usage."
    (pcase-let* ((buf (get-buffer candidate))
                 (`(,status . ,face) (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/gptel--status buf))
                 (presets (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/last-gptel-presets buf))
                 (model (or (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/last-presets-gptel-model buf)
                            (buffer-local-value 'gptel-model buf)))
                 (tools (or (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/last-presets-gptel-tools buf)
                            (buffer-local-value 'gptel-tools buf)))
                 (token-strings (buffer-local-value 'gptel--token-usage-strings buf))
                 (auto-continue (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/gptel--auto-continue-count buf))
                 (last-request (buffer-local-value 'fanshi/gptel--last-request-record buf)))
      (when buf
        (concat " "
                (propertize status 'face face)
                (when presets
                  (concat " " (propertize (format "(%s)" (mapconcat (lambda (p) (format "@%s" (gptel--to-string p)))
                                                                    presets ","))
                                          'face 'marginalia-list)))
                (when model
                  (concat " " (propertize (gptel--to-string model)
                                          'face 'marginalia-symbol)))
                (when (and token-strings (nth 1 token-strings))
                  (concat " " (propertize (nth 1 token-strings)
                                          'face 'marginalia-documentation)))
                (when tools
                  (concat " "
                          (propertize (format "(%s)"
                                              (mapconcat #'gptel--to-string
                                                         (mapcar #'gptel-tool-name tools)
                                                         ","))
                                      'face 'marginalia-list)))
                (when auto-continue
                  (concat " "
                          (propertize (format "(AC:%d)" auto-continue)
                                      'face 'marginalia-documentation)))
                ;; NOTE: `fanshi/gptel--last-request-record' and
                ;; `fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time' are defined in the next section;
                ;; both tangle into the same config.el.
                (when last-request
                  (concat " "
                          (propertize (format "(last %s)"
                                              (fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time
                                               (cdr (assq 'duration last-request))))
                                      'face 'marginalia-size)))))))
  (add-to-list 'marginalia-annotators '(gptel-buffer fanshi/gptel--annotate))
  (add-to-list 'marginalia-command-categories '(+llm/open-in-same-window . gptel-buffer))
  (add-to-list 'marginalia-command-categories '(fanshi/gptel-meta-request . gptel-buffer)))
#+end_src
** Time statistics for gptel requests
Every request that reaches a terminal state (done, error or abort) appends one JSON
object to ~fanshi/gptel-request-stats-file~, and ~fanshi/gptel-request-stats~ renders
those records as Org tables.

Timings are stamped on the request's own state machine (~gptel-fsm~) inside
~gptel--fsm-transition~, so parallel requests in one buffer never mix their numbers:
- entering ~WAIT~ starts the clock,
- the first chunk the curl filter sees marks the first byte (~ttft~),
- each further chunk with data moves the generating window's end forward,
- leaving ~TYPE~ adds up that round trip's generating time (~gen~),
- ~DONE~/~ERRS~/~ABRT~ writes the record and echoes the duration.

gptel leaves ~WAIT~ for ~TYPE~ as soon as it parses the HTTP status line, *before* any
token arrives, so leaving ~WAIT~ closes the generating window only for non-streamed
responses. ~ttft~ inherits the same offset and slightly over-states the time to the
first token.

An agent turn with tool calls spans several round trips: ~duration~ is the turn's wall
clock, ~tool~ the part spent in ~TPRE~/~TOOL~, and ~active~ is ~duration~ minus ~tool~
— the time attributable to network and model. ~rounds~ counts the trips.

*** Config
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes
(defcustom fanshi/gptel-request-stats-file
  (expand-file-name "gptel-request-stats.jsonl"
                    (if (bound-and-true-p doom-etc-dir) doom-etc-dir user-emacs-directory))
  "File where gptel request timings are recorded, one JSON object per line.
Read by `fanshi/gptel-request-stats'."
  :type 'file
  :group 'gptel)

(defcustom fanshi/gptel-request-stats-retention-days 30
  "Days of request timings to keep in the stats file.
Always pruned to this window, never to the shorter window
`fanshi/gptel-request-stats' is asked to summarize."
  :type 'integer
  :group 'gptel)

(defcustom fanshi/gptel-request-stats-recent-requests 40
  "Number of individual requests listed by `fanshi/gptel-request-stats'."
  :type 'integer
  :group 'gptel)

(defcustom fanshi/gptel-request-stats-notify t
  "Echo how long each gptel request took when it finishes."
  :type 'boolean
  :group 'gptel)

(defvar fanshi/gptel--stats-pruned-p nil
  "Non-nil once the request stats file has been pruned in this session.")

(defvar-local fanshi/gptel--last-request-record nil
  "The last request completed in this buffer, as an alist.")

(defun fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time (secs)
  "Format SECS as a compact duration, or \"-\" when nil."
  (cond
   ((null secs) "-")
   ((< secs 60) (format "%.1fs" secs))
   ((< secs 3600) (format "%.1fm" (/ secs 60.0)))
   (t (format "%.1fh" (/ secs 3600.0)))))

(defun fanshi/gptel--stats-round (n precision)
  "Round N to PRECISION decimal places."
  (let ((factor (expt 10.0 precision)))
    (/ (round (* (float n) factor)) factor)))

(defun fanshi/gptel--stats-sum (nums)
  "Sum NUMS; 0.0 for the empty list."
  (if nums (float (apply #'+ nums)) 0.0))

(defun fanshi/gptel--stats-active (entry)
  "ENTRY's model/network seconds: ~duration~ minus ~tool~."
  (let ((duration (or (cdr (assq 'duration entry)) 0.0))
        (tool (or (cdr (assq 'tool entry)) 0.0)))
    (max 0.0 (- duration tool))))

(defun fanshi/gptel--stats-median (nums)
  "The median of NUMS, or 0.0 when empty."
  (if (null nums)
      0.0
    (let* ((sorted (sort (copy-sequence nums) #'<))
           (n (length sorted)))
      (if (zerop (mod n 2))
          (/ (+ (nth (1- (/ n 2)) sorted) (nth (/ n 2) sorted)) 2.0)
        (float (nth (/ n 2) sorted))))))
#+end_src
*** Stamping the timings onto the request
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel--stats-backend-name (backend buffer)
    "A name for BACKEND, falling back to BUFFER's `gptel-backend'."
    (let ((backend (or backend
                       (and (buffer-live-p buffer)
                            (buffer-local-value 'gptel-backend buffer)))))
      (cond
       ((null backend) "unknown")
       ((gptel-backend-p backend) (gptel--to-string (gptel-backend-name backend)))
       (t (gptel--to-string backend)))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--stats-log (info state)
    "Append a timing record for INFO's request, in terminal STATE.
INFO is the request's FSM context (see `gptel-fsm'). Requests without a start stamp
are skipped; each FSM is logged once."
    (when-let* ((start (plist-get info :fanshi-start))
                ((not (plist-get info :fanshi-logged))))
      (let* ((end (current-time))
             (buffer (plist-get info :buffer))
             (model-name (gptel--to-string (or (plist-get info :model) "the model")))
             (tokens (or (plist-get info :tokens-full) (plist-get info :tokens)))
             (output (float (or (plist-get tokens :output) 0)))
             (duration (float-time (time-subtract end start)))
             (tool (or (plist-get info :fanshi-tool) 0.0))
             (ttft (plist-get info :fanshi-ttft))
             ;; Non-streamed responses never see a chunk; count them whole.
             (gen (or (plist-get info :fanshi-gen) duration))
             (tps (if (> gen 0.1) (fanshi/gptel--stats-round (/ output gen) 1) 0.0))
             (record
              `((version . 1)
                (timestamp . ,(fanshi/gptel--stats-round (float-time end) 2))
                (model . ,(gptel--to-string (or (plist-get info :model) "unknown")))
                (backend . ,(fanshi/gptel--stats-backend-name (plist-get info :backend) buffer))
                (buffer . ,(if (buffer-live-p buffer) (buffer-name buffer) ""))
                (duration . ,(fanshi/gptel--stats-round duration 2))
                (tool . ,(fanshi/gptel--stats-round tool 2))
                (active . ,(fanshi/gptel--stats-round (max 0.0 (- duration tool)) 2))
                (ttft . ,(and ttft (fanshi/gptel--stats-round ttft 2)))
                (gen . ,(fanshi/gptel--stats-round gen 2))
                (tps . ,tps)
                (in . ,(or (plist-get tokens :input) 0))
                (out . ,(round output))
                (rounds . ,(or (plist-get info :fanshi-rounds) 1))
                (status . ,(downcase (symbol-name state))))))
        (make-directory (file-name-directory fanshi/gptel-request-stats-file) t)
        (write-region (concat (json-encode record) "\n") nil
                      fanshi/gptel-request-stats-file 'append 'silent)
        ;; Mark it logged only once the line is on disk, so a failed write retries.
        (plist-put info :fanshi-logged t)
        (when (buffer-live-p buffer)
          (with-current-buffer buffer
            (setq fanshi/gptel--last-request-record record)))
        (when fanshi/gptel-request-stats-notify
          ;; Idle timer: this runs in a process filter, where `message' would be
          ;; overwritten by gptel's own message.
          (run-with-idle-timer
           0.05 nil
           (lambda ()
             (message "gptel: %s in %s%s%s"
                      model-name
                      (fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time duration)
                      (if (> tps 0.0) (format ", %.1f tok/s" tps) "")
                      (if (eq state 'done) "" (format " [%s]" state))))))
        ;; One prune per session, deferred: pruning is a read-modify-write that could
        ;; clobber a line appended by another Emacs instance.
        (unless fanshi/gptel--stats-pruned-p
          (setq fanshi/gptel--stats-pruned-p t)
          (run-with-idle-timer 5.0 nil #'fanshi/gptel--stats-prune)))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--fsm-transition-stats-a (fsm &optional new-state)
    "Track how long FSM's request takes.
Advice for `gptel--fsm-transition': stamps live in the FSM's INFO plist, so
concurrent requests never share timings."
    (with-demoted-errors "gptel request stats: %S"
      (let* ((info (gptel-fsm-info fsm))
             (from (gptel-fsm-state fsm))
             (to (or new-state (gptel--fsm-next fsm))))
        ;; Leaving a state closes the window that state owned.
        (cond
         ((eq from 'WAIT)
          (when-let ((start (plist-get info :fanshi-start)))
            (unless (plist-get info :fanshi-ttft)
              (plist-put info :fanshi-ttft
                         (float-time (time-subtract
                                     (or (plist-get info :fanshi-first) (current-time))
                                     start))))
            (unless (plist-get info :fanshi-first)
              (plist-put info :fanshi-gen
                         (+ (float-time (time-subtract (current-time) start))
                            (or (plist-get info :fanshi-gen) 0.0))))))
         ((and (eq from 'TYPE) (plist-get info :fanshi-first))
          (plist-put info :fanshi-gen
                     (+ (float-time (time-subtract
                                     (or (plist-get info :fanshi-last) (current-time))
                                     (plist-get info :fanshi-first)))
                        (or (plist-get info :fanshi-gen) 0.0))))
         ((memq from '(TPRE TOOL))
          (when-let ((tool-start (plist-get info :fanshi-tool-start)))
            (plist-put info :fanshi-tool
                       (+ (float-time (time-subtract (current-time) tool-start))
                          (or (plist-get info :fanshi-tool) 0.0)))
            (plist-put info :fanshi-tool-start nil))))
        ;; Entering a state opens the windows that state owns.
        (pcase to
          ('WAIT
           (unless (plist-get info :fanshi-start)
             (plist-put info :fanshi-start (current-time)))
           (plist-put info :fanshi-first nil)
           (plist-put info :fanshi-last nil)
           (plist-put info :fanshi-rounds (1+ (or (plist-get info :fanshi-rounds) 0)))
           ;; Drop the previous duration so marginalia does not show it as current.
           (when-let ((buf (plist-get info :buffer)))
             (when (buffer-live-p buf)
               (with-current-buffer buf
                 (setq fanshi/gptel--last-request-record nil)))))
          ((or 'TPRE 'TOOL)
           (unless (plist-get info :fanshi-tool-start)
             (plist-put info :fanshi-tool-start (current-time))))
          ((or 'DONE 'ERRS 'ABRT)
           (fanshi/gptel--stats-log info to))))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--stream-filter-chunk-a (process output)
    "Stamp streamed chunks arriving for PROCESS.
Advice for `gptel-curl--stream-filter': the first call marks the response's start,
later ones with data move the generating window's end forward."
    (with-demoted-errors "gptel request stats: %S"
      (when-let* ((fsm (car (alist-get process gptel--request-alist)))
                  (info (gptel-fsm-info fsm)))
        (unless (plist-get info :fanshi-first)
          (plist-put info :fanshi-first (current-time)))
        (when (and (stringp output) (> (length output) 0))
          (plist-put info :fanshi-last (current-time))))))

  (advice-add 'gptel--fsm-transition :before #'fanshi/gptel--fsm-transition-stats-a)
  (advice-add 'gptel-curl--stream-filter :before #'fanshi/gptel--stream-filter-chunk-a))
#+end_src

** Do not stream the response into a buffer that is not visible
*** The problem
~gptel-curl--stream-insert-response~ inserts every streamed chunk from the curl
process filter, and each insertion asks for a redisplay of the window showing the
buffer. With dozens of background agent sessions streaming at once, that is a lot of
pointless work on the main thread. ~gptel-stream~ is not the answer: it switches to the
*synchronous* path, which blocks Emacs for the whole generation. What should go is the
per-chunk insertion into an invisible buffer.

*** The approach
Queue plain text chunks on the request's FSM info and insert them in one go. Tool
calls, tool results and reasoning are single insertions, so they always go straight
through and the agent's activity stays visible. The queue is per request, so parallel
requests in one buffer keep their text apart.

Visibility is decided when the response starts and holds for its duration (text cannot
be pulled back out mid-generation). The drain writes at the request's tracking marker
and applies gptel's markdown-to-org converter once instead of per chunk.

*** Caveats
- A deferred response's text is not readable until the round trip ends, and
  ~gptel-post-stream-hook~ only fires for its first chunk, so the loop guard's Type A
  detector sees much less of a background response.
- A reasoning block or structured output ends the deferral for the rest of the round
  trip, so only the text before it is deferred.
- On an abort the text is still queued when ~gptel-post-response-functions~ runs, so
  those hooks see the response without the deferred tail.
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defcustom fanshi/gptel-defer-stream-when-invisible 'background
    "When to hold back a streamed response instead of inserting it chunk by chunk.

~nil~        never defer (gptel's own behavior).
~background~ (default) defer only while the gptel buffer is not shown in a visible
             window.
~always~     defer regardless of visibility.

Decided when the response starts and holds for its duration. Only plain text chunks
are deferred; tool calls, tool results and reasoning are inserted immediately."
    :type '(choice (const :tag "Never" nil)
            (const :tag "Only when the buffer is not visible" background)
            (const :tag "Always" always))
    :group 'gptel)

  (defun fanshi/gptel--stream-deferred-p (info)
    "Whether INFO's request should defer its response."
    (pcase fanshi/gptel-defer-stream-when-invisible
      ('nil nil)
      ('always t)
      ('background
       (let ((buf (plist-get info :buffer)))
         (and (buffer-live-p buf) (null (get-buffer-window buf 'visible)))))
      (_ nil)))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--stream-defer-a (orig-fn response info &optional raw)
    "Queue streamed RESPONSE instead of inserting it into the gptel buffer.
Around advice for ~gptel-curl--stream-insert-response~; calls ORIG-FN unless the
chunk was queued.

The first chunk always goes through gptel: it inserts the separator and prefix and
creates the tracking marker the drain needs.

The tracking-marker test is needed on every chunk:
~gptel--display-reasoning-stream~ and ~gptel--handle-tool-use~ also pass a plain
string and insert at the marker."
    (cond
     ;; A tool result arrives as a plain string with RAW non-nil, a tool call or
     ;; reasoning event as a cons. All display at the tracking marker, so whatever is
     ;; queued must land first.
     ((or (not (stringp response)) raw)
      (prog1 (funcall orig-fn response info raw)
        (fanshi/gptel--stream-deferred-flush info)))
     ((and (fanshi/gptel--stream-deferred-p info)
           (or (and (plist-get info :fanshi-deferred)
                    (eq (point-marker) (plist-get info :tracking-marker)))
               (prog1 (and (plist-get info :tracking-marker)
                           (not (equal "" response)))
                 (plist-put info :fanshi-deferred t))))
      (when (> (length response) 0)
        (let ((cell (list response)))
          (if-let* ((tail (plist-get info :fanshi-deferred-tail)))
              (setcdr tail cell)
            (plist-put info :fanshi-deferred-text cell))
          (plist-put info :fanshi-deferred-tail cell)))
      t)
     (t
      ;; Not deferred: stop deferring and drain what an earlier chunk left. The flush
      ;; comes after ORIG-FN, since the marker is insertion-type t.
      (prog1 (funcall orig-fn response info raw)
        (fanshi/gptel--stream-deferred-flush info)))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--stream-deferred-flush (info)
    "Insert the chunks deferred for INFO's request.
Drains at the request's tracking marker; a no-op when nothing is queued. Deferral
ends here."
    (when-let* ((chunks (prog1 (plist-get info :fanshi-deferred-text)
                          ;; Consume the queue before draining, so text cannot go in
                          ;; twice if the drain signals.
                          (plist-put info :fanshi-deferred-text nil)
                          (plist-put info :fanshi-deferred-tail nil)
                          (plist-put info :fanshi-deferred nil)))
                (text (mapconcat #'identity chunks ""))
                ((not (equal "" text))) ;nothing queued
                (tracking (plist-get info :tracking-marker))
                ((buffer-live-p (marker-buffer tracking))))
      (with-demoted-errors "gptel deferred stream: %S"
        (let ((transformer (prog1 (plist-get info :transformer)
                             (plist-put info :transformer nil))))
          ;; The converter is built against the response start marker, so apply it
          ;; before entering the buffer, as gptel does per chunk.
          (when transformer
            (setq text (funcall transformer text)))
          (add-text-properties
           0 (length text) '(gptel response front-sticky (gptel)) text)
          (with-current-buffer (marker-buffer tracking)
            (save-excursion
              (goto-char tracking)
              (insert text)))))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--display-tool-flush-a (_ info)
    "Flush the deferred response before a tool call or result displays.
Before advice for ~gptel--display-tool-calls~ and ~gptel--display-tool-results~."
    (fanshi/gptel--stream-deferred-flush info))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--stream-deferred-fsm-a (orig-fn fsm &optional new-state)
    "Flush FSM's deferred response when it reaches a terminal state.
Around advice for ~gptel--fsm-transition~. ~new-state~ is read rather than the FSM's
current state, since ~ABRT~ is reached while the queue is still full."
    (when (memq new-state '(DONE ERRS ABRT))
      (fanshi/gptel--stream-deferred-flush (gptel-fsm-info fsm)))
    (funcall orig-fn fsm new-state))

  (advice-add 'gptel-curl--stream-insert-response
              :around #'fanshi/gptel--stream-defer-a)
  (advice-add 'gptel--display-tool-calls
              :before #'fanshi/gptel--display-tool-flush-a)
  (advice-add 'gptel--display-tool-results
              :before #'fanshi/gptel--display-tool-flush-a)
  ;; Around rather than before: `gptel--fsm-transition' ends by running the state's
  ;; handlers, so a `:before' advice's value is what the process machinery sees.
  (advice-add 'gptel--fsm-transition
              :around #'fanshi/gptel--stream-deferred-fsm-a))
#+end_src

*** fanshi/gptel-request-stats
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! gptel
  (defun fanshi/gptel--stats-read (&optional days)
    "The request records of the last DAYS days as alists, oldest first.
DAYS defaults to `fanshi/gptel-request-stats-retention-days' and only decides which
records are returned: the file is always pruned to the retention window."
    (when (file-exists-p fanshi/gptel-request-stats-file)
      (let* ((days (or days fanshi/gptel-request-stats-retention-days))
             (cutoff (- (float-time) (* 86400.0 days)))
             (prune-cutoff (- (float-time)
                              (* 86400.0 fanshi/gptel-request-stats-retention-days))))
        (with-temp-buffer
          (insert-file-contents fanshi/gptel-request-stats-file)
          (let ((entries nil) (kept nil) (stale-p nil))
            (goto-char (point-min))
            (while (not (eobp))
              (unless (looking-at-p "$")
                (let* ((record (ignore-errors
                                 (let ((json-object-type 'alist))
                                   (json-read-from-string
                                    (buffer-substring-no-properties
                                     (line-beginning-position)
                                     (line-end-position))))))
                       (timestamp (and (consp record)
                                       (cdr (assq 'timestamp record)))))
                  (cond
                   ((not (numberp timestamp)) (setq stale-p t))
                   ((< timestamp prune-cutoff) (setq stale-p t))
                   ;; Kept on disk; reported only when inside DAYS.
                   (t (push record kept)
                      (when (>= timestamp cutoff)
                        (push record entries)))))
                (forward-line)))
            (when stale-p
              (erase-buffer)
              (dolist (record (nreverse kept))
                (insert (json-encode record) "\n"))
              (write-region (point-min) (point-max) fanshi/gptel-request-stats-file
                            nil 'silent))
            (nreverse entries))))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--stats-prune ()
    "Drop records outside the retention window from the stats file."
    (interactive)
    (fanshi/gptel--stats-read))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--stats-insert-table (header rows)
    "Insert an Org table of HEADER and ROWS (lists of strings) at point."
    (insert (format "| %s |\n" (mapconcat #'identity header " | "))
            (format "|%s|\n" (mapconcat (lambda (_) "---") header "|")))
    (dolist (row rows)
      (insert (format "| %s |\n" (mapconcat #'identity row " | "))))
    (forward-line -1)
    (org-table-align))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--stats-summary-rows (entries)
    "Summarise ENTRIES into table rows, one per model/backend, slowest first.
avg/median/slowest are computed over `active' time."
    (let ((groups nil))
      (dolist (entry entries)
        (let ((key (cons (cdr (assq 'model entry))
                         (cdr (assq 'backend entry)))))
          (if-let ((group (assoc key groups)))
              (push entry (cdr group))
            (push (cons key (list entry)) groups))))
      (mapcar
       #'cdr
       (sort
        (cl-loop
         for (key . es) in groups
         for durations = (mapcar (lambda (e) (or (cdr (assq 'duration e)) 0.0)) es)
         for actives = (mapcar #'fanshi/gptel--stats-active es)
         for ttfts = (delq nil (mapcar (lambda (e) (cdr (assq 'ttft e))) es))
         for out = (fanshi/gptel--stats-sum
                    (mapcar (lambda (e) (float (or (cdr (assq 'out e)) 0))) es))
         for gen = (fanshi/gptel--stats-sum
                    (mapcar (lambda (e) (or (cdr (assq 'gen e)) 0.0)) es))
         for errors = (length
                       (delq nil (mapcar (lambda (e)
                                           (and (equal "error" (cdr (assq 'status e))) t))
                                         es)))
         for aborts = (length
                       (delq nil (mapcar (lambda (e)
                                           (and (equal "abrt" (cdr (assq 'status e))) t))
                                         es)))
         collect
         (cons (fanshi/gptel--stats-sum actives)
               (list (or (car key) "?")
                     (or (cdr key) "?")
                     (number-to-string (length es))
                     (fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time (fanshi/gptel--stats-sum durations))
                     (fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time
                      (/ (fanshi/gptel--stats-sum actives) (float (length es))))
                     (fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time (fanshi/gptel--stats-median actives))
                     (fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time (apply #'max actives))
                     (if ttfts
                         (fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time
                          (/ (fanshi/gptel--stats-sum ttfts) (float (length ttfts))))
                       "-")
                     (if (> gen 0.1) (format "%.1f" (/ out gen)) "-")
                     (number-to-string (round out))
                     (cond
                      ((and (zerop errors) (zerop aborts)) "")
                      ((zerop aborts) (number-to-string errors))
                      (t (format "%de/%da" errors aborts))))))
        (lambda (a b) (> (car a) (car b)))))))

  (defun fanshi/gptel--stats-request-rows (entries)
    "ENTRIES (oldest first) as table rows, newest first."
    (mapcar
     (lambda (entry)
       (let ((gen (or (cdr (assq 'gen entry)) 0.0))
             (out (float (or (cdr (assq 'out entry)) 0)))
             (tool (or (cdr (assq 'tool entry)) 0.0)))
         (list (format-time-string "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"
                                   (or (cdr (assq 'timestamp entry)) 0))
               (or (cdr (assq 'model entry)) "?")
               (fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time (cdr (assq 'duration entry)))
               (fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time (cdr (assq 'ttft entry)))
               (fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time (if (> gen 0.0) gen nil))
               (if (> gen 0.1) (format "%.1f" (/ out gen)) "-")
               (fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time (if (> tool 0.05) tool nil))
               (number-to-string (or (cdr (assq 'rounds entry)) 1))
               (or (cdr (assq 'status entry)) "?"))))
     (reverse entries)))

  (defun fanshi/gptel-request-stats (&optional days)
    "Timing statistics for the gptel requests of the last DAYS days.
With a prefix arg, prompt for the number of days."
    (interactive
     (list (when current-prefix-arg
             (read-number "Days to summarize: "
                         fanshi/gptel-request-stats-retention-days))))
    (let ((entries (fanshi/gptel--stats-read days))
          (buffer (get-buffer-create "*gptel request stats*")))
      (with-current-buffer buffer
        (org-mode)
        (let ((inhibit-read-only t))
          (erase-buffer)
          (insert (format-time-string "#+TITLE: GPTel Request Statistics %Y-%m-%d\n\n"))
          (if (null entries)
              (insert (format "Nothing recorded in %s yet.\n"
                              (abbreviate-file-name fanshi/gptel-request-stats-file)))
            (insert (format "%d requests, %s of model time (%s of turn time) in the last %s days.\n\n"
                            (length entries)
                            (fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time
                             (fanshi/gptel--stats-sum
                              (mapcar #'fanshi/gptel--stats-active entries)))
                            (fanshi/gptel--stats-format-time
                             (fanshi/gptel--stats-sum
                              (mapcar (lambda (e) (or (cdr (assq 'duration e)) 0.0)) entries)))
                            (or days fanshi/gptel-request-stats-retention-days)))
            (insert "** By model\n\n")
            (fanshi/gptel--stats-insert-table
             '("model" "backend" "requests" "turn total" "model avg"
               "model median" "model slowest" "first byte" "tok/s"
               "tokens out" "err")
             (fanshi/gptel--stats-summary-rows entries))
            (insert "\n** Recent requests\n\n")
            (fanshi/gptel--stats-insert-table
             '("time" "model" "turn" "first byte" "generating" "tok/s"
               "tools" "rounds" "status")
             (fanshi/gptel--stats-request-rows
              (last entries
                   (min (length entries)
                        fanshi/gptel-request-stats-recent-requests)))))
          (goto-char (point-min)))
        (display-buffer buffer))
      buffer)))
#+end_src

* oMLX
** fanshi/omlx-unload-model
#+begin_src elisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! org
  (defun fanshi/omlx-unload-model (model-id)
    "Unload MODEL-ID from oMLX server."
    (let ((url (format "http://127.0.0.1:8000/v1/models/%s/unload" model-id))
          (url-request-method "POST")
          (url-request-extra-headers `(("Authorization" . ,(concat "Bearer " (+pass-get-secret 'omlx/1)))
                                       ,@url-request-extra-headers)))
      (url-retrieve url
                    (lambda (status)
                      (if (eq (car status) :error)
                          (message "omlx: failed to unload %s: %s" model-id (cdr status))
                        (message "omlx: unloaded %s" model-id)))))))
#+end_src
** fanshi/omlx-list-loaded-model
#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle yes :lexical t
(after! org
  (defun fanshi/omlx-list-loaded-model (&optional unload-all)
    "List currently loaded models from oMLX server. if UNLOAD-ALL unload all loaded models"
    (interactive "P")
    (let ((url "http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/status")
          (url-request-method "GET")
          (url-request-extra-headers `(("Authorization" . ,(concat "Bearer " (+pass-get-secret 'omlx/1)))
                                       ,@url-request-extra-headers)))
      (url-retrieve url
                    (lambda (status)
                      (if (eq (car status) :error)
                          (message "omlx: failed to list loaded models: %s" (cdr status))
                        (goto-char url-http-end-of-headers)
                        (let* ((data (json-parse-string (buffer-substring-no-properties (point) (point-max))
                                                        :object-type 'plist))
                               (models (plist-get data :loaded_models)))
                          (if (seq-empty-p models)
                              (message "omlx: no models loaded")
                            (message "omlx: loaded models: %s" (string-join models ", "))
                            (when unload-all
                              (seq-map #'fanshi/omlx-unload-model models)
                              (message "omlx: unloaded all"))))))))))

(defun fanshi/org-gtd-delegate--finalize-a (f &rest args)

      (let ((org-gtd-default-file-name "org-gtd-ai-tasks"))
        (funcall f args)))
